# 新托业词汇本领书（第 3 版）· 词库

> 按 Unit 整理词条（Part A / Part B），字段：词条 / 音标 / 词性 / 英文注释 / 中文注释 / 例句 / 例句译文；不含 Drills、Answers、Cross Word、Exercise 等练习部分。

## Unit 1　Office Matters 办公室事宜

### Unit 1 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | appointment | /əˈpɔintmənt/ | n. | an arrangement for a meeting at a given time and place | 约会； 约定 | If you would like to meet with Mr. Higgins. you must first make an appointment with his secretary. | 你若想见希金斯先生一面， 就得先跟他的秘书约好时间。 |
| 2 | attendance | /əˈtendəns/ | n. | the act of being present； the number of persons present | 出席； 出席人数 | I was very pleased with the number of high-ranking officials in attendance at today’s press conference. | 我很高兴今天的新闻发布会有这么多高级官员到场。 |
| 3 | cabinet | /ˈkæbinit/ | n. | an upright case or cupboard with shelves， drawers， or compartments for safekeeping or display of objects | 橱柜 | He keeps all his files in the cabinets in the back of the office. | 他把所有档案放在办公室后面的柜子里。 |
| 4 | calendar | /ˈkælində/ | n. | chart showing the months of a year （including days and weeks）； a schedule | 日历； 月历； 行事历 | Please check the calendar on the wall and see which date Mother’s Day falls on this year. | 请查一下墙上的日历， 看看今年的母亲节是几月几号。 |
| 5 | clerk | /kləːk/ | n. | a person who works in an office， bank， or law court and whose job is to look after the records or accounts | 办事员； 书记 | She was offered a job as an accounts clerk with a travel firm. | 有一家旅行社聘用她当会计。 |
| 6 | directory | /diˈrektəri/ | n. | an alphabetical or classified list of names and addresses | 人名住址簿 | If you need to speak to him， you can use the office directory and look up his personal extension. | 如果你需要跟他谈话， 你可以用办公室里的名册查出他私人的分机号码。 |
| 7 | duplicate | /ˈdjuːplikeit/ | n. | （1） n. an identical copy vt. to make an identical copy of； to replicate | 副本 （2） 复制 | Please don’t bring a duplicate， since I will need to see your original birth certificate for this passport. | 办护照时， 我需要看你的出生证明正本， 所以不要带影印本。 |
| 8 | filing | /ˈfailiŋ/ | n. | putting in useful order； cataloguing | 归档 | Developed in the early 1900’s， the Dewey Decimal System is a filing system still used by libraries today. | 在20 世纪初期发展出的杜威十进制分类系统至今仍为各图书馆所使用。 |
| 9 | in-tray | /ˈintrei/ | n. | a flat， shallow container used for holding items that are to be accomplished | 待处理文件盒 | The more Dennis procrastinated， the more the stack of paperwork in his in-tray resembled the Rocky Mountains. | 丹尼斯越是拖延， 他的 “待处理文件盒” 里的文件就越堆得像落基山一般高。 |
| 10 | monitor | /ˈmɔnitə/ | vt. | to watch， or keep track of， often by means of an electronic device | 检测； 监视； 追踪 | Seismic readouts are carefully monitored by seismologists， who are always on the lookout for the next big earthquake. | 地震学家小心监测地震仪指数， 随时注意下一个大地震的来临。 |
| 11 | out-tray | /ˈautˌtrei/ | n. | a tray or shallow basket used in offices to put letters and documents in when they have been dealt with and are ready to be sent out of the office | 已处理文件盒 | I will leave that file in my out-tray on my desk， and you can come pick it up anytime in the afternoon. | 我会把那份文件留在我桌上的 “已处理文件盒” 里， 你可以在下午的任何时 间来拿。 |
| 12 | partition | /paːˈtiʃən/ | n. | division into parts； something that separates or divides， as a wall | 分隔； 分隔物 （如墙壁等） | We don’t have the money to add an entirely new office， but what we can do is add a partition and divide one or two of the larger offices into multiple smaller ones. | 我们没有钱增建一间全新的办公室， 但是我们可以用隔板把一两间比较大间 的办公室隔成几间比较小的办公室。 |
| 13 | postage | /ˈpəustidʒ/ | n. | the charge for mailing an item | 邮费 | How much postage is required for a small package to be sent to HongKong？ | 寄一个小包裹到香港需要多少邮资？ |
| 14 | punctuality | /ˌpʌŋktjuˈæliti/ | n. | the quality or state of observing an appointed or regular time | 准时； 守时 | In addition to your dress and appearance， punctuality and a concise delivery are really important when you are giving such an important presentation. | 除了服装仪容外， 准时和简单扼要的陈述在做如此重要的报告时是非常重要的。 |
| 15 | schedule | /ˈskedʒul/ | n. | a plan that gives a list of events or tasks and the times at which each one should happen or be done | 时间表； 计划表 | The plane arrived in Cairo four minutes ahead of schedule. | 飞机比预定时间早4 分钟到达开罗。 |
| 16 | shift | /ʃift/ | n. | a person’s scheduled period of work | 换班； 轮班； 值班 | They often heard him complain about having to work the graveyard shift in order to pay the bills. | 他们经常听到他抱怨为了要付账单必须上夜班。 |
| 17 | staff | /staːf/ | n. | the personnel of an enterprise | 全体职员 | The staff at the five-star Formosa Hotel delivers prompt and friendly service. | 五星级宝岛大饭店的员工服务非常及时、 周到。 |
| 18 | strike | /straik/ | n. | a stoppage of work for a period of time by workers， usually in order to try to get better pay benefits or other conditions for themselves | 罢工 | I’m glad that our employees were satisfied and didn’t find the need for a strike to express their concerns. | 我很高兴我们的员工都很满意， 觉得没有必要借罢工来表达他们所关切的事。 |
| 19 | task | /taːsk/ | n. | a piece of assigned work | 任务； 工作 | This task must be made top priority because a very important client wants to pick up these papers today. | 这项工作必须被视为第一优先， 因为有一个非常重要的客户今天要来拿这些 文件。 |
| 20 | work force | /wəːk fɔːs/ | n. | the total number of workers employed in a specific project； the total number of persons employed | 工作人员； 劳动 人口 | After months of unemployment， Tim joined the American work force as the night manager at KFC. | 在失业几个月后， 提姆加入了美国劳动人口， 开始在肯德基炸鸡店当起夜班 经理。 |

### Unit 1 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | assignment | /əˈsainmənt/ | n. | the act of appointing， designating or allotting； a particular task or duty； an appointment | 工作； 分配； 分派 | Since he has been covering the same story for two weeks ， I think he would really be excited about a new assignment. | 既然他已经采访报道同一个新闻两个星期了， 我想他会很高兴接到新的工作 指派。 |
| 2 | bulletin | /ˈbulitin/ | n. | a printed or broadcast statement on a matter of public interest； a regular publication issued by an organization | 公报； 告 示； 定期报告 （书） | We sent out a bulletin to all of the executives， informing them of the changes discussed at today’s meeting. | 我们发了一份公告给所有的主管人员， 告知他们会议上讨论到的一些改变。 |
| 3 | calculator | /ˈkælkjuleitə/ | n. | a mechanical or electronic machine that automatically performs mathematical operations | 计算机 | I made so many mistakes in my checkbook； I had to get out my calculator to add the figures up again. | 我在支票本里弄错很多账目， 所以我得拿出计算器重新合计。 |
| 4 | carbon copy | /ˈkaːbən ˈkɔpi/ | n. | a replica， as of a letter， made by using carbon paper | 用复写纸复制的副本 | You will meed to keep the carbon copy of that receipt for your record and give me the original. | 你必须保留收据的复写本存档， 然后把正本给我。 |
| 5 | colleague | /ˈkɔliːg/ | n. | a fellow member， especially in a profession； associate | 同事； 同僚 | Because my doctor can’t help me with my problems， he is going to refer me to a colleague of his at another hospital. | 因为我的医生无法帮我解决问题， 所以他打算把我转介到他在另一家医院工 作的同事那里。 |
| 6 | document | /ˈdɔkjumənt/ | n. | a paper that provides evidence， information， or proof about something | 文件； 证件 | The Declaration of Independence is a document with tremendous historical significance. 《 | 独立宣言》 是一项具有重大历史意义的文件。 |
| 7 | extension | /iksˈtenʃən/ | n. | a telephone line that is connected to the switchboard of a company or institution， and that has its own number； an additional period of time given one to meet or carry out an obligation | 分机； 延期 | For further information， please contact 2344-3849， extension 104. | 如需了解更详细的信息， 请拨2344-3849， 分机104。 |
| 8 | intercom | /ˈintəkɔm/ | n. | a system for two-way communication， as between two rooms | 对讲机 | Would you please page Mr. William over the intercom and tell him to meet me at the front desk？ | 麻烦你用对讲机叫威廉先生到前面的柜台跟我会面， 好吗？ |
| 9 | memo | /ˈmeməu/ | n. | a short note； an informal written communication； a written agreement （ = memorandum） | 便条； 便笺； 备忘录 | Everyone’s curiosity was piqued upon circulation of a memo announcing the senior vice-president’s resignation. | 由于一项宣布资深副总裁要离职的备忘录在公司内流传， 每个人的好奇心 都被激起来了。 |
| 10 | operator | /ˈɔpəreitə/ | n. | a person who operates the telephone switchboard | 接线生 | If you know the extension of the party you wish to reach， dial it now or wait on the line for operator assistance. | 如果你知道分机号码请直拨， 或稍候由总机为您服务。 |
| 11 | overtime | /ˈəuvətaim/ | n. | working hours in addition to those of the regular schedule | 加班的时间 | Federal regulations require businesses to pay extra money to employees for their overtime. | 联邦政府规定， 公司必须付给加班员工加班费。 |
| 12 | portfolio | /pɔːtˈfəuliəu/ | n. | a portable case for holding drawings or a collection of drawings in this； a case for carrying business papers | 作品 （夹）； 公事包 | The architect will want to look at your portfolio before he considers you for the job of designing the new building. | 在考虑让你设计这栋新建筑物之前， 建筑师会想要看看你的作品。 |
| 13 | printed matter | /ˈprintid ˈmætə/ | n. | printed material that qualifies for a special postal rate | 印刷品 | Please check all the letters with printed matter on them as we can get a better rate for them at the post office. | 请把注明印刷品的信件找出来， 因为我们可以在邮局以比较低的邮资寄送。 |
| 14 | receptionist | /riˈsepʃənist/ | n. | one employed chiefly to receive callers and answer the telephone | 接待员 | Before entering the boardroom， Mr. Withers told the receptionist to hold all of his calls until after the meeting. | 在进入会议室前， 威特斯先生告诉接待员， 他在会议结束前不接任何电话。 |
| 15 | secretary | /ˈsekrətri/ | n. | a person who is employed to do office work， such as typing letters， answering phone calls， and arranging meetings | 秘书 | The manager was mad at his secretary because she failed to remind him of an important appointment. | 经理对他的秘书很不悦， 因为她未能提醒他去参加一个重要的约会。 |
| 16 | shorthand | /ˈʃɔːthænd/ | n. | a system of rapid handwriting； employing symbols to represent words， phrases， and letters； stenography | 速记； 速记法 | Because his secretary was so adept at shorthand， he felt comfortable dictating to her at his own pace. | 因为他的秘书速记非常熟练， 所以他以非常轻松自然的速度口述。 |
| 17 | stapler | /ˈsteiplə/ | n. | a machine for fastening together sheets of paper | 订 书机 | The stapler gets jammed every time I want to use it. | 每一次我要用这订书机就会卡住。 |
| 18 | tardy | /ˈtaːdi/ | adj. | slow in moving， acting， etc； late； delayed | 迟缓的； 迟到的； 迟延的 | We apologize for the tardy response to your letter. | 来函迟复， 谨表歉意。 |
| 19 | typist | /ˈtaipist/ | n. | one who operates a typewriter | 打字员 | Don’t forget to remind the typist to delete the last three sentences. | 不要忘了提醒打字员把最后3 句话删掉。 |
| 20 | xerox | /ˈziərɔks/ | vt./vi. | to print with a copying machine | 影印 | You don’t need to copy the entire book by hand； just xerox it and mail it to the publisher. | 你不需要用手抄写整本书， 只要影印一份， 然后把它寄给出版社就可以了。 |

## Unit 2　Personnel & Management 人事及管理

### Unit 2 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | allocate | /ˈæləukeit/ | vt. | to set apart for a specific purpose； to distribute； to allot （ | 为某种用途） 拨出； 分配； 配置 | It would be best if we could take the extra funds and allocate them evenly among all the different divisions. | 如果我们可以把这些额外的奖金平均分配给不同的部门， 那就再好不过了。 |
| 2 | applicant | /ˈæplikənt/ | n. | one who applies | 申请人 | Although many of the applicants were more than qualified for the position， the boss chose his son， who has just recently graduated from college. | 虽然很多申请人都非常符合这个职位的资格， 老板却选择了他刚从大学毕业 的儿子。 |
| 3 | authorize | /ˈɔːθəˌraiz/ | vt. | to grant authority or power to | 授权； 委任 | I absolutely will not authorize such a risky， poorly planned investment scheme. | 我绝对不会授权执行这么冒险、 规划草率的投资方案。 |
| 4 | bonus | /ˈbəunəs/ | n. | something given or paid in addition to what is usual or expected | 红利； 额外津贴； 奖金 | Anthony has just bought a brand-new Mercedes and is counting on his Christmas bonus for the payment. | 安东尼刚买了一辆全新的奔驰轿车， 他指望用他的圣诞节奖金来付这笔款。 |
| 5 | capability | /ˌkeipəˈbiliti/ | n. | ability； capacity； potential | 能力； 才干； 潜力； 性能 | He has the extraordinary capability to solve any problem. | 他有非凡的才能可以解决任何问题。 |
| 6 | collaboration | /kəˌlæbəˈreiʃən/ | n. | working together； cooperation with the enemy | 合作； 通敌 | The credit for our company’s success goes to all of our five managers and their collaboration on the winning project. | 我们公司的成功要归功于我们的5 个经理和他们在这个制胜专案上的合作。 |
| 7 | consultation | /ˌkɔnsəlˈteiʃən/ | n. | a meeting for deliberation， discussion， or decision； conference | 咨询； 商量； 商议； 会议 | Before deciding on a course of action， he would first like to have a consultation with his accountant and stockbroker. | 在他采取行动以前， 他想先跟他的会计员和股票经纪人商量一下。 |
| 8 | curriculum vitae | /kəˈrikjuləm ˈvaitiː/ | n. | chronological description of one’s employment and educational history （ = resume） | 履历 （简称 CV） | Would you please submit a curriculum vitae， outlining all previous work experiences and educational background？ | 是否能请你提交一份履历， 说明一下先前的工作经验和教育背景？ |
| 9 | eligible | /ˈelidʒəbl/ | adj. | qualified， as for an office or position | 合格的； 合适 的 | If you miss another day this quarter， you won’t be eligible for that promotion you’ve been hoping for. | 如果本季度中你再缺一次， 就没有资格获得你一直期待的升迁了。 |
| 10 | employer | /imˈplɔiə/ | n. | one who hires others | 雇主 | My duty as an employer is to be fair， honest and provide a work environment that fosters challenge and growth. | 我身为雇主的职责就是要公平、 诚实并提供一个有挑战性的鼓励成长的工作环境。 |
| 11 | executive | /igˈzekjutiv/ | n. | a person or group having administrative or managerial authority in an organization | 行政或管理人员 | All of the executives seem to be doing quite well， each driving a Mercedes Benz and wearing the best custom-tailored suits. | 所有管理阶层人员好像都很吃得开， 每个人开的都是奔驰轿车， 穿的都是特 别定做的西装。 |
| 12 | income | /ˈinkəm/ | n. | money or its equivalent received in a given period， as wages， rent， interest， etc. | 收入或所得 | “I have such a phenomenal annual income，” Mark bragged， “I can retire by the time I’m 30 years old. ” | 马克吹嘘说： “我的年收入非常高， 我可以在30 岁时就退休。” |
| 13 | lay off | /lei ɔf/ | v. | to dismiss an employee （ especially temporarily because of slack business） （ | 暂时） 解雇 | Due to severe budget cuts and a recent drop in stock prices， we are going to be forced to lay off almost 15% of our employees. | 由于严重的预算删减和最近股票价格的下跌， 我们将被迫遣散将近15%的员工。 |
| 14 | occupation | /ˌɔkjuˈpeiʃən/ | n. | an activity that serves as one’s regular source of livelihood | 职业 | Being teacher is my chosen occupation， but I have to work as a waitress right now to pay for my tuition. | 当老师就是我所选择的职业， 但我现在必须当女服务生来赚钱付我的学费。 |
| 15 | part-time | /ˈpaːt ˈtaim/ | adj. | engaged in work， study， etc. for less than the usual time | 兼任的； 兼职的 | Many college students find it advantageous to find a part-time job to help pay the bills， as long as they can balance it with their schoolwork. | 只要他们能够兼顾学校功课的话， 很多大学生觉得找一个兼差工作是有好处 的， 因为那样可以帮他们付一些账单。 |
| 16 | permanent | /ˈpəːmənənt/ | adj. | long-lasting or non-fading； everlasting | 不 变的； 永久的 | Alex bought the permanent purple hair dye advertised on TV and couldn’t wait to try it. | 亚历克斯买了电视上做了广告的永久性紫色染发剂， 等不及想赶快试试。 |
| 17 | promote | /prəˈməut/ | vt. | to advance in position or rank； to encourage the sales of a product | 升迁； 促销 | Mr. Deitrich liked to reward the diligence of his employees by promoting them every six months. | 狄崔志先生喜欢以每6 个月升职一次来奖励他员工的努力。 |
| 18 | recruit | /riˈkruːt/ | vt./vi. | to seek out and engage （persons） for work or service | 吸收； 征募 | We like to go straight to each college campus to recruit each year’s new interns rather than wait for them to come to us. | 我们喜欢直接去每所大学网罗每年需要的实习生， 而不是等他们找上门来。 |
| 19 | résumé | /riˈzjuːm/ | n. | a summary， especially of work experience， submitted when applying for a job | 履历表 | It would be helpful if you were to bring both your résumé and your college transcript to the job interview. | 如果你可以在工作面试时把履历表和大学成绩单带来的话， 会对我们很有帮助。 |
| 20 | salary | /ˈsæləri/ | n. | regular compensation for services or work | 薪水 | My salary at this job is twice what it was when I worked in Mexico doing the same thing. | 我现在工作的薪水是我以前在墨西哥做同样工作时的两倍。 |

### Unit 2 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | amateur | /ˈæmətə（ː）/ | n. | one who engages in an activity as a pastime rather than as a profession； one who lacks expertise； nonprofessional | 业余技术家； 外行人； 非专家 | I have never been paid as an actor， so even though I have been in several plays， I am still considered an amateur. | 我从来没有领取过正式演员的薪水， 所以即使我曾参加多部戏剧的演出， 我 依然被认为是一个业余演员。 |
| 2 | appoint | /əˈpɔint/ | vt. | to designate for an office or position | 任命； 指定 | One of the duties of an American president is to appoint justices to the Supreme Court. | 美国总统的权责之一是任命最高法院的大法官。 |
| 3 | benefit | /ˈbenifit/ | vt./vi. | to be helpful or advantageous to； to derive advantage； profit | 有益于； 受益 | If the deal goes through as planned， both our firm and the participating schools will benefit greatly from the joint effort. | 如果交易按计划进行， 我们公司以及其他参与的学校都会从这项合作中获益 良多。 |
| 4 | candidate | /ˈkændideit/ | n. | one who seeks or is nominated for an office， prize， honor， and so forth | 候选人 | Of the three， which do you think is the most likely candidate for the Employee of the Month award？ | 你觉得他们3 个人之中哪一个最有可能获选 “本月最优秀员工”？ |
| 5 | certificate | /səˈtifikit/ | n. | a document testifying to a fact， qualification or promise | 凭证； 证书 | His birth certificate states his name is Leroy Jackson， but he had it legally changed to Cornelious Watson a few years ago. | 他出生证明上的名字是李洛依·杰克逊， 但他在几年前把它合法地改成了 寇尼斯·沃特森。 |
| 6 | competent | /ˈkɔmpitənt/ | adj. | properly or well qualified； capable | 胜任的； 有能力的 | I think he has been the most competent and capable group leader we’ve had since the creation of this project. | 我觉得他是我们这个计划开始以来最能干的一个组长了。 |
| 7 | coordinate | /kəuˈɔːdinit/ | vt. | bring into proper order or relation； adjust | 协 调； 调整 | If we want this project to go as smoothly as possible， we need you to take charge and coordinate the efforts of all three groups. | 如果我们想要这个计划尽可能顺利进行， 我们需要你负责指挥并协调全部 3 个团队的力量。 |
| 8 | deadline | /ˈdedlain/ | n. | a time or date before which a particular task must be finished or a particular thing must be done | 截止期限 | We were not able to meet the deadline because of manufacturing delays. | 因为制造方面的延宕， 我们无法准时交货。 |
| 9 | employee | /ˌemplɔiˈiː/ | n. | one who works for another | 受雇者 | Steve had been an employee at 7-11 for three and a half years and had not received a single raise the entire time. | 史蒂夫已经在7-11 公司工作3 年半了， 但是在这整段时间内他连一次加薪都 没获得过。 |
| 10 | evaluation | /iˌvæljuˈeiʃən/ | n. | appraisal； assessment | 评价； 估价 | Bill nervously awaited the release of the management’s evaluation of his proposal. | 比尔紧张地等待管理部人员对他的计划评估的结果。 |
| 11 | expertise | /ˌekspəˈtiːz/ | n. | expert skill or knowledge | 专门技术或知识 | We need you to fly to our office in Boston and lend them your expertise in computer security. | 我们需要你飞到我们在波士顿的办公室， 以借重你在计算机安全方面的专才。 |
| 12 | interview | /ˈintəvjuː/ | n. | a face-to-face meeting | 会面； 面谈 | Would you be available to come in this Thursday afternoon to meet with our supervisor for an interview ？ | 你可以在这个星期四下午来和我们的主管面谈吗？ |
| 13 | novice | /ˈnɔvis/ | n. | a person new to something； beginner | 生手； 新手； 初学者 | My dressmaker has been making my clothes for years， but her daughter is a novice and I would rather not have her make them. | 我的裁缝师已经帮我做衣服好几年了， 但是她的女儿是新手， 我可不愿意交 给她做。 |
| 14 | overtime | /ˈəuvətaim/ | adv. | beyond the usual time， especially working time | 超时 | His paychecks are so large for the sole reason that he works overtime every week. | 他的薪水之所以那么高的唯一原因就是他每个星期都加班。 |
| 15 | pension | /ˈpenʃən/ | n. | a sum of money paid regularly， especially as a retirement benifit | 养老金； 退休金 | He hasn’t worked for years， but he receives a monthly pension for as long as he lives because he was a government official before. | 他已经好几年没有做事了， 但是因为他以前是政府官员， 所以只要他活着， 每个月就可以领到一笔退休金。 |
| 16 | personnel | /ˌpəːsəˈnel/ | n. | the body of persons employed by or active in an organization； a personnel department or office | 全体人员； 人事部门 | I believe that all concerns about work related issues should be addressed in the personnel office. | 我认为关于工作的所有问题都应该在人事部办公室里讨论。 |
| 17 | recommendation | /ˌrekəmenˈdeiʃən/ | n. | letter of reference； suggestion； advice | 推荐； 推荐书； 劝告 | I was wondering if you could write me a letter of recommendation addressed to my new employer. | 我不知道您是否愿意帮我写一封推荐信给我的新雇主。 |
| 18 | resign | /riˈzain/ | vt./vi. | to give up （a position）； to quit | 辞职 | If he doesn’t quit， they are going to force him to resign in order to preserve the company’s reputation. | 如果他不主动辞职， 他们将强迫他辞职以维护公司的名声。 |
| 19 | retire | /riˈtaiə/ | vi. | to withdraw from business or public life | 退休 | After he retires， Mr. Williams plans to split his time between his family and the crippled children at the hospital. | 退休后， 威廉斯先生计划要把他的时间平均分配给他的家人和医院里的残障 儿童。 |
| 20 | substitute | /ˈsʌbstitjuːt/ | n. | a person or thing serving or used in place of another | 代理人； 代用品 | The students used to think their math teacher， Mrs. Titters， was horrible until they met the substitute teacher， Mr. Ventura. | 学生们以前觉得他们的数学老师狄特女士很恐怖， 但直到他们碰上代课老师 范徒拉先生， 他们才了解什么叫真正的恐怖。 |

## Unit 3　Business Development 业务拓展

### Unit 3 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | accrue | /əˈkruː/ | vt./vi. | to come as a gain or increase | 产生； 增加 | Due to an addiction to excessive gambling， Benny had accrued massive debt and made a lot of enemies. | 由于过度沉溺于赌博， 本尼已经赌债累累， 仇敌四处。 |
| 2 | acquisition | /ˌækwiˈziʃən/ | n. | the act of acquiring | 获得 （的东西）； 习得 | Lee smiled as he examined his new acquisition， a vintage MG roadster. | 李含着笑审视他的新收藏品———一辆古典的 MG 敞篷汽车。 |
| 3 | amplify | /ˈæmplifai/ | vt./vi. | to make larger or more powerful | 放大； 加强； 扩展 | The fact that he is a very persuasive and influential person amplified all of the problems that he was causing among the other employees. | 他以非常有说服力和影响力的事实扩大了他在其他员工间所引起的问题。 |
| 4 | asset | /ˈæset/ | n. | anything owned that has value； a desirable thing； the property of a person | 有用的东西； 优点； 长处； 资产 | Her leadership qualities were the greatest asset of the company. | 她的领导能力是公司最大的资产。 |
| 5 | buyout | /ˈbaiˈaut/ | n. | the purchase of a company or business buy out / bai aut/ v. to purchase all the business shares belonging to another | 全部买 下 （2 ） 买进全部产权或股权； 收购全部 | The reason that Microsoft is accused of forming a monopoly is that they buy out their competitors， leaving no one else to compete with them. | 微软之所以被指控垄断市场， 是因为他们把所有竞争者都买了下来， 以致于 无人可以与之匹敌。 |
| 6 | corporation | /ˌkɔːpəˈreiʃən/ | n. | a body of persons acting under a legal charter as a separate entity with privileges and liabilities | 法人； 公司 | As an international corporation， we handle clients from 80 different countries and have over $900 billion in assets. | 身为跨国大公司， 我们的客户分布全世界80 个国家， 并且我们拥有9000 亿 美元以上的资产。 |
| 7 | cutback | /ˈkʌtbæk/ | n. | a reduction or discontinuance | 减少； 取消 | London Underground said it may have to axe 500 signaling jobs because of government cutbacks in its investment. | 因为政府裁减投资金额， 伦敦地铁说他们可能需要解雇500 个信号工人。 |
| 8 | enterprise | /ˈentəpraiz/ | n. | a business organization | 企业； （冒险性的） 计划 | It was a risky enterprise， but Steve assured Mr. Chang that they both would make a lot of money. | 那是非常冒险的事业， 但是史蒂夫向常先生担保过他们双方一定会赚大钱。 |
| 9 | equity | /ˈekwiti/ | n. | fairness； justice； assets minus liabilities； net worth | 公平； 公正； 扣除抵押、 税金等后的剩余财产价值 | To keep his equity， Murphy must either sell the company or refinance. | 为了保有剩余资产， 墨菲不是得把公司卖掉， 就是得再筹措资金。 |
| 10 | financier | /faiˈnænsiə（r）/ | n. | a person， company， or government that provides money for projects or enterprises | 财政家； 金融业者； 资本家； 投资者 | Three well-known financiers are backing the Bell Construction Company in the building of the new hotel. 3 | 个有名的资本家在资助贝尔建筑公司盖新的旅馆。 |
| 11 | franchise | /ˈfræntʃaiz/ | n. | anthorization granted by a manufacturer to sell his products | 经销权； 加盟权 | Ever since he had eaten breakfast there as a child， Bobby Chen dreamed of owning his own McDonald’s franchise. | 自从小时候在麦当劳吃过早餐后， 博比·陈就一直梦想有一天能拥有自己的 麦当劳分店。 |
| 12 | launch | /lɔːntʃ/ | vt./vi. | to start； to put into action； to inaugurate； to initiate | 开办； 展开； 发起； 开始 | We are going to launch a new building project in Boston next Wednesday. | 我们下个星期三将在波士顿推出一个新的建筑计划。 |
| 13 | monopoly | /məˈnɔpəli/ | n. | exclusive ownership or control， as of a given commodity or business activity | 垄断； 独占； 专卖 | A federal grand jury declared Microsoft a monopoly and that its power was too broad for a free market economy. | 联邦大陪审团宣告微软是垄断公司， 而且对一个自由市场经济来说它拥有太 大的力量。 |
| 14 | phase | /feiz/ | n. | one of a sequence of apparent forms | 阶段 | During this phase of the building plans， we are going to install all electrical and plumbing outlets and begin installing a central air-conditioning unit. | 在建筑的这个阶段， 我们会装配所有的水电通路， 并开始装设中央空调系统。 |
| 15 | profile | /ˈprəufail/ | n. | a side view of an object or structure， especially of a human head； a brief biography； the state of being noticed | 侧面像； 略传； 引人注目的状态 | The O. J. Simpson Murder Trial was one of the most high profile cases in American legal history. O. J. | 辛普森被控杀人的案子是美国法律史上最具知名度的案件之一。 |
| 16 | proposition | /ˌprɔpəˈziʃən/ | n. | something offered for consideration； proposal | 提案； 建议； 计划 | That is a very attractive proposition， but he’s still hesitant about investing anything until he is absolutely sure that the risks are minimal. | 这是一个很吸引人的计划， 但是在他完全确定其风险性已经降到最低前， 他 还是不想太轻易就投资。 |
| 17 | prospectus | /prəsˈpektəs/ | n. | a formal summary of a proposed business or other ventures， sent out to prospective or buyers | 计划书； 说明书； 募 股书 | The mayor reviewed the prospectus outlining plans for the new baseball stadium. | 市长研究了这份描述兴建新棒球场的企划书。 |
| 18 | rival | /ˈraivəl/ | n. | （1 ） n. one who competes with or attempts to outdo another； one that equals another adj. competing | 竞争者； 对手； 匹敌者； 势均力敌者 （2） 竞争的 | The recent breakthrough in our CD technology will certainly stun our rivals as well as the public. | 最近我们在 CD 技术上的突破一定会使我们的竞争者和大众感到十分震惊。 |
| 19 | subsidiary | /səbˈsidiəri/ | n. | one that is connected with but of less importance than the main one， especially a company owned by another company | 子公司 | We will not directly handle the offices in Bangkok as they are just subsidiaries of our parent company. | 我们不会直接管理曼谷的公司， 因为他们只是我们的子公司。 |
| 20 | track record | /træk ˈrekɔːd/ | n. | a person’s， a company’s， or a product’s past performance， achievements， or failures | 过去的记录 | The job needs someone with a good track record in investment. | 这个工作需要的是一个有良好投资记录的人。 |

### Unit 3 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | acquire | /əˈkwaiə/ | vt. | to gain possession of | 获得 | The real estate tycoon went about trying to acquire as much property as he could before the market exploded and prices began to soar. | 这个不动产大亨试着要在市场爆炸、 价格飞涨之前取得尽可能多的房产。 |
| 2 | affiliate | /əˈfilieit/ | n. | a branch organization； a subsidiary | 支部； 分会； 子 公司 | The World Chess Federation has affiliates in around 120 countries. | 国际象棋联盟在大约120 个国家有分会。 |
| 3 | assess | /əˈses/ | vt. | to evaluate， especially for taxation； to judge the quality， importance， or worth of | 评估 （财产价值）； 评定 | After the earthquake， the carpenter inspected the house to assess the damage. | 地震过后， 木工检查房子以评估损害程度。 |
| 4 | benchmark | /ˈbentʃmaːk/ | n. | a standard of excellence， achievement， etc.； a standard or reference by which others can be measured or judged | 基准； 水准点； 标准 | The car industry is a benchmark for the economy. | 汽车工业可以作为经济指标。 |
| 5 | carry out | /ˈkæri aut/ | v. | to put into action or complete； to put into effect | 执行； 贯彻； 完成 | As Mr. Smith’s right hand man， Johnny would carry out any of his orders. | 身为史密斯先生的左右手， 强尼会贯彻他的任何指令。 |
| 6 | cut down | /kʌt daun/ | v. | to decrease； to reduce | 削减； 缩短 | All Nick’s friends told him to cut down on his coffee consumption， but he felt he couldn’t make it through the day without seven or eight cups. | 尼克所有的朋友都劝告他要少喝点咖啡， 但是他觉得如果他一天没喝上七八 杯咖啡， 他就撑不下去了。 |
| 7 | embezzle | /imˈbezl/ | vt./vi. | to steal money， etc. entrusted to one | 盗用； 挪用； 侵占 （公款等） | One former director embezzled $2 million of company funds. | 某前任经理侵占了公司两百万美元资金。 |
| 8 | entrepreneur | /ˌɔntrəprəˈnəː/ | n. | one who organizes， operates， and especially assumes the risk of a business venture | 企业家； 主办人 | He quit his job at the bank and became an entrepreneur with three different businesses. | 他放弃了在银行的工作， 成了拥有3 家不同公司的企业家。 |
| 9 | expand | /iksˈpænd/ | vt./vi. | to increase in size， extent， dimensions， and so forth | 扩张； 扩大 | Jim started with a small retail outlet and expanded it into a large multinational corporation. | 吉姆把刚开始时的一家小型零售店扩张成一家大型的跨国企业。 |
| 10 | firm | /fəːm/ | n. | a commercial partnership of two or more persons | 公司； 商行 | He has been one of our law firm’s most competent and valued lawyers for over 20 years. 20 | 多年来， 他一直是我们法律事务所里最有能力且最被重视的律师之一。 |
| 11 | integrate | /ˈintigreit/ | vt./vi. | to make into a whole； to unify； to join with something else | 统合； 整合 | Why don’t we combine the two teams and let them integrate the best aspects of each？ | 为什么我们不整合这两个团队， 使他们将各自的优点合而为一呢？ |
| 12 | merger | /ˈməːdʒə/ | n. | an act or result of combining， especially the union of two or more commercial interests or corporations | 合并； 并吞 | The merger of Time-Warner and America Online has annoyed Disney， who fears it will be cut out of the cable TV market. “ | 时代华纳” 和 “美国在线” 的合并案使迪士尼非常困扰， 因为他们害怕被 挤出有线电视市场。 |
| 13 | pending | /ˈpendiŋ/ | adj. | not yet decided or settled； inpending | 未决定的； 待决定的； 即将发生的 | A growing number of customers have been inquiring about the pending price rises. | 越来越多的顾客在询问近期将发生的物价调涨问题。 |
| 14 | preliminary | /priˈliminəri/ | adj. | prior to the main matter， action， or business | 初步的； 开始的 | Preliminary reports indicate that those trapped in the collapsed building are still alive though some are badly wounded. | 初步的报告显示被困在倒塌的建筑物里面的人都还活着， 虽然有些人严重 受伤。 |
| 15 | propose | /prəˈpəuz/ | vt./vi. | to put forward for consideration； to suggest | 提议； 建议 | I’d like to propose an alternative which might， in the long run， prove to be even more financially rewarding. | 我想要提议另外一个计划， 长期来看， 这个计划会有更高的投资报酬率。 |
| 16 | prospect | /ˈprɔspekt/ | n. | something expected or foreseen； outlook； anticipation | 期望或预见的事； 展望； 期望 | Having been on the job hunt for six weeks， Joe sat down to examine his prospects for future employment. | 找工作找了6 个星期， 乔坐下来思考他将来找到工作的可能性。 |
| 17 | prosperity | /prɔsˈperiti/ | n. | the condition of being successful， especially in financial respects | 繁荣； 成功； 兴隆 | The prosperity of this new generation does not help them understand the plight of those who are less fortunate. | 新一代的富裕无助于他们了解那些较不幸人们的困境。 |
| 18 | scheme | /ˈskiːm/ | vt./vi. | to plan or design systematically | 计划； 设计； 图 谋 | He planned and schemed， trying to devise a way to get a promotion. | 他又计划又设计的， 想尽办法以得到升迁。 |
| 19 | thrive | /θraiv/ | vi. | to grow vigorously； to flourish； to prosper | 茂盛； 兴 旺； 繁荣 | Coming from a broken home， Timmy thrived on attention from his peers and would do anything to get it. | 出身破碎的家庭的提米靠引起同僚的注意而风光， 并且会不择手段得到众人 瞩目。 |
| 20 | undervalue | /ˈʌndəˈvæljuː/ | vt. | to value below the real worth； to have insufficient regard for | 低估价值； 看轻 | Many companies deal with their female employees in a way that undervalues them. | 很多公司以看轻女性员工价值的方式来对待她们。 |

## Unit 4　Finance & Investment 财务及投资

### Unit 4 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | account | /əˈkaunt/ | n. | a precise list or enumeration of financial transactions | 账目； 账单； 账户 | Based on a premonition， he removed all of his money from his bank account days before the stock market crash. | 凭着预感， 他在股市崩盘几天前将所有资金从银行账户转出去。 |
| 2 | balance | /ˈbæləns/ | n. | an amount that remains or that is left over | 余额； 差 额 | Judging by the balance of his bank account， Mike could tell that none of his checks had been cashed. | 根据账户里的余额判断， 迈克知道他所有的支票都没有被兑现。 |
| 3 | bond | /bɔnd/ | n. | an official paper promising to pay a sum of money to the person who holds it， esp. one by which a government or company borrows money from the public with the promise of paying it back with interest at a fixed time | 债券 | Many people find it safe and financially rewarding to invest in stocks and bonds that will accrue interest over a long period of time. | 许多人都认为投资于能长期累积利息的股票和债券是安全和报酬率高的投资 方式。 |
| 4 | chamber of commerce | /ˈtʃeimbə əv ˈkɔməs/ | n. | a group of businessmen， esp. in a particular town or area， working together for the purpose of improving trade | 商会 | If you want to know more about the retail stores in the area， you can go to the Chamber of Commerce and see what information they have on them. | 如果你想更了解这地区的零售商店， 你可以到商会去询问他们有哪些信息。 |
| 5 | credit | /ˈkredit/ | n. | belief or confidence； favorable reputation； praise or approval； a sum available to one （as in a bank account）； trust in one’s ability to meet payments when due | 信任； 信用； 信誉； 功劳； 荣誉； 存款 数额； 融资； 赊销 | Shopping on the Internet and paying with a credit card can be convenient and dangerous at the same time. | 通过网络购物并使用信用卡付费可能很方便， 但也很危险。 |
| 6 | debt | /det/ | n. | something owed to someone else | 债； 债务 | The national debt has gotten to the point where it will probably never be paid off. | 国家债务已高到可能永远都无法还清的地步。 |
| 7 | deficit | /ˈdefisit/ | n. | the amount by which something less than what is needed， esp. the amount by which money that goes out is more than money that comes in | 赤字； 亏损额 | The last time I went to the bank to withdraw money， they said my account had a deficit of over a hundred dollars. | 我上次到银行提钱时， 他们告知我账户中有超过100 美元的欠款。 |
| 8 | depreciation | /diˌpriːʃiˈeiʃən/ | n. | decrease in value | 跌价； 贬值 | Severe inflation has led to a great depreciation in the value of that nation’s currency. | 严重的通货膨胀致使那个国家的货币大幅度贬值。 |
| 9 | devaluation | /ˌdiːvæljuˈeiʃən/ | n. | a reduction in the value of something， esp. in the official exchange value of money | 贬值 （尤指货币） | A crash in the Hong Kong stock market brought a devaluation of some Asian countries’ currency. | 香港股市的崩盘导致一些亚洲国家货币的贬值。 |
| 10 | investor | /inˈvestə（r）/ | n. | a person or organization that buys stocks or shares， or pays money into a bank in order to receive a profit | 投资者； 出资者 | The investor can consider the brokers’ or experts’ advice and recommendations. | 投资者可以考虑一下股票经纪人或专家们的建议和推介。 |
| 11 | exempt | /igˈzempt/ | adj. | freed from a duty， service， payment， etc. | 被 免除的； 豁免的 | Susan had already made an A on her test so she was exempt from taking the final one. | 苏珊考试已经得了一个 A， 所以她被豁免可以不用考最后一次。 |
| 12 | expense | /ikˈspens/ | n. | cost in money， time or effort | 消耗； 消费 | Add up all of these receipts and submit a report of your expenses. | 把所有收据的数字加一加， 然后提出你消费金额的报告。 |
| 13 | fluctuation | /ˌflʌktjuˈeiʃən/ | n. | continual change from one point or condition to another | 波动； 起伏； 涨落 | All of last yeas’s fluctuations in the budget will no longer pose a problem as we have revised our spending policies. | 在修改支出政策后， 所有去年在预算上的波动将不再成为问题。 |
| 14 | gross | /grəus/ | adj. | without deductions； total | 总共的； 全部的 | The national gross sales in June totaled $729 million. | 国内6 个月销售总额为729， 000， 000 美元。 |
| 15 | inflation | /inˈfleiʃən/ | n. | a continuing rise in prices | 物价飞涨； 通货膨胀 | Inflation continues to cause the value of the New Taiwan Dollar to decrease each year. | 通货膨胀持续使新台币逐年贬值。 |
| 16 | overdraft | /ˈəuvədraːft/ | n. | an act or instance of overdrawing a checking account； the amount overdrawn | 透支； 透支额 | Jennie noticed her account had an overdraft and she cursed herself for being so careless as she would doubtlessly have to deal with bounced checks and irate retailers. | 珍妮发觉她的账户透支， 她谴责自己的不小心， 因为她无疑将必须面对被银 行拒收的支票和愤怒的零售商。 |
| 17 | profit | /ˈprɔfit/ | n. | money gained by trade or business； benefit | 利润； 收 益； 营利 | It seems that if we were to put more funding into marketing and less in research， we could produce a substantially larger profit by attracting more buyers. | 如果我们投入更多的资金到市场， 而少花些钱做研究， 我们可以借此吸引更 多的消费者来创造更大、 更可观的利润。 |
| 18 | revenue | /ˈrevinjuː/ | n. | income， esp. that which the government receives as tax | 收益； 岁入； 税收 | The government was short of money because of falling export revenues. | 由于外销岁入的减少， 政府的财力有些拮据。 |
| 19 | stock | /stɔk/ | n. | money lent to a government or company， on which interest is paid； the shares of a particular company or corporation | 公 债； 证券； 股票 | Emma learned how to speculate in the stock market by tracking the status of stocks that her father owned. | 通过了解她父亲持有的投票涨跌的情形， 爱玛学会了如何投机玩股票。 |
| 20 | withdrawal | /wiðˈdrɔːəl/ | n. | the act of taking back or removing | 收回； 取 回； 撤回； 提款 | ATMs make it convenient for people to make withdrawals without having to go to the bank and stand in line. | 自动提款机使人们可以很便利地领到钱， 而不需要跑到银行去大排长龙。 |

### Unit 4 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | accountant | /əˈkautənt/ | n. | one that keeps， audits， and inspects the financial records of individuals or business concerns and prepares financial and tax reports | 会计师； 会计员 | After studying for four years at the university to be an accountant， Jane decided she didn’t want to work with numbers but wanted to go into acting. | 珍念了4 年大学打算成为一位会计师， 这会儿却决定她不想和数字打交道 了， 而要改行当演员。 |
| 2 | bankrupt | /ˈbæŋkrʌpt/ | adj. | unable to pay one’s debts | 破产的； 倒闭的 | If we continue to take in so little with such high expenditures， we will find ourselves bankrupt before we know it. | 倘若我们持续入不敷出， 我们将很快破产。 |
| 3 | capital | /ˈkæpitəl/ | n. | wealth， esp. money used to produce more wealthy or for starting a business | 资金； 资本 | A smart investor will invest his capital to the best advantage. | 聪明的投资者会将资金做最有利的投资。 |
| 4 | convert | /kənˈvəːt/ | vt./vi. | to change into another form， substance， or state | 改变； 转换 | It would be wise to convert some of your money into foreign currency before traveling abroad. | 在出国旅游以前先兑换一些外币是明智之举。 |
| 5 | currency | /ˈkʌrənsi/ | n. | the money in circulation in any country； general use or acceptance | 货币； 流通； 通用 | Blake got so excited upon seeing the Roman coin because he is an avid collector of foreign currency. | 布莱克非常兴奋能看到那枚罗马钱币， 因为他热衷收集外币。 |
| 6 | deduction | /diˈdʌkʃən/ | n. | the process of subtracting or taking away （a quantity）； the amount deducted | 扣除； 扣除额 | Making donations to various charities is an excellent way to get a tax deduction. | 捐钱给一些不同的慈善机构是减税的好方法。 |
| 7 | deposit | /diˈpɔzit/ | vt./vi. | to pay money that will be returned later if certain conditions are kept； to place （money， etc.） for safekeeping， as in a bank | 付押金； 付保证金 | Upon receipt of the goods， according to our contract， we will deposit the money directly into your bank account. | 根据合约， 我们会在收到货品后直接将钱存入您的银行账户。 |
| 8 | depression | /diˈpreʃən/ | n. | a period of reduced business and high unemployment； a feeling of sadness and hopelessness | 不景气； 忧伤； 消沉； 沮丧 | Urging consumers not to panic， the president reassures that the current downward trend in the economy is temporary and not the onset of a depression. | 总统力促消费者勿恐慌， 他再次保证目前经济衰退的趋势只是暂时性的， 而 非经济不景气的开始。 |
| 9 | discrepancy | /disˈkrepənsi/ | n. | difference， or lack of agreement or similarity | 差异； 不同； 不一致 | In reviewing the reports of two of the witnesses， the detective found many discrepancies and decided to investigate the case further. | 在看过两位目击证人证词的报告书后， 警探发现很多矛盾之处， 所以决定 对此案件作进一步的调查。 |
| 10 | estimate | /ˈestimeit/ | n. | （1） n. the act of evaluating or appraising vt. to judge or calculate the nature， value， size， amount， etc. of something | 估计； 估价 （2） 估计； 估价 | After seeing the damage done to the front fender of the car， the mechanic quoted a repair estimate of $700. | 在看过车子前面挡泥板的损害程度之后， 技工报出的预估修理费为700 美金。 |
| 11 | expenditure | /ikˈspenditʃə/ | n. | spending or using up； the amount of money， time， etc. spent | 消费； 开销 | The employee argued with his boss， saying that a health club membership was a necessary business trip expenditure. | 职员与雇主争论健身俱乐部的会员费是商务旅行的必要花费。 |
| 12 | fiscal | /ˈfiskəl/ | adj. | of or related to public money， taxes， debts， etc. | 财政的； 会计的 | Our fiscal policies will remain unchanged and we will continue to strive for higher and higher profit margins. | 我们的财经政策将维持不变， 而我们也将持续为获取更大的利益而努力。 |
| 13 | fund | /fʌnd/ | n. | a supply or sum of money set apart for a special purpose | 基金； 专款 | It would be much appreciated if you would continue to support our research through government funds. | 如果你们能持续以政府的专款来赞助我们的研究工作， 我们将会非常感激。 |
| 14 | holding | /ˈhəuldiŋ/ | n. | land； （usually pl. ）property owned， esp. stocks or bonds | 土地； 财产 （常用复数） | The company’s net worth， including all stocks， bonds， and holdings， exceeds 5 billion dollars. | 这个公司的净值包含股票、 债券、 地产等， 超过50 亿美元。 |
| 15 | ledger | /ˈledʒə/ | n. | an account book recording the money taken in and given out by a business， bank， etc. | 总账； 分类账 | Upon further examination， it was discovered that the ledger was falsified and that allowed him to embezzle from the company. | 在进一步的审查后， 我们发现账簿被做假， 使他有机会盗用公司的公款。 |
| 16 | overdue | /ˈəuvəˈdjuː/ | adj. | left unpaid too long | 过期未付的 | Due to seven overdue credit card payments， we will be unable to process your loan request until you can improve your credit rating. | 由于您仍有7 笔过期未付的信用卡款项， 除非您的信用度有所改善， 否则我 们将无法处理您的贷款案。 |
| 17 | remuneration | /riˌmjuːnəˈreiʃən/ | n. | pay； payment； reward | 报酬； 酬劳 | Ted just received remuneration for the house-painting job he did on the Smith house last week. | 泰德刚收到上周替史密斯家的房子油漆的酬劳。 |
| 18 | shareholder | /ˈʃɛəhəuldə/ | n. | an owner of shares in a business | 股东 | If we purchase another $100， 000 in stocks， we will surpass James Dunn as the primary shareholder. | 如果我们再购入价值10 万美金的股票， 我们将会取代詹姆斯·唐而成为最 大股东。 |
| 19 | stockbroker | /ˈstɔkbrəukə/ | n. | someone whose job is buying and selling stocks and shares for other people | 证券和股票经纪人 | Actually I am not an accountant but a stockbroker since I handle finances and stock market investments for several companies. | 事实上我不是会计师而是个股票经纪人， 因为我帮数家公司管理财务和投资 股票。 |
| 20 | yield | /jiːld/ | n. | （1 ） n. that which is produced or the amount that is produced vt. to produce， bear， or provide， esp. as a result of work or effort | 生产量； 收益 （2） 生产； 产生 | If we were to combine all the yield from all six branches， you would see clearly the incredible productivity the new machines have afforded us. | 如果我们能将6 家分公司的生产量结合， 你将可以清楚地看到这些新设备提 供给我们的惊人产能。 |

## Unit 5　Commerce 贸易

### Unit 5 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | bid | /bid/ | n. | （1） n. a bidding； the amount bid vt. /vi. to offer to pay for goods or to charge for one’s work or services； to tender | 投标； 出价 （2） 出价； 投标； 喊价 | Raising his hand， Jim signaled that he was placing a bid of $1， 500 for the antique chair. | 吉姆举手示意出价1， 500 美金， 想买下那只古董椅。 |
| 2 | commercial | /kəˈməːʃəl/ | adj. | of or related to business； made or done for profit | 商业化的； 商用的； 营利的 | I’ve considered going into commercial business， but I still have a year before I graduate from college to make that decision. | 我考虑过进入商界， 不过在大学毕业前， 我还有一年时间可以作决定。 |
| 3 | competitor | /kəmˈpetitə/ | n. | one who tries to win or contends with another or others | 竞争者； 对手 | Even though there are so many other fast food chains， the revenue of McDonald’s is so much higher that one could hardly call them competitors. | 即使有那么多其他的快餐连锁店， 麦当劳的营业额还是高出很多， 以至于那 些店根本不能真的被称为是它的 “竞争者”。 |
| 4 | consolidate | /kənˈsɔlideit/ | vt./vi. | to combine into one； to become stronger or firmer | 结合； 合并； 强化； 巩固 | With ABC Loans， you can consolidate all of your bills into one low monthly payment. | 有了 ABC 的贷款， 你可以将所有账款合并成一项月付的低利支出。 |
| 5 | contract | /ˈkɔntrækt/ | n. | a formal written agreement， having the force of law， between two or more people or groups | 契约； 合同 | When two parties enter into a legal contract ， they are both bound by law to complete what they have agreed to do. | 当两方签订合法合约后， 二者都为法律所束缚， 必须完成所承诺的事项。 |
| 6 | corporate | /ˈkɔːpərit/ | adj. | belonging to， or shared by all the members of a group； of or belonging to a corporation | 全体的； 团体的； 公司的； 法 人的 | We have corporate offices located in ten states and three different foreign countries. | 我们公司的办事处遍及10 个州和3 个不同的国家。 |
| 7 | credible | /ˈkredəbl/ | adj. | deserving to be believed， trusted， or taken seriously | 可信的； 可靠的 | Several years after we founded this company， people began to look at us as a credible and legitimate provider of insurance. | 公司成立几年之后， 人们开始视我们为一家诚信、 合法的保险业者。 |
| 8 | debit | /ˈdebit/ | n. | a record in a book of accounts of money spent or owed （ | 账簿上的） 借方； 借项 | Many college students who are prone to overspending have begun to use debit cards. | 许多具有过度消费倾向的大学生都已开始使用金融卡 （直接从银行户头里扣款）。 |
| 9 | earnings | /ˈəːniŋz/ | n. | money made by working； money made by a company or government | 薪水； 工资； 收益 | Through playing the stock market， Roger has yearly earnings that exceed $300， 000. | 经由股票市场的获利， 罗杰每年所得超过30 万美金。 |
| 10 | export | /ˈekspɔːt/ | n. | （1 ） n. （ the business of） exporting； something exported vt. /vi. to send （goods） out of a country for sale | 输出； 出口货 （2） 输出； 出口 | America’s major exports include beef， sugar and produce. | 美国主要的出口货物包含牛肉、 糖和农产品。 |
| 11 | haggle | /ˈhægl/ | vi. | to argue about terms， price， etc. | 讨价还价 | Mrs. Kirk likes to haggle with the sales clerk at the department store. | 柯克太太喜欢跟百货商店的售货员讨价还价。 |
| 12 | import | /ˈimpɔːt/ | n. | （1 ） n. （ the business of） importing； something imported vt. /vi. to bring in （ something， esp. goods） from another place or esp. another country | 进 口； 输 入 物 （2 ） 输入； 进口 | There has been a rise in the number of cars imported from Japan recently. | 最近从日本进口的汽车数量增加了。 |
| 13 | invoice | /ˈinvɔis/ | n. | a list of goods supplied or work done， stating quantity and price | 发票； 发货清单； 完成工作的清单 （明列数量和价钱） | For every order， you will need to fill out an invoice and staple it to the receipt. | 对于每一项订货， 你都必须填写发货单， 并把它钉在收据上。 |
| 14 | long-range | /ˈlɔŋˈreindʒ/ | adj. | about or covering a long time or distance | 长期的； 远程的 | One of our long-range goals is to set up a new branch sometime next summer. | 我们的长远计划之一是在明年夏天建立一家分公司。 |
| 15 | stock | /stɔk/ | n. | （a supply of） goods for sale， esp. in a shop （ | 商店的） 现货； 存货 | They don’t have much stock left after the Christmas rush. | 圣诞节的购物热潮之后， 他们的现货所剩不多。 |
| 16 | payment | /ˈpeimənt/ | n. | the act of paying | 支付； 付款 | All payments should be made by checks or money orders and mailed to the address indicated on the bottom of the order form. | 所有付款请以支票或汇票寄至订购单下方注明的地址。 |
| 17 | quote | /kwəut/ | vt. | to state the price of something | 报价 | They quoted a very reasonable price for the new car. | 他们新车的报价非常合理。 |
| 18 | supply | /səˈplai/ | n. | （1） n. the amount available for use or sale； stock vt. to provide （something that is needed） | 供 应 （品）； 供给 （物）； 库存 （2 ） 供应 （ 所需物品 ） | If the supply of a particular item exceeds the demand， the price will go down. | 通常若一样产品供过于求， 价格就会往下降。 |
| 19 | tariff | /ˈtærif/ | n. | a tax collected by a government on goods coming into or sometimes going out of a country | 关税 | America wants to eliminate tariffs on items such as electronics. | 美国想要取消如电子产品等货物的关税。 |
| 20 | voucher | /ˈvautʃə/ | n. | one who gives， or serves as， assurance， a guarantee， etc.； a paper serving as evidence or proof； a kind of ticket that may be used instead of money for a particular purpose； warrant； receipt； coupon | 保证人； 凭证； 凭单； 折价券 | The money was lent without any voucher. | 这钱没有任何担保就借出了。 |

### Unit 5 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | bill of lading | /bil əv ˈleidiŋ/ | n. | a list of goods carried， esp. on a ship | 提 货单 | The foreman walked around the large vessel inspecting the latest delivery while looking at the bill of lading to make sure it was all there. | 领班在大船上走动， 检查刚进的一批货， 并按照提货单确定货物是否齐全。 |
| 2 | commodity | /kəˈmɔditi/ | n. | an article of trade or commerce， esp. a mineral or farm product | 商品； 农产品 | If you file for bankruptcy， I believe they will confiscate all of your personal commodities and leave you with nothing. | 如果你宣告破产， 我相信你的所有财产将会充公， 结果你会一无所有。 |
| 3 | consignee | /ˌkɔnsaiˈniː/ | n. | the person to whom something is delivered | 收 件人； 受托人 | Frank agreed to be the consignee for these goods. | 弗兰克同意作为这批货物的收件人。 |
| 4 | consumer | /kənˈsjuːmə/ | n. | a person who buys and uses goods and services | 消费者； 顾客 | Many large companies do surveys to ascertain their consumers’ opinions and shopping habits. | 许多大公司都会做调查来探知消费者的意见和购物习惯。 |
| 5 | contractor | /kənˈtræktə/ | n. | a person or company that formally agrees to do work or provide supplies in large amounts， esp. to provide building materials or workers for building jobs | 立契约人； 承包商 | We have hired a new contractor to handle the details of the construction of the large cultural center. | 我们已雇用一个新的承包商来负责这个大型文化中心的细部建筑工程。 |
| 6 | cost-effective | /ˈkɔstiˈfektiv/ | adj. | bringing the best possible profits or advantages for the lowest possible cost | 符合成本效益的 | Many manufacturers are trying to find more cost-effective ways to advertise their products so that they can save money. | 许多制造商已开始寻找更具成本效益的方式来为他们的商品打广告以节省 经费。 |
| 7 | dealer | /ˈdiːlə/ | n. | a person in a stated type of business | 商人； 经销商 | The average age of an inner-city drug dealer has dramatically dropped in the last ten years. | 贫民区的毒贩平均年龄在最近10 年大幅度地下降。 |
| 8 | due | /djuː/ | adj. | owed or owing as a debt or right； payable； expected or supposed to happen， arrive， etc. | 应付的； 到期的； 该发生的 | The total amount due includes all main courses， beverages， desserts and 15% gratuity. | 全部的应付账款包含主菜、 饮料、 点心和15% 的小费。 |
| 9 | endorse | /inˈdɔːs/ | vt. | to write on the back of （a title， check， etc.）； to support； to approve； to sanction | 背书； 支援； 赞同 | A well-known actress endorsed their product. | 有一个著名的女演员为他们的产品背书。 |
| 10 | forward | /ˈfɔːwəd/ | vt. | to send or pass on to a new address | 送到； 转递 | I am not getting my mail because the people who live in my old house will not forward my mail to me. | 我没收到信， 因为住在我旧房子的人不会把信转给我。 |
| 11 | headquarters | /ˈhedˌkwɔːtəz/ | n. | the main office or center of operations | 总公司； 总部； 司令部 | The headquarters of Hershey Chocolates， in Hershey， Pennsylvania offers free tours daily between the hours of 9 A. M. and 4 P. M. | 位于宾州贺喜的好时巧克力总公司每日从早上9 点到下午4 点提供免费参观。 |
| 12 | inventory | /ˈinventəri/ | n. | an itemized list of goods， property， etc，； the store of goods； stock | 详细目录； 清单； 存货 | Before leaving， he made an inventory of everything that was to stay. | 在离开之前， 他详细列出要留下来的东西。 |
| 13 | letter of credit | /ˈletə əv ˈkredit/ | n. | an official letter from a bank allowing a named person to take money from another bank， esp. in a foreign country | 信用状 | We will send out the shipment as soon as we receive your letter of credit. | 我们一接到信用担保书， 就会马上把货物寄过去。 |
| 14 | order | /ˈɔːdə/ | n. | a request to supply goods； a written or printed paper which allows the holder to do something， e. g. to be paid money | 订货； 订 单； 汇票； 汇单 | We have no shampoo in stock but it is on order. | 我们没有洗发精的存货， 但已经订购了。 |
| 15 | patent | /ˈpeitənt/ | n. | （1 ） n. a paper from a government office giving someone the right to make or sell a new invention for a certain number of years vt. to obtain a patent | 专利 （2） 取得……的专利 | Albert Einstein worked for many years as a patent clerk before becoming a famous scientist. | 爱因斯坦在成为有名的科学家之前曾担任专利人员多年。 |
| 16 | quota | /ˈkwəutə/ | n. | an amount that has been officially fixed as someone’s share | 定量； 定额； 配额 | The university has exceeded its quota of law students. | 那所大学的法律系学生已经超额了。 |
| 17 | shipment | /ˈʃipmənt/ | n. | a load of goods sent together by sea， road or air | 一批货 | The goods you ordered last week are ready for shipment as soon as a truck is available. | 等货车一有空， 你上星期订的货立刻可以装运。 |
| 18 | surplus | /ˈsəːpləs/ | n. | （1） n. an additional amount adj. additional to what is needed or used | 过剩的量； 盈余 （2 ） 过剩的； 剩余的 | Due to a drop in demand， our warehouses have been stuffed with loads of surplus goods. | 由于需求降低， 我们的仓库塞满了大量过剩的货品。 |
| 19 | trademark | /ˈtreidmaːk/ | n. | a special name， sign， word， etc. used by a manufacturer or dealer to distinguish a product， usually protected by law （ | 注册） 商标 | Trademarks of companies are protected by law and cannot be copied or duplicated without permission. | 公司的商标是受法律保护的， 未经许可， 不可盗印或复制。 |
| 20 | warehouse | /ˈwɛəhaus/ | n. | a building where goods are stored； storehouse； depot； wholesale store | 仓库； 货价； 大商店 | Companies often have large warehouses in which they can store surplus items for future sale. | 公司通常有大型的仓库来存放多余的货物， 以便将来出售。 |

## Unit 6　Business Meeting & Negotiation 商务会议及谈判

### Unit 6 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | adjourn | /əˈdʒəːn/ | vt./vi. | to bring （a meeting， trial， etc.） to a stop， esp. for a short period or until a later time | 休会； 延期 | The judge has decided to adjourn for today because of the fight in the courtroom. | 因为法庭里面的争斗事件， 法官决定中止今日的审判议程。 |
| 2 | agreement | /əˈgriːmənt/ | n. | an understanding between two people， parties， countries， etc.； accord； pact | 同意； 协定 | I hope the two firms will be able to reach a mutually satisfying agreement. | 我希望这两家公司可以达成彼此都满意的协议。 |
| 3 | amendment | /əˈmendmənt/ | n. | a change to improve a rule， law， statement， etc. | 修正 | There will be a meeting today to vote on a new amendment to be added to the company’s charter. | 今天会有一场会议来投票表决是否要将一项新的修正案加到公司的规章里。 |
| 4 | approval | /əˈpruːvəl/ | n. | favorable opinion or judgment | 赞成； 同意 | I’m not sure whether or not your proposal received approval from the board； however， I do know that two of the members mentioned their strong support for it. | 我不确定委员会是否同意了你的这项提案， 但我知道有两位委员对此提案深 表支持。 |
| 5 | attentive | /əˈtentiv/ | adj. | taking careful notice， listening carefully | 注意的； 留心的； 专心的 | Due to a condition called Attention Deficit Disorder， Bob found it very difficult to be attentive to what the teacher said. | 因为患了注意力短缺失调症， 鲍伯发现自己很难专心听老师讲课。 |
| 6 | board | /bɔːd/ | n. | an official body or group that has responsibility for a particular organization or activity | 理事会； 委员会； 董事会； 局； 部； 会 | The board only meets when there are urgent matters concerning the welfare of the entire company. | 董事会只在与全公司员工福利相关的紧急事故发生时才召开会议。 |
| 7 | brainstorm | /ˈbreinˌstɔːm/ | vt. | to try to solve problems， develop new ideas， etc. by unrestrained and spontaneous participation in discussion | 脑力激荡 | Our last advertising effort failed miserably， so I think we should spend more time brainstorming to get the best ideas possible. | 我们上回的广告惨遭失败， 所以 我想我们应该找些时间集思广益， 看是否能想出最好的点子。 |
| 8 | concede | /kənˈsiːd/ | vt./vi. | to admit as true， correct or proper， etc.； to give as a right | 容忍； 容许； 让步 | After much discussion， we have conceded to support their plan to reconfigure the computer system. | 几经讨论， 我们同意支持他们重组电脑系统的计划。 |
| 9 | conference | /ˈkɔnfərəns/ | n. | a formal meeting for discussion | 会议； 协商 会； 讨论会 | The journalists’ conference will be attended by some of the top reporters in the nation. | 此次新闻从业人员会议将有国内一些顶尖的记者参与。 |
| 10 | convention | /kənˈvenʃən/ | n. | generally accepted practice； a meeting or formal assembly， as of representatives or delegates | 惯例； 常规； 习俗； 会议； 大会 | The International Trade Convention will be held in Geneva next month. | 国际贸易大会下个月将在日内瓦举行。 |
| 11 | convince | /kənˈvins/ | vt. | to make someone completely certain about something | 使信服 | I just can’t come up with a way to convince my boss that these weekly meetings are a real waste of a time. | 我实在想不出好方法来使老板相信每周的会议其实都在浪费时间. |
| 12 | cross-reference | /ˈkrɔsˈrefərəns/ | n. | （1 ） n. a note directing the reader from one place in a book to another place in the same book vt. to provide with cross references （ | 前后参照 （2） 使） 前后对照 | Make sure you cross-reference all of your sources and add them to your bibliography. | 你要确定交叉列举所有的参考资料， 并将它们加注在参考书目里。 |
| 13 | delegate | /ˈdeligit/ | n. | a person authorized to act as representative for others； representative | 代表 | He was selected to be one of the delegates to go on a tour to Bosnia to promote peace in the war-torn country. | 他被推选为代表团的一员， 到饱受战火肆虐的波斯尼亚推动和平。 |
| 14 | demand | /diˈmaːnd/ | vt. | to ask for firmly and not be willing to accept a refusal | 要求； 强求 | The kidnappers demanded no less than one million dollars for the safe return of his daughter. | 绑匪要求超过100 万美元的赎金， 以换取他女儿的平安归来。 |
| 15 | dispute | /disˈpjuːt/ | n. | （1） n. an argument or quarrel vt. /vi. to argue about something （angrily） | 争端； 争执； 纠纷 （2） 争论； 争执； 争吵 | In order to settle the dispute peacefully， both sides have to compromise and give up something. | 为了和平解决纷争， 双方都必须妥协， 放弃一部分的需求。 |
| 16 | negotiation | /niˌgəuʃiˈeiʃən/ | n. | an act of discussing with a view to reaching agreement | 协商； 谈判 | We’ll not begin official negotiations until they commit to ending this silly dispute. | 除非他们保证不再做这种愚蠢的争论， 否则我们就不会开始正式的协商。 |
| 17 | persuade | /pəˈsweid/ | vt./vi. | to make someone willing to do something by reasoning， arguing， repeatedly asking， etc. | 说服； 劝服 | Is there any way I could persuade you to put out that cigarette？ | 我有什么办法能说服你捻熄那根烟呢？ |
| 18 | postpone | /pəˈspəun/ | vt./vi. | to put off until a later time； to defer； to delay | 延迟； 延期 | He decided to postpone the expedition until the storm is over. | 他决定把探险延到暴风雨结束之后。 |
| 19 | reconcile | /ˈrekənsail/ | vt. | to find agreement between two opposing ideas， situations， etc. | 调和； 调解 | It would be better to reconcile differences between employees and their boss than to constantly have contention at the work place. | 能调解员工与老板之前的差异要比弄得职场纷争不断好多了。 |
| 20 | settlement | /ˈsetlmənt/ | n. | an agreement ending an argument； a payment of money claimed | 协议； 支付 | She accepted an out-court settlement of $2. 5 million. | 她接受了一笔250 万美金的庭外和解费。 |

### Unit 6 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | agenda | /əˈdʒendə/ | n. | a list of the subjects to be dealt with or talked about at a meeting | 议程 | According to today’s agenda， we are supposed to talk about the new project. | 根据今日的议程， 我们应该讨论那项新企划案。 |
| 2 | alternative | /ɔːlˈtəːnətiv/ | n. | something， esp. a course of action， that may be taken or chosen instead of one or more others | 可供选择的事物、 方式等 | I don’t think that there are any alternatives left except to sell all of the company’s assets. | 我认为除了卖掉公司所有的资产外， 我们别无选择。 |
| 3 | announce | /əˈnauns/ | vt./vi. | to make known publicly | 通告； 宣布； 宣告； 公之于众 | The press conference announced yesterday that there would be an official merger of the two companies. | 昨日的记者会宣布了这两家公司将正式合并的消息。 |
| 4 | articulate | /aːˈtikjulit/ | adj. | expressing or able to express thoughts and feelings clearly and effectively | 能清楚有力地表达思想感情的； 表达能力 强的 | The child was unable to offer an articulate description of what she had witnessed. | 这个小孩无法清楚表达她所目睹的一切。 |
| 5 | attorney | /əˈtəːni/ | n. | a lawyer | 律师 | My attorney suggests we have a discussion with him before making any decisions. | 我的律师建议我们在作决定之前应先与他讨论。 |
| 6 | breakthrough | /ˈbreikˌθruː/ | n. | an important advance or discovery （often after earlier failures） | 突破； 重大进展 | The company looks poised to make a significant breakthrough in China. | 这公司看来已经准备好进军中国。 |
| 7 | compromise | /ˈkɔmprəmaiz/ | n. | setting an argument or difference of opinion by each side agreeing to some of the demands of the other； an agreement reached in this way | 妥协； 折中； 和解 | We won’t， for any reason， compromise our ethical and moral standards for any amount of profit or advantage. | 不论在任何情况下， 我们的伦理与道德标准绝不因利益好处而有所妥协。 |
| 8 | conclusive | /kənˈkluːsiv/ | adj. | putting an end to doubt or uncertainty | 决定 性的； 勿庸置疑的 | The DNA testing of the bloody clothes was conclusive in its implication that this man is the murderer. | 对血衣的 DNA 测试显示， 这个男人勿庸置疑就是凶手。 |
| 9 | consensus | /kənˈsensəs/ | n. | a general agreement | 一致的意见； 共识 | We have discussed this for hours and the general consensus is that you should resign. | 经过我们数小时的讨论， 大家的共识是你应该辞职。 |
| 10 | converse | /kənˈvəːs/ | vi. | to talk informally； to have a conversation | 谈话； 交谈 | If you have time tomorrow， I’d really like to meet you for lunch and converse a little about my new ideas. | 如果你明天有空， 我真的很希望跟你见面吃顿午饭， 谈谈我的新构想。 |
| 11 | criterion | /kraiˈtiəriən/ | n. | a standard against which something is measured | 标准； 规范 （pl. criteria） | The most important criterion for entry is that applicants must pass the TOEIC test. | 录取最重要的标准是申请者必须通过 TOEIC 考试。 |
| 12 | decline | /diˈklain/ | vi. | to go from a better to a worse position | 衰落； 衰退； 下降 | Lots of people think the standards of morality have declined much in recent years. | 许多人认为， 近几年来人们的道德标准下降了不少。 |
| 13 | demonstration | /ˌdemənsˈtreiʃən/ | n. | an act of showing or proving something； a public show of strong feeling or opinion， often with marching， signs， etc. | 示范； 实证； （游行） 示威 | I think it would be best if you can give a demonstration of how this new product works. | 我认为最好是你能示范一下这个新产品的用法。 |
| 14 | diplomacy | /diˈpləuməsi/ | n. | the art and practice of establishing and continuing relations between countries； skill at dealing with people and getting them to agree | 外交 （手腕） ； 交际手段 | You wouldn’t believe how much of a part diplomacy plays in normal everyday business negotiations. | 你不能想象外交手腕在我们每天的商业谈判上占多重要的地位。 |
| 15 | dissuade | /diˈsweid/ | vt. | to advise someone against doing something | 劝阻 | I’m trying as hard as I can to dissuade him from selling his shares of the company’s stock. | 我尽我所能地劝阻他不要抛售持有的公司股份。 |
| 16 | on behalf of | /ɔn biˈhaːf əv/ |  | for， in the interest of， or as the representative of | 代表 | On behalf of my wife and my family， I would like to thank all those who helped us in our time of need. | 我谨代表妻子和家人感谢所有在我们需要帮助时伸出援手的人。 |
| 17 | petition | /piˈtiʃən/ | n. | （1） n. a request or demand made to a government or other body， usually signed by many people vi. to make or send a petition | 请愿 （书） （2） 请愿 | In order to stop the developers from building on the wetlands， we need 1， 000 signatures on this petition. | 为了阻止开发者在湿地上建筑房舍， 我们需要 1000 人在这张请愿书上联合 署名。 |
| 18 | premise | /ˈpremis/ | n. | a statement or idea on which reasoning is based | 前提 | On what premise has he decided to go ahead without the approval of the rest of us？ | 他在什么前提之下决定不顾我们的意见一意孤行？ |
| 19 | seminar | /ˈseminaː/ | n. | a small group， esp. a class of advanced students with a teacher， meeting to talk about a subject | 研讨会； 讨论 课； 讲习会 | There is a special seminar at the local bank on personal accounting. | 在当地银行有一场个人理财的特别研讨会。 |
| 20 | statement | /ˈsteitmənt/ | n. | something that is stated； a written or spoken declaration， exp. of a formal kind | 声明； 陈述 | We can’t reimburse you until we receive an official statement listing all of your expenditures. | 要等收到正式的全部费用的明细之后， 我们才能付还你们的支出。 |

## Unit 7　Technology & Equipment 科技及设备

### Unit 7 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | advance | /ədˈvaːns/ | vt./vi. | to move or bring forward | 前进； 进展 | I remember when the original car phones were invented， they were thought to be a huge breakthrough， but look how much cellular phones have advanced since then. | 我还记得最初汽车电话发明时被认为是一项重大突破， 而如今， 移动电话是 多么进步。 |
| 2 | capacity | /kəˈpæsiti/ | n. | the potential or suitability for holding， storing， or accommodating； the maximum amount or number that can be contained or accommodated； ability or power | 容量； 才能； 能力 | Though the bus’s capacity was clearly marked， the driver still tried cram more people in than it could legally hold. | 虽然这辆公交车已清楚标明载容量， 司机仍然尝试挤入超过其所能承载的 人数。 |
| 3 | component | /kəmˈpəunənt/ | n. | any of the parts that together make a whole | 构成要素； 成分； 零件 | In an effort to save money， lots of companies will buy computer and electronic components from Asian countries where they are manufactured and sold at lower costs. | 为了尽量节省成本， 许多公司会购进亚洲国家制造并出售的价格低廉的电脑 和电子零件。 |
| 4 | device | /diˈvais/ | n. | a piece of equipment intended for a particular purpose； a plan or scheme； a picture or drawing | 装置； 设备； 图案； 设计 | The curious workmanship of the device prompted Eddie to stop and reach down to pick it up. | 这个装置独特的工艺品促使艾迪停下去把它捡起来。 |
| 5 | engineer | /ˌendʒiˈniə/ | n. | a person who is professionally trained to plan the making of machines， roads， bridges， electrical equipment， etc.； a skilled person who controls an engine or engines | 工程师 | My dad graduated from college as an electrical engineer， but decided he could make more money in real estate. | 我父亲有电机工程学位， 但最后却决定走入房地产领域， 因为他认为可以赚 较多的钱。 |
| 6 | facilitate | /fəˈsiliteit/ | vt. | to make easy or easier | 促进； 使 （更） 容易 | Frances wrapped the towel around the lid of the mayonnaise jar in order to facilitate its opening. | 弗兰西斯用毛巾缠着蛋黄酱瓶的瓶盖， 以便容易将它打开。 |
| 7 | fuel | /fjuəl/ | n. | a material used to produce heat or power by burning； nutritive material； a material from which atomic energy can be liberated， esp. in a reactor | 燃料 | Many scientists fear the continual use of fossil fuel at its current rate will deplete our natural resources. | 许多科学家害怕若持续以现有的消耗量使用化石燃料， 将会很快使我们的自 然资源消耗殆尽。 |
| 8 | generator | /ˈdʒenəreitə/ | n. | a machine which produces something； a machine by which mechanical energy is changed into electrical energy | 产 生器； 发电机 | Many people bought new gasoline-powered generators in preparation for the “Y2K” disaster. | 许多人购买了新的汽油发电机来防备 Y2K 的大灾难。 |
| 9 | innovative | /ˈinəuveitiv/ | adj. | newly invented or introduced； different from and better than the previous ones | 创新的； 革新的 | I thought your presentation at the seminar yesterday was not only exciting but also very innovative. | 我认为你昨日在研讨会上的演说不但精彩， 而且非常有创意。 |
| 10 | inventor | /inˈventə/ | n. | a person who makes or produces a new thing | 发 明家 | As a prospective inventor， Billy loved going into Doctor Grant’s lab to see what contraptions the doctor had been working on. | 作为未来的发明家， 比利喜欢进入葛兰博士的实验室， 去看看他正在研制什 么新奇的装置。 |
| 11 | leading edge | /ˈliːdiŋ edʒ/ | n. | something that is or represents the most advanced or innovative aspect of a field， activity profession， etc. out in front of leading-edge adj. having leading edge | 领先的优势 （2） 居领先地位的 | Microsoft and Bill Gates will continue to hold the leading edge in the computer software industry. | 微软和比尔·盖茨将持续在电脑软件工业占有领先地位。 |
| 12 | malfunction | /ˌmælˈfʌŋkʃən/ | n. | （1） n. a fault in operation vi. to function imperfectly or badly； to fail to operate normally | 故障 （2） 发生故障； 机能 失常 | This new modem has malfunctioned six times since we bought it last month. | 自从上个月我们买下来这个新的调制解调器以后， 它已经发生6 次故障了。 |
| 13 | mode | /məud/ | n. | a particular form or variety of something； a way of behaving， living， operating， etc.； customary usage or current fashion | 样式； 模式； 方式； 风尚； 时尚 | I don’t think this mode of transportation is the fastest or the most cost-effective in getting us to the airport. | 我认为用这种交通工具送我们到机场并不是最快的方式， 也并非最符合经济 效益。 |
| 14 | operate | /ˈɔpəreit/ | vt./vi. | to work or be in action； to cause to function； to carry on trade or business | 操作； 运转； 经营 | I still don’t understand exactly how to operate this version of Windows， but I will give it a try and hope nothing goes wrong. | 我还是不确切地知道如何操作这个版本的视窗， 但我会试一次， 并希望不要 出什么错。 |
| 15 | outlet | /ˈautlet/ | n. | aplaceoropeningthroughwhichsomethingisletout； a store， merchant， oragencysellingthegoodsofaparticularwholesaleror manufacturer； a point on a wiring system at which current is taken to supply electric devices | 出口； 排水口； 销售店； 商行； 插座 | All of the electrical outlets in the house were out because of a blown fuse. | 家里所有的电源插座都因保险丝烧断的原因而不能用了。 |
| 16 | portable | /ˈpɔːtəbl/ | adj. | capable of being carried or moved about | 可携带 的； 轻便的 | I think that portable laptop computers are ingenious and make working so much more convenient. | 我认为手提式电脑是一项天才的发明， 让工作便利许多。 |
| 17 | raw material | /rɔː məˈtiəriəl/ | n. | material before being processed or manufactured into a final form | 原料 | We import raw material and energy， and export industrial products mainly. | 我们进口原材料和能源， 而出口的大部分是工业产品。 |
| 18 | satellite | /ˈsætəlait/ | n. | a body in space that moves around a larger one， esp. a planet； a man-made object rocketed into orbit around the earth， moon， etc. | 卫星； 人造卫星 | Every 24 hours we pass right under the satellite that sits in space around the Earth’s orbit receiving and relaying transmissions. | 每24 个小时我们就会从卫星底下通过， 这个卫星被安置在地球轨道上的太 空中接收并传送讯号。 |
| 19 | specification | /ˌspesifiˈkeiʃən/ | n. | a detailed plan or set of descriptions or directions | 规格 （说明） ； 载明； 详述 | There are detailed specifications as to what type of experience and education you should have to work here. | 关于你必须具备什么样的工作经验和教育背景才能在这儿工作有详细的说明。 |
| 20 | substandard | /ˌsʌbˈstændəd/ | adj. | below the standard； below the norm； not as good as the average | 标准以下的； 不合规格的； 次等的 | The residents in general are poor and undereducated， and live in substandard housing. | 这些居民一般都很穷困、 未受良好教育， 并且住在不合标准的房子里。 |

### Unit 7 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | automaton | /ɔːˈtɔmətən/ | n. | a machine that moves or works by itself， esp. a robot | 自动操作装置； 机器人 | With all the technological advances， it’s a wonder we haven’t developed fully functional automatons like those found in science fiction movies. | 科技如此的进步， 我们不免好奇何以至今仍未能发展出像科幻电影里那样具 有全自动功能的机器人。 |
| 2 | cell （ular） phone | /ˈsel（jələr）ˈfəun/ | n. | a mobile telephone system | 移动 电话； 手机 | The man pulls out his cell phone and calls emergency services. | 这位男士拿出手机打电话给急救中心。 |
| 3 | consecutive | /kənˈsekjutiv/ | adj. | following in regular unbroken order | 连续 的； 连贯的 | The Lakers have won 14 consecutive games， and it doesn’t look like anyone will beat them anytime soon. | 湖人队已连胜14 场， 目前看来似乎还没有人能打败他们。 |
| 4 | devise | /diˈvaiz/ | vt. | to plan or invent， esp. cleverly | 设计； 发明； 策划 | They can’t seem to devise a feasible design for this year’s new cell phone models. | 他们似乎无法为今年新的移动电话机种类发明出合适的设计。 |
| 5 | equipment | /iˈkwipmənt/ | n. | the set of things needed for a particular activity | 装备； 设备 | I don’t know if we have sufficient equipment to effectively resolve the internal combustion problems of your car. | 我不知道我们是否有足够设备可以有效解决你车子的内燃问题。 |
| 6 | facility | /fəˈsiliti/ | n. | （often pl.） something designed， built， installed， etc. to serve a specific function affording a convenience or service | 便利 的设施； 设备 | The country club had fantastic facilities that offered weight training. | 这个乡村俱乐部有非常好的设备提供举重训练。 |
| 7 | gadget | /ˈgædʒit/ | n. | a small mechanical device or useful apparatus | 小巧 的机械装置； 小玩意 | I have been fiddling with this gadget for hours， but I still can’t seem to figure out how it works. | 这个小玩意我已经弄了几个小时了， 但仍未搞懂它是怎么运作的。 |
| 8 | hands-on | /ˈhændzɔn/ | adj. | requiring manual operation， control， adjustment， or the like； characterized by or involved in active personal participation in an activity | 实地操作的； 亲身体验的 | Lots of computer companies require hands-on experience， so plan on taking their three-month training course on matter how much you think you know. | 许多电脑公司都要求实际经验， 因此不管你认为你有多懂电脑， 你还是得准 备接受他们的3 个月训练课程。 |
| 9 | inspect | /inˈspekt/ | vt./vi. | to view closely in critical appraisal | 检查； 审查； 视察 | Jane pulled over to the side of the road， popped the hood， and waited for the smoke to clear before she could inspect the engine. | 珍将车子靠边停下， 把引擎盖打开， 等烟雾散去后再检查引擎。 |
| 10 | laboratory | /ləˈbɔrətri/ | n. | room or building fitted with equipment for scientific experiments | 实验室 | Dr. Frankenstein had been working long and almost maddening hours in the laboratory on his greatest creation. | 弗兰肯斯坦医生已经在实验室里为他最伟大的创造疯狂地工作了很长的一 段时间。 |
| 11 | maintenance | /ˈmeintinəns/ | n. | the act of keeping something in good condition | 维护； 保养 | I’m not that familiar with car internals， but I know basic maintenance skills like changing the oil and replacing a flat fire. | 我并不是那么熟悉车子的内部构造， 但我懂一些像换机油、 换爆胎等的基本 维修技巧。 |
| 12 | manual | /ˈmænjuəl/ | n. | （1） n. a small book giving information about how to do something， esp. how to use a machine （ adj. of or using the hand | 使用） 手册； 指南 （2） 手 （工） 的 | Why don’t you stop fooling with it and read the manual before you end up breaking it？ | 为什么你不停止乱搞， 先读一下使用手册， 免得把东西搞坏了？ |
| 13 | network | /ˈnetwəːk/ | n. | a large system of lines， tubes， wires， etc. that cross one another or are connected with one another； a group of radio or TV stations in different places using many of the same broadcasts； a set of computers that are connected to each other and can be used as a means of sending and sharing information or messages | 网状系统； 广播、 电视网； 电脑网 | If all of the computers are hooked up to a network， we can then play computer games and compete with one another. | 如果所有电脑都连上网， 我们就可以玩电脑游戏， 彼此竞赛。 |
| 14 | outage | /ˈautidʒ/ | n. | an interruption or failure in the supply of power， esp. electricity | 电力中断； 断电 | You had better save your work in case there is a power outage and everything gets lost. | 你最好先存档， 以免断电什么都没了。 |
| 15 | petroleum | /piˈtrəuliəm/ | n. | a mineral oil obtained from below the surface of the earth， and used to produce gasoline and various chemical substances | 石油 | One day all of the Earth’s natural petroleum will be exhausted， and the world will be in dire straits searching for new forms of fuel. | 有一天地球上的天然石油会被用尽， 届时全世界将会因须寻找新能源而陷入 极端的困境。 |
| 16 | procedure | /prəˈsiːdʒə/ | n. | a set of actions necessary for doing something | 程序； 手续 | When installing new software onto the computer， one should always follow the given procedure to avoid potential serious problems. | 在电脑上安装软件时应该始终依照执行程序， 以避免可能引发的严重问题。 |
| 17 | reverse | /riˈvəːs/ | adj. | opposite or contrary to the usual or former， esp. in position or direction | 相反的； 颠倒的 | Please check the items listed on the reverse side. | 请检查列在反面的项目。 |
| 18 | solar | /ˈsəulə/ | adj. | of， from， or having to do with the sun （ | 源自） 太阳 的； 依太阳运行而订的 | Many scientists believe， if harnessed properly， solar energy could be the answer to all energy problems. | 许多科学家相信， 如果适度地利用， 太阳能可以解决所有的能源问题。 |
| 19 | static | /ˈstætik/ | n. | （1） n. atmospheric electrical discharges causing noise on radio or TV adj. not moving， changing or developing； of electricity not flowing in a current， but collecting on the surface of objects | 静电干扰 （2） 静止的； 静态的； 静电的 | Since the lightning storm began， there has been static from almost every major TV station. | 自夹带闪电的暴风雨开始之后， 几乎所有大电视台的节目都有杂讯。 |
| 20 | turn down | /təːn daun/ | v. | to reduce the height or intensity of by turning a control | 关小 （声） | Iwish you would be considerate and turn down the volume on your stereo. | 我希望您能体谅一下， 将收音机声音关小一点。 |

## Unit 8　Computer 电脑

### Unit 8 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | access | /ˈækses/ | n. | permission， liberty， right， or ability to enter， approach， use， or communicate with | 进入； 接近； 使用； 取得 | This site has restricted access； you must be at least 18 years old to view it. | 这个网站是限制级的， 你必须满18 岁才可浏览。 |
| 2 | automatically | /ˌɔːtəˈmætikli/ | adv. | acting or done spontaneously or unconsciously； done or produced as if by machine | 无意识地； 不自觉地； 自动地 | Many modern word processing programs will automatically save what you’re typing periodically to prevent you from accidentally erasing it. | 许多最新的文字处理系统每隔一段时间会将你正在输入的资料自动存档， 以 免你不小心把它们删除。 |
| 3 | cable | /ˈkeibl/ | n. | a thick， heavy rope， often of wire strands； a bundle of insulated wires to carry an electric current | 缆线； 电缆 | Could you please hand me the black cable that attaches the monitor to the hard drive？ | 可以请你将那条连接显示器和硬盘驱动器的黑色电线递给我吗？ |
| 4 | compliance | /kəmˈplaiəns/ | n. | obedience to a rule， agreement， demand， etc.； the tendency to agree willingly to other people’s wishes or demands | 服从； 顺从； 附和； 屈从 | In compliance with copyright laws， we don’t make illegal copies of certain programs and software. | 依照著作权法， 我们不会违法翻印某些程序和软件。 |
| 5 | cutting edge | /ˈkʌtiŋ edʒ/ | n. | （1 ） n. the quality of sharp directness； forefront； lead cutting-edge adj. the most advanced； leading | 尖刻； 尖锐； 先锋地位 （2） 尖端的 | I think that Asia is usually on the cutting edge of new and better technological advancements. | 我认为， 亚洲通常在科技创新改良方面处于领先地位。 |
| 6 | delete | /diˈliːt/ | vt./vi. | to eliminate especially by blotting out， cutting out， or erasing | 删除 | If you find that there is too much space being wasted on your hard drive， you can always delete unnecessary files and programs. | 如果你发现硬盘驱动器中有太多的空间被浪费， 你大可删除一些不必要的文 件或程序。 |
| 7 | digital | /ˈdidʒitl/ | adj. | of or based on a system in which information is represented in the form of changing electrical signals； showing quantity in the form of numbers rather than as point on a scale | 数位的； 数字显 示的 | The new mini-disc players are better than CD players in that they use optical connections instead of digital connections. | 新式的迷你光盘机比CD 机好， 因为它是利用光的连接运作而非数位连接。 |
| 8 | download | /ˈdaunˌləud/ | vt. | to transfer （ software， data etc.） from a distant to a nearby computer， from a larger to a smaller computer， or from a computer to a peripheral device | 下载 | You can now download much of the desired softwate off of the Internet without having to pay a lot. | 你现在不需要付很多钱就可以从网络上下载许多想要的软件。 |
| 9 | hacker | /ˈhækə/ | n. | a computer enthusiast； a person who illegally gains access to and sometimes tampers with information in a computer system | 热衷电脑的人； 企图不法进入或修改别人电脑系统的人； 电脑黑客 | The FBI was flustered when they realized that the suspect they had arrested was not the hacker they were pursuing. | 当联邦调查局发现他们所逮捕的嫌疑犯并非他们原先在追查的电脑黑客时， 显得很狼狈。 |
| 10 | input | /ˈinput/ | n. | （1） n. data fed into a computer or other device vt. to put （information） into a computer | 输入的资 料 （2） 输入电脑 | The computer acts as a word processor where the text of a speech can be input at any time. | 这电脑被用来当作文字处理机， 演讲的内容可以随时被输入。 |
| 11 | Internet | /ˈintənet/ | n. | worldwide network of computers which allows communication and data transfer between people connected to it | 国际互 联网 | Millions of people are using the Internet daily， but in reality I don’t have the slightest idea of how it actually works. | 千百万的人每天在使用因特网， 但事实上我根本不知道它是如何运作的。 |
| 12 | laptop | /ˈlæptɔp/ | n. | a portable computer which is small enough to be held on one’s knees for use | 笔记本电脑 | The new IBM laptop is so small that you can fit it and all of its accessories in your briefcase. | 新款的 IBM 笔记本电脑体积非常小， 你可以把它和所有配备轻易放入你的 公事包里。 |
| 13 | multimedia | /ˌmʌltiˈmiːdjə/ | n. | （1） n. the combined use of several media， as sound and full-motion video in computer applications （2） adj. of or involving multimedia | 多媒体 | In that science class we had to create a multimedia presentation about the molecular structure of a hydrogen bomb. | 在那堂科学课中， 我们必须针对氢弹的分子结构做出一个多媒体的简介。 |
| 14 | retrieval | /riˈtriːvəl/ | n. | an act or process of bringing back | 取回； 恢复 | When a problem this serious occurs with the computer， oftentimes retrieval of lost work is impossible. | 当电脑发生这么严重的问题时， 通常要恢复已经遗失的资料是不可能的。 |
| 15 | search engine | /ˈsəːtʃ ˈendʒin/ | n. | computer software used to search data for specified information | 搜索引擎 | Yahoo is a user-friendly search engine that gives a summary of and link for all the known web pages existing on the Internet. | 雅虎是一种用户友好型的搜索引擎， 它提供网站概要并可链接到所有互联网 上的网站。 |
| 16 | shut down | /ʃʌt daun/ | v. | to （cause to） stop operating （ | 使） 关闭； （使） 停业 | Unauthorized permission may cause a computer to instantly shut down. | 未经授权使用电脑可能会使电脑立即关机。 |
| 17 | state-of-the-art | /ˈsteitəvðiːaːt/ | adj. | using the most modern and recently developed methods， materials， or knowledge | 使用最先进技术 的； 达到最高水准的 | Their computer network system is state-of-the-art and unarguably the best that money can buy. | 他们的电脑网络系统最先进， 毫无疑问， 是钱所能买到的最好的。 |
| 18 | terminology | /ˌtəːmiˈnɔlədʒi/ | n. | the technical or special terms used in a business， art， science， or special subject | 专业术语； 专有名词 | The foreign computer terminology easily confuses the beginning programmer as he attempts to copy a file. | 外国的电脑专业术语很容易让初级程序设计师在试着拷贝档案时感到困惑。 |
| 19 | troubleshooting | /ˈtrʌblʃuːtiŋ/ | adj. | discovering and removing causes of trouble in machines， organizations， etc. | 检修的； 解决困难的 | If you still can’t figure it out by tonight， then I’ll loan you my troubleshooting guide. | 如果你在今晚之前仍束手无策， 我会把我的检修手册借给你。 |
