| 20 | up-to-date | /ˈʌptəˈdeit/ | adj. | extending up to the present time； in accordance with the latest or newest ideas， standards， techniques styles， etc. | 最近的； 最新的 | The United States is one of the countries which are up-to-date in computer technology. | 美国是世界上电脑科技领先的国家之一。 |

### Unit 8 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | activate | /ˈæktiveit/ | vt. | to make able to take action； to bring into use； to cause to happen more quickly | 使活动； 触发； 激活 | Just click on the icon on the screen to activate the anti-virus software. | 只要点击屏幕上的图标， 就能启动防毒软件。 |
| 2 | browse | /brauz/ | vt./vi. | to look over casually | 浏览； 随便翻阅 | I like to get on the Internet and browse different websites that are of interest to me. | 我喜欢上网浏览一些我感兴趣的网站。 |
| 3 | command | /kəˈmaːnd/ | n. | order； direction； the ability to control and use | 命令； 指令； 掌握 | Before Windows came out， people had to type in all of their commands with the keyboard. | 在视窗操作系统出现前， 人们必须以键盘打入所有的指令。 |
| 4 | configuration | /kənˌfigjuˈreiʃən/ | n. | the arrangement of the parts of something； shape | 结构； 构造； 配置 | Changing the configuration of the files will sometimes solve problems with computers. | 更改文件的设置有时候可以解决一些电脑问题。 |
| 5 | database | /ˈdeitəbeis/ | n. | a large collection of data that is stored in a computer system in such a way that it can easily be found by a computer user | 数据库 | They maintain a database of hotels that caters to businesswomen. | 他们保存有一个专门为职业女性服务的旅馆数据库。 |
| 6 | desktop | /ˈdesktɔp/ | adj. | of a size convenient for use on a desk | 桌上型的 | Nowadays laptop computers have become more and more popular， but I still prefer the full desktop computers even though they are much larger and not as mobile. | 虽然现在笔记本电脑越来越受欢迎， 我还是比较喜欢台式电脑， 即使它们比 较大也比较缺乏机动性。 |
| 7 | downgrade | /ˈdaungreid/ | vt. | to give a lower position to or lower level to | 使 降级； 使降职 | Due to severe budget cuts， most of the local high schools had to downgrade many of the computers and high-tech pieces of equipment they were using. | 由于预算遭大幅删减， 本地大部分高中正在使用中的许多电脑和高科技设备 都必须降级。 |
| 8 | floppy （disk） | /ˈflɔpi（disk）/ | n. | a thin， flexible plastic disk coated with magnetic material that is used for storing computer data and programs | 软 磁盘 | The computer won’t start up because there is a floppy disk in the drive， not because of any internal problems. | 这电脑无法启动是因为有一张软盘在驱动器里， 而不是因为任何内部的问题。 |
| 9 | hyperlink | /ˈhaipəlink/ | n. | an electronic link providing direct access from one distinctively marked place in a hypertext or hypermedia document to another in the same or a different document | 超链接 | My web page has a section with hyperlinks to all of my friends’ homepages and my favorite web sites. | 我的个人网页有一个部分可以超链接到所有朋友的网页和我最喜欢的网 站上。 |
| 10 | install | /inˈstɔːl/ | vt. | to set up for use | 安装； 安置 | Trying to install a new piece of hardware can be difficult and frustrating if one does not follow the instructions. | 如果你不遵循说明书上的指示， 安装一个新的电脑硬件可能会很困难而且令 人沮丧。 |
| 11 | know-how | /ˈnəuˈhau/ | n. | practical skill； professional knowledge； expertise | 技能； 专业知识； 窍门 | He hasn’t gotten the know-how to run a farm. | 他还没学会如何管理一座农场。 |
| 12 | modem | /ˈməudəm/ | n. | device which allows the transfer of data from one computer to another through a telephone line | 调制解调器 | He sent his work to his publishers by modem. | 他将他的作品用调制解调器传送到他的出版商那里。 |
| 13 | omit | /əuˈmit/ | vt. | to leave out or leave unmentioned； to fail to do something | 省略； 删除； 疏忽 | A few members of the committee wanted to omit the clause on paid lunches and vacations. | 委员会里有几个委员想删除关于支付公务餐费和假期的条款。 |
| 14 | scanner | /ˈskænə/ | n. | a device for sensing recorded data | 扫描仪 | I could do the entire web page myself if I had a scanner to put the pictures and graphics up with. | 如果有扫描仪可以让我将照片和图片扫描进去， 我就能独立完成所有的网页 制作。 |
| 15 | server | /ˈsəːvə/ | n. | a computer in a network that is used to provide services （as access to files or shared peripherals or the routing of Email） to other computers in the network | 服务器 | Trying to see the test results was an arduous task due to the slow server. | 要想查询考试结果相当费力， 因为服务器太慢了。 |
| 16 | spreadsheet | /ˈspredʃiːt/ | n. | an accounting program for a computer， the ledger layout modeled by such a program | 电子制表软件； 电子表格 | Spreadsheets are useful applications when creating schedules， calendars， or lists. | 用电子制表软件来制作行程表、 月历或名单很方便。 |
| 17 | switch | /switʃ/ | n. | an apparatus for shopping or starting the flow of an electric current； a complete， esp. unexpected， change | 开关； 骤变 | The On / Off switch is the large red one on the back of the hard drive. | 开关是机箱后面那个红色的大按钮。 |
| 18 | transmission | /trænzˈmiʃən/ | n. | an act， process， or instance of sending out electric signals， messages， news， etc. | 传输； 传送 | Normally such large transmissions are communicated through satellite relay to location around the world. | 通常这种大型信息传输都是经由卫星转送到世界各地。 |
| 19 | upgrade | /ˈʌpgreid/ | vt. | to promote to a higher position or rank； to improve or enhance the quality or value of | 升级； 提升 | If it weren’t so expensive， I would upgrade my computer to the fastest processor. | 要不是价钱太高， 我会将我的电脑升级， 使用最快速的处理器。 |
| 20 | virus | /ˈvaiərəs/ | n. | an ultramicroscopic agent that causes infectious disease； a segment of self-replicating code planted illegally in a computer program， often to damage or shut down a system or network | 滤过性病毒； 电脑病毒 | I wish that the Y2K virus hadn’t caused such a big panic toward the end of 1999. | 我真希望 Y2K 病毒1999 年底时并未造成那样大的恐慌。 |

## Unit 9　Business Correspondence & Advertisement 商业书信

### Unit 9 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | advertisement | /ˌædvərˈtaizmənt； ədˈvəːtismənt/ | n. | a public notice， usually paid for | 广告 | They put our billboard advertisement too far away from the highway. | 他们把我们的广告牌放得离公路太远。 |
| 2 | appendix | /əˈpendiks/ | n. | additional material at the end of a book | 附录 | If you need more information， you can look at the appendix. | 如果你需要更多资料， 可以看附录。 |
| 3 | attach | /əˈtætʃ/ | vt./vi. | to fasten in position； to fix or affix； to join or connect | 贴上； 附上； 附加 | I need to attach a file to that e-mail before you send it on to the corporate office. | 在你把电子邮件寄给公司前， 我要在信上附加一份档案。 |
| 4 | campaign | /kæmˈpein/ | n. | a series of military operations with a particular objective； a series of planned actions， as for electing a candidate； a series of connected actions intended to obtain a particular result， esp. in politics or business | 战役； 竞选活动； 宣传活动 | Following the advertising campaign， sales shot up fifty percent. | 广告促销活动之后， 营业额迅速提升了50% 。 |
| 5 | cohesive | /kəuˈhiːsiv/ | adj. | holding fast； sticking together； well-integrated | 有黏着力的； 有结合力的； 凝聚性的 | The more cohesive our team is， the easier it will be for us to come up with new and better ideas. | 我们团队的凝聚力越大， 我们也就能越容易想出更好更新的点子。 |
| 6 | confidential | /ˌkɔnfiˈdenʃəl/ | adj. | spoken or written in secret and intended to be kept secret | 秘密的； 机密的 | We will keep what you’ve told us strictly confidential. | 我们会将你告诉我们的事视为高级机密。 |
| 7 | correspondence | /ˌkɔrisˈpɔndəns/ | n. | communication by letters； the letters exchanged between people | 通信； 信件 | Maintaining weekly correspondence with your customers is a surefire way of keeping them satisfied. | 每周与客户通信保持联系是维持客户满意度的绝对保障。 |
| 8 | dictate | /dikˈteit/ | vt./vi. | to say （words） for someone else to write down； to command forcefully； to give authority （orders） with | 口述； 支配； 命令 | Because I am only a supervisor， not the boss， I can’t dictate how you go about doing every little thing. | 我只是主管， 不是老板， 所以我无法告诉你如何去做每件小事。 |
| 9 | elaborate | /iˈlæbərit/ | vt. | （1） vt. /vi. to add more detail or information adj. full of detail； carefully worked out | 详尽说 明 （2） 详尽的； 精心制作的 | I must prefer simplicity to such unnecessarily elaborate design. | 我比较喜欢简单大方而非如此不必要的复杂设计。 |
| 10 | forward | /ˈfɔːwəd/ | vt. | to send forward or pass on | 转寄； 发送 | I’m afraid you have the wrong address， but if you like， I can just forward these letters to the appropriate address for you. | 你恐怕弄错地址了， 如果你愿意， 我倒可以帮你将这些信转寄到适当的地址。 |
| 11 | inquire | /inˈkwaiə/ | vt./vi. | to ask for information； to make investigation | 询问； 调查 | If you would like to talk to someone in the PR department， please inquire at the information desk on the tenth floor. | 如果你想跟公关部门的人谈话的话， 请询问十楼的服务台。 |
| 12 | jot down | /ˈdʒɔtˌdaun/ | v. | to make a brief note of； to write down hastily | 约略记下； 匆匆记下 | Afraid of missing any important information， the new trainee found it necessary to jot down what he heard. | 因为害怕错失任何重要资讯， 新的练习生觉得有必要赶快记下所听到的话。 |
| 13 | leaflet | /ˈliːflit/ | n. | a ususlly folded printed sheet intended for free distribution | 传单 | In an effort to promote the upcoming anniversary issue of our Bikini World magazine， we are going to distribute a small leaflet with sample pictures and articles. | 为了促销即将发行的 《比基尼世界》 杂志周年特辑， 我们打算发送一份附 有照片样本和文章的小传单。 |
| 14 | notify | /ˈnəutifai/ | vt. | to tell （someone）， esp. formally； to inform | 通知； 告知 | They have notified the police of their loss. | 他们已把损失情况告知警方。 |
| 15 | promote | /prəˈməut/ | vt. | to help further the growth of； to advance in rank or position | 发扬； 晋升； 促进 | We need to employ new methods to promote our new product because it’s not selling as well as we expected. | 我们需要用新的方法来促销我们的新产品， 因为它卖得没有我们预期中的好。 |
| 16 | publicity | /pʌbˈlisiti/ | n. | any information or action that brings a person， cause， etc. to the notice of people in general | 公开； 宣传； 尽人皆知 | The Simpson case generated enormous publicity in the United States a few years ago. | 辛普森案件几年前在美国几乎尽人皆知。 |
| 17 | response | /risˈpɔns/ | n. | something said or done in answer； a reply | 回答； 回应 | The public’s response to the new commercial has been very positive. | 大众对新广告的反响非常好。 |
| 18 | revise | /riˈvaiz/ | vt./vi. | to make a new， improved， or up-to-date version of | 修订； 修改； 校订 | I want you to revise this proposal and see if you can make it a little more financially beneficial to our company. | 我希望你能修改这份企划书， 并且看是否能把它变得在财务上对我们公司 更有利些。 |
| 19 | slogan | /ˈsləugən/ | n. | a brief attention-getting phrase used in advertising or promotion | 口号； 标语 | “Just Do It” has been a great slogan for NIKE for many years， but I think it’s time for a change. “ | 放手去做” 已成为多年来耐克的最佳标语， 但我认为也该是有所创新的时 候了。 |
| 20 | urgent | /ˈəːdʒənt/ | adj. | very important and needing to be dealt with quickly or first | 紧急的； 急迫的 | You will just have to wait until the meeting is over unless this is an urgent call. | 除非这是紧急电话， 否则你只好等到会议结束。 |

### Unit 9 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | affirm | /əˈfəːm/ | vt./vi. | to state or declare positively； to assert； to confirm | 坚称； 申明； 断言； 确认 | He nodded， affirming his plans to become a farmer instead of a doctor. | 他点头确认他将成为农民而非医生的计划。 |
| 2 | as soon as possible |  |  |  | 越快越好 | NIKE was angry with Malaysia for not completing an urgent order and demanded them to finish it ASAP. | 耐克对于马来西亚厂未能即时赶完一批急货而生气， 并要求他们尽快完成。 |
| 3 | banner | /ˈbænə/ | n. | a strip of cloth on which a sign is painted； a flag | 标语； 旗帜 | We have everything ready for the party except the banner that says “Happy Birthday”. | 我们把所有派对要用的东西都准备齐全了， 只差印有 “生日快乐” 的布条。 |
| 4 | circular | /ˈsəːkjulə/ | n. | a printed advertisement， paper， or notice intended for distribution | 广告； 传单； 通告 | The proposal has been widely publicized on BBC， and the press information circulars sent to 1，800 newspapers. | 这份企划案已经在 BBC 上大肆宣传， 广告传单也已送到1800 家报社。 |
| 5 | composition | /ˌkɔmpəˈziʃən/ | n. | the act or process of forming something to become a whole； a piece of music or a short essay | 写作； 作品； 作文； 作曲 | I am getting very anxious about the composition that is due tomorrow which I have not started writing yet. | 我对明天要交的作文感到无比紧张， 我甚至还没开始下笔写呢！ |
| 6 | convene | /kənˈviːn/ | vt./vi. | to come together， to call to meet； to assemble for a meeting | 集合； 聚集； 开会 | We will all convene in the lobby of the hotel before we drive to see the Great Wall of China. | 开车去参观万里长城之前， 我们将在旅馆大厅集合。 |
| 7 | courier | /ˈkuriə/ | n. | a person or company that is employed to carry messages or other official paper， esp. of an urgent kind | 递送急件的信 差； 邮务专递公司 | Punctuality and speed are two of the most important requirements a courier must have to successfully complete his job. | 准时和效率是快递员要成功做好他的工作最重要的两项必备条件。 |
| 8 | draft | /draːft/ | n. | an early version of a letter， book， or speech； a written order for payment of money by a bank | 草稿； 汇票 | I have yet to see how well the finished product turns out， but your initial draft was very appealing. | 我还未看到你的成品结果如何， 但你的初稿非常吸引人。 |
| 9 | enclose | /inˈkləuz/ | vt. | to include along with something else in a parcel or envelope | 装入； 放入封套 | Please don’t forget to enclose the videotape in the package along with the contracts and business proposals. | 请不要忘了把录影带连同合约和企划书一起装入同一个包裹里。 |
| 10 | inform | /inˈfɔːm/ | vt./vi. | to give knowledge of something to | 通知； 告知 | Immediately inform local officials if there is an emergency. | 如果有紧急情况， 请立刻通知当地官员。 |
| 11 | issue | /ˈisjuː/ | vt./vi. | to go， come， or flow out； to put forth or distribute officially | 流出； 放出； 发行； 发布 | Having the proper identification and documents will allow the government to promptly issue your passport and visa. | 将身份证明和文件准备好会使政府单位快速发给你护照和签证。 |
| 12 | layout | /ˈleiˌaut/ | n. | the plan or design or arrangement of something | 规 划； 设计； 编排 | The plan detailed business presentations requires first creating a general layout of the proposed idea. | 要计划详尽的商业报告， 需要先对建议内容有概括的规划。 |
| 13 | legible | /ˈledʒəbl/ | adj. | （of handwriting or print） that can be read， esp. easily （ | 字迹） 可以辨认的； 易读的 | Since parts of your resume wasn’t very legible， I had trouble understanding what it said. | 因为你的履历表有一些部分不是很清楚， 所以我无法确切了解你写了什么。 |
| 14 | postage | /ˈpəustidʒ/ | n. | the money charged for mailing a letter， carrying a parcel， etc.， esp. as represented by stamps | 邮资 | Please make sure that you have attached enough postage on the package. | 请确认你在包裹上贴了足够的邮资。 |
| 15 | proofread | /ˈpruːfriːd/ | vt./vi. | to read and mark corrections on | 校正； 校对 | Before you hand in your next paper， please proofread it first. | 在你交下一份报告之前， 请先校对一下。 |
| 16 | regarding | /riˈgaːdiŋ/ | prep. | on the subject of； in connection with； concerning about | 关于； 有关 | We are just checking to see if you have received the letter regarding your current financial status. | 我们只是确认一下你是否收到了有关你最近财务状况的信函。 |
| 17 | restate | /riːˈsteit/ | vt. | to state again or in a different or new way | 再声明； 重新叙述； （换一种方式） 重说 | If you would kindly restate the question， we can continue with the trial. | 如果你愿意重新陈述这个问题， 我们可以继续这项审判。 |
| 18 | signature | /ˈsignitʃə/ | n. | a person’s name written by himself/herself； the act of writing one’s own name | 签名； 签字 | We need your signature here on this dotted line. | 我们需要你在这虚线上签名。 |
| 19 | stationery | /ˈsteiʃənəri/ | n. | writing materials， as pens； pencils； paper， and envelopes | 文具； 信纸； 信笺 | The small craft store， adjacent to the library， sells exceptional stationery. | 临近图书馆的小型手工艺店卖非常特别的文具用品。 |
| 20 | write-up | /ˈraitʌp/ | n. | a written report write up / ˈraitˈʌp/ v. to write a report on， esp. giving a judgment | 报告书 （2） 详细写 | You are always late for work； I am afraid I have to do a write-up about it to your supervisor. | 你上班总是迟到， 我恐怕要写一份报告书呈交给你的主管。 |

## Unit 10　Travel 旅游

### Unit 10 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | accommodation | /əˌkɔməˈdeiʃən/ | n. | adjustment； a place to live or work in； （pl. ） lodging， food and services | 适应； 住所； 膳宿 | We welcome you to Chicago and hope that you enjoy your stay and the accommodations we have prepared for you. | 我们欢迎您到芝加哥， 希望您停留期间愉快， 并对我们所提供给您的住宿 服务感到满意。 |
| 2 | boarding pass | /ˈbɔːdiŋˌpaːs/ | n. | a ticket one receives after checking in for an airplane flight | 登机牌 | They won’t let you get on the airplane without a boarding pass. | 如果没有登机牌， 他们不会让你上飞机。 |
| 3 | brochure | /ˈbrəuʃər/ | n. | a small thin book giving instructions or details of a service； pamphlet； leaflet | 小册子 | Free brochures about popular scenic spots can be found in the periodical section of any public library. | 在任何公立图书馆的杂志部门都可以找到一些免费的小册子， 介绍一些受欢 迎的旅游景点。 |
| 4 | checkout | /ˈtʃekaut/ | n. | the time at which a guest must leave a hotel room check out / ˈtʃekˈaut/ v. to leave a hotel after paying the bill | 结账退房的时间 （2） 办理退房； 结账离开 | Why don’t you run downstairs and check out while I pack， so that we can get out of here as fast as possible？ | 你何不在我打包行李时下楼去退房， 这样我们就可以尽早离开？ |
| 5 | confirmation | /ˌkɔnfəˈmeiʃən/ | n. | formal approval | 确认 | They should have given you a ticket confirmation code when you purchased your tickets. | 他们应该在你买机票时就给你一个机位确认号码。 |
| 6 | customs | /ˈkʌstəmz/ | n. | a place where travelers’ belongings are searched when leaving or entering a country； taxes paid on goods entering or leaving a country | 海关； 关税 | Keep in mind that everything you purchase abroad has to be declared at customs when you come back. | 记得你在国外所买的所有东西回国时都必须报关。 |
| 7 | departure | /diˈpaːtʃə/ | n. | an act of leaving or going away | 离开； 出发 | What is the departure time of the flight to San Francisco？ | 飞往旧金山的班机何时起飞？ |
| 8 | en route | /ˌenˈruːt/ | adv. | on the way | 在途中 | We ran into quite a few problems en route to the wedding， but we made it nonetheless. | 我们去参加婚礼的路上碰到很多问题， 不过虽然如此， 我们最后还是到 达了。 |
| 9 | expedition | /ˌekspiˈdiʃən/ | n. | （the people， vehicles， etc. going on） a （long） journey or voyage for a particular purpose | 探险； 远征队 | It took four months for us to prepare for the expedition to the South Pole. | 我们花了4 个月为南极探险作准备。 |
| 10 | flight | /flait/ | n. | （an act of） flying； an airplane carrying passengers on a particular journey； （the distance covered in） a journey through air | 飞 行； 班机； 航程 | My departure flight to Los Angeles leaves next Friday at 8∶ 30 in the morning. | 我到洛杉矶的班机将在下个星期五早上8∶ 30 起飞。 |
| 11 | itinerary | /aiˈtinərəri/ | n. | a travel plan； a travel route | 行程表； 旅程 | I seem to have misplaced my itinerary； could you please fax me a copy so I can see when I need to be at the airport？ | 我好像丢了我的旅程表， 可以请你再传真一份给我， 让我知道何时该到机 场吗？ |
| 12 | lodge | /lɔdʒ/ | n. | （1） n. a small house； a resort hotel or motel vi. to live in a place for a time； to live as a paying guest | 小屋； 旅馆 （2） 住宿； 投宿 | If you want to travel in the country， you’d better make sure first you can find a place to lodge at night. | 如果你要到乡下旅行， 最好先确定可以找到住宿过夜的地方。 |
| 13 | luxurious | /lʌgˈzjuəriəs/ | adj. | very fine and expensive； providing the greatest comfort | 奢华的； 非常舒适的 | When I went on my business trip， my company paid for my room in the most luxurious five-star hotel. | 当我出差时， 公司付钱让我住最豪华的五星级旅馆。 |
| 14 | motel | /məuˈtel/ | n. | a hotel for motorists | 汽车旅馆 | There are a lot of roadside motels in America. | 在美国有很多路边的汽车旅馆。 |
| 15 | packing | /ˈpækiŋ/ | n. | the act of putting things in cases or boxes | 包装； 打 包 | Because I can only take one suitcase to Beijing， it makes packing for this trip very difficult. | 因为我只能带一个皮箱到北京， 这让打包变得非常困难。 |
| 16 | peak season | /ˈpiːkˌsiːzn/ | n. | season when there is most demand for something or most use of something | 旺季 | If you buy a plane ticket during the peak season， it’s usually a lot more expensive. | 在旺季时买飞机票， 通常会贵很多。 |
| 17 | reservation | /ˌrezəˈveiʃən/ | n. | an arrangement made in advance to have a place in a hotel， restaurant， or on a plane | 预约； 预订； 保留 | We have made reservations at that new Italian restaurant for 7∶ 30. | 我们预订了那家新意大利餐厅今晚7∶ 30 的位子。 |
| 18 | sightseeing | /ˈsaitsiːiŋ/ | n. | the visiting of famous or interesting places | 观 光； 游览 | Your trip to Paris will be very boring if you have no time for sightseeing. | 如果你没有时间观光， 你的巴黎之行将会无聊透顶。 |
| 19 | suite | /swiːt/ | n. | a set of rooms （in a hotel） （ | 旅馆的） 套房 | As compensation for the inconvenience， they have booked you in the Presidential Suite， which is the best room in the hotel. | 为了补偿他们所造成的不便， 他们替你订了总统套房， 那是整个旅馆最棒的 一间房间。 |
| 20 | valid | /ˈvælid/ | adj. | that can legally be used for a stated period or in certain condition | 依法有效的； 有效的 | I don’t think that these free car rental vouchers are still valid， but you can try and see. | 我不认为这些免费的租车凭单依然有效， 但是你可以试试看。 |

### Unit 10 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | baggage claim | /ˈbægidʒˌkleim/ | n. | an area where passengers collect their suitcase and luggage after an airline flight | 行李提领处 | This airport has its arrival gate and baggage claim in two separate buildings. | 这个机场的入境门和行李提领处是在两栋不同的大楼。 |
| 2 | breathtaking | /ˈbreθˌteikiŋ/ | adj. | very exciting； very surprising or shocking； astonishingly beautiful | 惊人的； 惊险的； 惊艳的 | The aerial view from the plane of the city at night is truly breathtaking. | 从高空看这个城市的夜景实在是美不胜收。 |
| 3 | carry-on | /ˈkæriˈɔn/ | n. | a small piece of luggage that is allowed inside the cabin of a passenger airplane | 登机行李； 手提行李 | Individual airlines have the right to regulate the size of a carry-on luggage as they deem necessary. | 各家航空公司有权按照需要规定旅客登机行李的大小。 |
| 4 | concierge | /ˌkɔːnsiˈɛəʒ/ | n. | a caretaker or head porter， as of an apartment house or hotel | 管理员； 门房 | A friendly and courteous concierge can represent a hotel’s quality. | 一个和善、 有礼的门房能代表一家旅馆的品质。 |
| 5 | cruise | /kruːz/ | n. | a sea voyage for pleasure | 乘船游览 | We can go on the cruise and see the entire island. | 我们可以搭船游览全岛。 |
| 6 | deluxe | /diˈlʌks/ | adj. | especially elegant or luxurious | 豪华的； 高级的 | This is a bottle of rare， highly prized deluxe wine. | 这是一瓶很罕见、 非常珍贵的高级酒。 |
| 7 | destination | /ˌdestiˈneiʃən/ | n. | a place to which one is going | 目的地 | If your final destination is Chicago， your connecting flight is at gate A5. | 如果你的目的地是芝加哥， 你转接的班机在 A5 号登机门。 |
| 8 | exotic | /igˈzɔtik/ | adj. | strangely beautiful， enticing， ect.； foreign | 异国风 情的； 异国的 | I want to go somewhere exotic like Egypt or Tahiti for our honeymoon！ | 我想要到像埃及或大溪地那种充满异国风情的地方去度蜜月！ |
| 9 | fasten | /ˈfaːsn/ | vt./vi. | to make or become firmly fixed or closed； to make secure | 系紧； 拴住； 扣住 | We are supposed to fasten our seatbelts before take off. | 我们应该在起飞前系紧我们的安全带。 |
| 10 | flight attendant | /ˈflait əˈtendənt/ | n. | a person who waits on passengers and sees to their comfort and safety on an airplane | 空中服务员 | If you are really uncomfortable， tell the flight attendant ； maybe she will help you find another seat. | 如果你真的很不舒服， 就告诉空中服务员， 也许她能帮你另外找一个位子。 |
| 11 | jet lag | /ˈdʒetˌlæg/ | n. | feeling of exhaustion and disorientation caused by traveling between time zones | 时差 | The trip from the United States in South Africa is always the hardest for me because of the time in the air and the jet lag. | 从美国到南非的航程对我来说总是最困难的， 因为飞行时间太长， 并且我会 有时差的问题。 |
| 12 | luggage | /ˈlʌgidʒ/ | n. | the cases， bags， boxes， etc. of a traveler； baggage | 行李 | The airlines will directly transfer your luggage to connecting flights so that all you have to do is pick it up when you get to your final destination. | 航空公司会直接把你的行李转到连接的航班上， 你只要到目的地以后再去领 取就行了。 |
| 13 | metal detector | /ˈmetl diˈtektə/ | n. | device that detects the presence of metal | 金属探测器 | You had better be careful that your pistol-shaped cigarette lighter doesn’t trigger the metal detector and get everyone nervous. | 你最好小心一点， 别让你的手枪型打火机触发金属探测器而使每个人大为 紧张。 |
| 14 | overseas | /ˌəuvəˈsiːz/ | adv. | （1） adv. beyond the sea； abroad adj. to， at， or in somewhere across the sea； foreign | 在 （或向） 海 外； 在 （或向） 国外 （2） 海外的； 国外的 | It looks like my company is going to send me overseas for the next two or three months. | 看来我的公司将在往后的两三个月派我到国外工作。 |
| 15 | passenger | /ˈpæsindʒə/ | n. | one who travels by some form of conveyance | 乘客； 旅客 | Each passenger， according to FAA （Federal Aviation Administration） regulations， must have a flotation device and oxygen mask in the case of an emergency. | 根据联邦航空局的法规规定， 紧急情况发生时， 每个乘客都必须有一个漂浮 装置和氧气罩。 |
| 16 | porter | /ˈpɔːtə/ | n. | a person employed to carry travelers’ bags at railway stations， airports， ect，； a person in charge of the entrance to a hotel， school， ect. | 搬运工； 门房 | It is common courtesy to tip the porter who carries your luggage for you. | 给帮你搬运行李的门房小费是很普通的礼貌。 |
| 17 | round-trip | /ˈraundˌtrip/ | adj. | （of a ticket or its cost） for a journey to a place and back again | 来回的， 双程的 | I have found that buying a round-trip ticket is usually cheaper than buying two separate one-way tickets. | 我发现， 买一张往返票通常比买两张单程票要便宜。 |
| 18 | stopover | /ˈstɔpəuvə/ | n. | a short stay between parts of a journey | 中途停 留 | I originally thought it was a direct flight， but it appears that we will have a stopover in Chicago before we get to Boston. | 我本来以为这是直飞的班机， 但是看来我们到波士顿前会中途在芝加哥停 留一下。 |
| 19 | vacant | /ˈveikənt/ | adj. | not held， occupied as a seat， house， etc. | 空 的； 未被占用的 | Go to the front desk and see if any of the rooms facing the mountains are vacant. | 去柜台看看他们是否有面山的房间是空着的。 |
| 20 | voyage | /ˈvɔiidʒ/ | n. | a journey， usually long， made by boat or ship； a journey by aircraft or spacecraft | 航行； 旅行 | I have always dreamed of taking a long voyage on a ship to other countries. | 我一直梦想可以乘船到其他国家游玩。 |

## Unit 11　Transportation （Air， Ocean， Land） 交通运输

### Unit 11 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | accelerate | /ækˈseləreit/ | vt./vi. | to cause to move or happen faster | 加速； 促进； 前进 | When merging onto the freeway， it is wise to gradually accelerate until you are at the same speed as the lane of traffic. | 进入高速公路时最好逐渐加速， 直到你的车速和其他同一线道的车子速度一 样为止。 |
| 2 | aircraft | /ˈɛəkraːft/ | n. | a flying machine of any type， with or without an engine | 飞机； 飞行器； 航空器 | Because the aircraft is running into some turbulence， the flight attendant asks everyone to remain seated and fasten their seat belts. | 因为飞机碰到了乱流， 所以空中服务员请所有旅客在座位上坐好并系上安全带。 |
| 3 | arrival | /əˈraivəl/ | n. | （the act of ） reaching a place at the end of a journey | 到达； 抵达 | Could you wait for me at the arrival gate when I get off the plane？ | 我下飞机的时候， 你可不可以在入境门等我？ |
| 4 | buckle up | /ˈbʌkl ʌp/ | v. | to fasten a safety belt | 系安全带 | I would appreciate it if you would buckle up every time you got in my car without me having to remind you. | 如果你能在每次上我的车时就系上安全带而不用我每次都得提醒你， 我会很 感激的。 |
| 5 | cargo | /ˈkaːgəu/ | n. | the load carried by a ship， truck， plane， etc.； freight | 货物 | It took us a long time to load the plane because there was some cargo from the Red Cross that had to be carried on this flight too. | 装载货物花了我们很多时间， 因为有一些红十字会的货物也必须用这班飞机 运送。 |
| 6 | convertible | /kənˈvəːtəbl/ | n. | an automobile with a top that can be folded back | 敞篷车 | I think convertibles look cool， but I don’t like riding in them because the wind messes up my hair. | 我认为敞篷车看来很酷， 但是我不喜欢坐， 因为风会把我的头发吹乱。 |
| 7 | crash landing | /kræʃ ˈlændiŋ/ | n. | forced landing | 迫降 | The pilot was highly commended for having handled the crash landing successfully. | 这个机长因成功地使飞机迫降而得到高度赞赏。 |
| 8 | delay | /diˈlei/ | n. | an instance of being caused to be late； the state of being late | 耽搁； 延迟 | The delay of our flight made us miss our business meetings. | 飞机的延误害我们错过了商业会议。 |
| 9 | forbid | /fəˈbid/ | vt. | to order （a person） not to do （something）； to prohibit | 禁止 | The law forbids you to bring any plants or animals into this country. | 依法规定， 不得携带任何动植物进入本国。 |
| 10 | ground crew | /graund kruː/ | n. | the team of people at an airport who maintain aircraft between flights | 地勤人员 | The ground crew were having a terrible time trying to deice the airplane so it could take off. | 地勤人员很努力地想把飞机上的结冰弄掉， 以便让它可以升空。 |
| 11 | jaywalk | /ˈdʒeiwɔːk/ | vi. | to walk across a street without obeying traffic rules and signals | 不遵守交通规则穿越马路； 闯红灯 | Since there is no crosswalk by the shopping center， many people simply jaywalk to get to it. | 由于购物商场那边没有行人穿越道， 所以很多人干脆直接就穿越马路。 |
| 12 | launch | /lɔːntʃ/ | vt./vi. | to send into the sky or space； to begin an activity， plan， etc. | 发射； 开始 | Russia threatened to launch nuclear missiles at Canada. | 俄罗斯威胁要对加拿大发射核弹。 |
| 13 | navigate | /ˈnævigeit/ | vt./vi. | to direct the course of a ship， plane， etc. | 航行； 驾驶 | Are you sure you know how to navigate this boat？ | 你确定你知道怎样驾驶这艘船吗？ |
| 14 | pilot | /ˈpailət/ | n. | one who flies an aircraft | 飞行员 | Despite the weather， our pilot made a smooth landing in Tokyo. | 虽然天气恶劣， 我们的飞行员还是顺利降落东京。 |
| 15 | route | /ruːt/ | n. | chosen direction or line of travel | 路径； 路线 | Obviously this taxi driver is not taking the fastest route. | 显然， 这个出租车司机并没有走捷径。 |
| 16 | seat belt | /ˈsiːtˌbelt/ | n. | anchored straps that buckle across the hips to protect a seated passenger from an abrupt jolt | 安全带 | According to the law， everyone in the front seat of a moving vehicle must have their seat belts fastened at all times. | 法律规定， 车辆行驶中每个坐在前座的人都应该随时系好安全带。 |
| 17 | subway | /ˈsʌbwei/ | n. | underground railway | 地铁 | In preparation for the oncoming Olympic Games， the city has begun constructing a new subway system. | 为了准备即将来临的奥运， 该市已经开始着手兴建一条新的地铁线路。 |
| 18 | taxicab | /ˈtæksiˌkæb/ | n. | an automobile which transports passengers for a fee | 出租车 | The taxicab drivers in Amsterdam are said to be the most courteous in Europe and they never overcharge. | 阿姆斯特丹的出租车司机据说是全欧洲最有礼貌的， 而且他们不会漫天 要价。 |
| 19 | transit | /ˈtrænsit/ | n. | the moving or going of people or goods from one place to another | 运输； 通过 | A new transit system can certainly solve the traffic problems in the city. | 一个新的运输系统一定能解决市内的交通问题。 |
| 20 | vehicle | /ˈviːikl/ | n. | any device for carrying or conveying persons or objects | 交通工具； 车辆 | I like all the vehicles I own， but this red Ford truck is my favorite. | 我喜欢我拥有的全部车子， 但是这辆红色福特卡车是我的最爱。 |

### Unit 11 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | acrophobia | /ˌækrəuˈfəubjə/ | n. | an abnormal fear of being in high places | 恐高症 | When I was a child， I had the worst acrophobia and I would be absolutely petrified when flying or riding on roller coasters. | 我小时候有很严重的恐高症， 在飞行时或坐过山车时我都会吓得手脚发软。 |
| 2 | airliner | /ˈɛəlainə/ | n. | a large passenger airplane | 大型客机 | The airliners that carry passengers overseas are larger than the ones that just fly within the continental United States. | 飞国际线的客机比只飞美国大陆境内的客机要大些。 |
| 3 | aviation | /ˌeiviˈeiʃən/ | n. | the practice or science of flying in aircraft | 飞行； 航空学 | Aviation experts say that a seagull caught in the engine caused the plane crash. | 航空专家指出： 因为一只海鸥被卡在引擎里面， 所以才造成飞机失事。 |
| 4 | cabin | /ˈkæbin/ | n. | a small roughly built house； a room on a ship； the space for passengers， crew， or cargo in an aircraft | 小屋； 客舱； 机舱 | Special instruments and advanced technology maintain constant pressure in the cabin of the plane and prevent the passengers from getting sick. | 特殊仪器和高科技使机舱内能维持恒压， 避免旅客感到不舒服。 |
| 5 | commuter | /kəˈmjuːtə/ | n. | one who travels regularly between two points， e. g. between home and work | 通勤者 | The subway takes all the commuters from Brooklyn to their jobs in downtown New York City. | 地铁把所有通勤者从布鲁克林送到纽约市中心工作。 |
| 6 | conveyance | /kənˈveiəns/ | n. | the act of carrying from one place to another； a means of transportation | 运输； 交通工具 | The conveyance of these large crates will require some trucks or possibly a freight liner. | 要运送这些大型板条箱可能需要几辆卡车甚至一艘货轮。 |
| 7 | crossroads | /ˈkrɔsrəudz/ | n. | the place where roads intersect； intersection | 十字路口 | Please turn right at the first crossroads. | 请在第一个十字路口右转。 |
| 8 | ferry | /ˈferi/ | n. | a boat that goes across a river or any other stretch of water， carrying people and things | 渡轮 | The last ferry across the river leaves in twenty minutes， so we had better hurry. | 最后一班过河的渡轮将在20 分钟内开船， 我们最好快一点。 |
| 9 | freight | /freit/ | n. | goods carried by ship， train plane， etc.； cargo | 货运； 货物 | I have always been interested to know how boats remain afloat despite the heavy freight they carry. | 我一直很想知道船只在载运了那么重的货物后为什么还能浮在水面上。 |
| 10 | intersection | /ˌintə（ː） ˈsekʃən/ | n. | a point where roads ， lines， etc. cross | 十字路口； 交叉点 | Be careful when you cross that intersection by the bank because the traffic lights are not working and cars aren’t stopping for pedestrians. | 你通过银行旁边的十字路口时要小心， 因为交通信号灯坏了， 而车辆根本就 不顾行人死活。 |
| 11 | jeopardy | /ˈdʒepədi/ | n. | risk of loss， defeat， harm， etc.； danger； peril | 风险； 危险； 危难 | If the pilots are not careful， they could easily place our lives in jeopardy. | 如果飞行员不小心谨慎的话， 很容易就会使我们身处险境。 |
| 12 | life vest | /ˈlaifˌvest/ | n. | a sleeveless vest which keeps you afloat in water | 救生衣 | No passenger could ever have imagined the luxurious and plush cruise ship， the Titanic， did not carry enough life vests and boats to save all of its passengers. | 没有乘客敢相信像 “泰坦尼克” 号这么豪华奢侈的游轮竟然会没有足够拯救 所有乘客的救生衣和救生艇。 |
| 13 | pedestrian | /peˈdestriən/ | n. | a person going or traveling on foot | 行人； 步 行者 | Always be careful of pedestrians when you are driving a car， because anything that happens will be the fault of the driver. | 开车时你应该随时注意行人， 因为不管发生什么事， 驾驶员都是有错的 一方。 |
| 14 | restriction | /risˈtrikʃən/ | n. | the act of keeping within limits； limitation | 限 制； 约束 | There are several restrictions on what airlines will allow passengers to bring in and out of foreign countries. | 航空公司对于乘客能带出和入境的东西有一些限制。 |
| 15 | runway | /ˈrʌnwei/ | n. | a strip of specially prepared hard surface on which airplanes take off and land | 飞机跑道 | Jeanne was worried that the sleet and ice on the runway would delay the departure time of her flight. | 琴很担心在飞机跑道上的冰雪会延误飞机起飞时间。 |
| 16 | shortcut | /ˈʃɔːtkʌt/ | n. | a shorter route； any way of saving time， effort， etc. | 近路； 捷径 | If you’re in a hurry， turn left at the next light and I’ll show you a shortcut. | 如果你赶时间的话， 在下个路口左转， 我会告诉你一条捷径。 |
| 17 | take off | /teik ɔːf/ | v. | to rise into the air at the beginning of a flight | 起飞 | We took off at 11 o’clock in the morning and arrived in Venice at 1∶ 30 in the afternoon. | 我们在早上11∶ 00 起飞， 在下午1∶ 30 到达威尼斯。 |
| 18 | timetable | /ˈtaimteibl/ | n. | a schedule showing the times at which trains， planes， etc. arrive and depart； any schedule or plan designating the times at or within which certain things occur or are to occur | 时间表； 时 刻表 | For a local bus timetable， please contact UTA bus company. | 想要一份当地公交车时间表， 请联络 UTA 公交车公司。 |
| 19 | transport （1） | /trænsˈpɔːt/ | n. | the act of transporting or state of being transported； a means or system of transportation ˈtrænspɔːt/ vt. to carry people or goods from one place to another in a vehicle | 运输； 交通工具； 运 输系统 （2） / 运输； 运送 | The extra money could be spent on improving public transport. | 多余的钱将可以被用来改善公共运输。 |
| 20 | via | /ˈvaiə/ | prep. | by way of； by means of | 经由； 借由 | They flew to New York via Tokyo. | 他们经东京飞到纽约。 |

## Unit 12　Shopping 购物

### Unit 12 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | affordable | /əˈfɔːrdəbl/ | adj. | within one’s financial means | 负担得起的 | Smaller shops have items that are much more affordable for the average shopper. | 小型商店的物品对一般购物者来说价钱比较合理。 |
| 2 | bazaar | /bəˈzaː/ | n. | a marketplace or a group of shops | 市场； 集市 | The city’s bazaar was crowded with thieves， who harmed sales and tourism for many years. | 城里的集市有很多小偷， 多年来除了破坏生意外， 也影响了观光业。 |
| 3 | client | /ˈklaiənt/ | n. | a person who buys goods or services； a person who gets help and advice from a professional person， e. g. a lawyer | 顾客； 委 托人 | I think what our clients want is a wider selection of different cosmetic products. | 我想， 我们的顾客要的是更多不同种类的化妆品。 |
| 4 | customer | /ˈkʌstəmə/ | n. | a person or organization who buys goods or services from a shop， business， etc. | 顾客； 客户 | Pay more attention to the customers who enter the store. | 多注意些进到店里来的客人。 |
| 5 | discount | /ˈdiskaunt/ | n. | a reduction in the cost of goods | 折扣 | I never pay full price for my towels because I can find them on discount at so many outlet stores. | 我从来不付全价买毛巾， 因为我可以在很多零售店找到折扣品。 |
| 6 | distributor | /disˈtribjutə/ | n. | a person or organization that supplies or markets goods | 供应商； 经销商 | You need to contact the distributor if the safety seal of the product has been previously tampered with. | 如果产品的安全封口已有被打开过的迹象， 你就需要联络经销商。 |
| 7 | expiration | /ˌekspaiəˈreiʃən/ | n. | a coming to an end ； termination | 终结； 期 满 | “Sour milk” is a term used for either milk that has passed its expiration date or the bitter taste of learning a painful lesson. “ | 酸乳” 是一个词语， 用来指过期的牛奶或得到痛苦教训的苦涩感觉。 |
| 8 | itemize | /ˈaitəmaiz/ | vt./vi. | to set out all the details of； to list the individual units or parts of | 详细列举； 分条列举 | You have to itemize and make an inventory of everything in the store. | 你必须把店里的每一件东西逐项细列， 制成清单。 |
| 9 | latest | /ˈleitist/ | adj. | most recent； newest | 最新的 | I want to go to the electronics store and try the latest version of the race car simulator game. | 我要去电器行试试最新版的赛车模拟游戏。 |
| 10 | neon sign | /ˈniːɔnˌsain/ | n. | a sign made from glass tubes filled with neon gas which light up when an electric current goes through them | 霓虹灯招 牌 | If you head straight down Maple Boulevard， you will see a large green neon sign which is right above the doorway to the restaurant. | 如果你顺着枫叶大道直走， 你就会看到一个在餐厅入口处正上方的大型绿色 霓虹招牌。 |
| 11 | pickpocket | /ˈpikˌpɔkit/ | n. | a person who steals things from people’s pockets | 扒手 | Some pickpockets use razor blades to cut open your pocket and steal your wallet. | 有些扒手会用刮胡刀把你的口袋割开， 偷走你的皮夹。 |
| 12 | pushcart | /ˈpuʃkaːt/ | n. | a small cart pushed by hand； handcart | 手推车 | The pushcarts at the local supermarket are in bad shape because the store patrons often leave them out on the streets. | 本地超市的手推车挺破烂的， 因为超市的购物者常把它们留在外面的路上。 |
| 13 | receipt | /riˈsiːt/ | n. | a written acknowledgement that something has been received | 收据 | These items are all for my company； could I please get a receipt， so I can get refunded？ | 这些物品都是公司要的， 能否帮我开张收据， 以便我可以在公司报销？ |
| 14 | reimburse | /ˌriːimˈbəːs/ | vt. | to pay back； to repay | 偿还； 补偿 | Each month we are supposed to hand over all receipts and order forms to the accounting office， and they will then reimburse us for any expenditures. | 每个月我们都会交出所有的收据和订购单给会计部门， 然后他再将一些开支 的花费退还给我们。 |
| 15 | request | /riˈkwest/ | n. | an act of asking for something | 请求； 要求 | Due to insufficient company funds， the manager denied our request for a new computer. | 因为公司没有足够的资金， 经理拒绝了我们买新电脑的请求。 |
| 16 | sample | /ˈsæmpl/ | n. | a small part representing the whole； an example | 样品； 样本 | If you want to know how it tastes， just go to aisle 15， where they are giving away free samples. | 如果你想要知道味道如何， 就到第15 通道， 他们在那里提供免费品尝。 |
| 17 | solicit | /səˈlisit/ | vt./vi. | to ask for money， help， favor， etc. | 请求； 恳 求； 乞求 | I would ask you not to solicit business near our store， please. | 请你不要在我们商店附近兜售东西。 |
| 18 | trendy | /ˈtrendi/ | adj. | of or in the latest style； fashionable | 时髦的； 流 行的 | Always obsessed with the latest styles in fashion， Dave was labeled chronically trendy. | 总是沉迷于最新流行时尚， 戴维被认为是得了慢性 “流行” 病。 |
| 19 | Very Important Person |  |  |  | 贵宾 | If you have a VIP pass， you can go backstage and meet Modonna in person. | 如果你有一张贵宾证， 你可以到后台与麦当娜见面。 |
| 20 | wholesale | /ˈhəulseil/ | adj. | （1） adj. of being， or employed in the sale of goods in large quantities adv. in large bulk or quantity | 批发的 （2） 大量 地； 大批地 | If you buy these items wholesale， in the long run， you will save a lot of money. | 如果你成批地购买这些物品， 长期下来， 你就能省下不少钱。 |

### Unit 12 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | bargain | /ˈbaːgin/ | n. | （1） n. something for sale or bought for less than its real value vt. /vi. to talk about the conditions for a sale， agreement， or contract | 便宜货； 廉价品 （2） 讨价还价 | If we bargain with them， they might reduce the price. | 如果我们跟他们讨价还价， 他们可能会降价。 |
| 2 | cash register | /ˈkæʃˌredʒistə/ | n. | a machine for calculating and recording the amount of each sale and the money received | 收银机 | The girl at the cash register could not give me the correct change when I gave her pesos. | 收银台小姐无法找给我正确的零钱， 因为我给她的是比索。 |
| 3 | coupon | /ˈkuːpɔn/ | n. | a certificate entitling one to a specified right， as a discount， gift， ect. | 折价券； 赠奖券 | Karen’s grandmother loves to cut out coupons in the newspaper for her favorite foods. | 凯伦的祖母喜欢从报纸上剪下她最喜欢的食物的折价券。 |
| 4 | defective | /diˈfektiv/ | adj. | not working properly； faulty； imperfect | 有毛病 的； 有问题的； 有瑕疵的 | The bread maker’s defective lid made it impossible for anyone to use it. | 面包机有缺陷的盖子让人无法使用这个机器。 |
| 5 | display | /diˈsplei/ | n. | （1） n. an act of displaying something，or something that is displayed vt. to arrange or spread out for public view | 陈列； 展示品 （2） 陈列； 展示 | Mark longed for the newly developed CD player after seeing it on display in the store. | 自从在店里看到那个新推出的 CD 唱机展示之后， 马克就一直非常想拥 有它。 |
| 6 | drugstore | /ˈdrʌgstɔː/ | n. | a store where drugs， medical supplies， and various items are sold and prescriptions are filled | 兼卖杂货的药房 | I usually have my prescriptions filled at the drugstore across the street. | 我通常拿处方笺到街对面的那家药房买药。 |
| 7 | flea market | /ˈfliːˌmaːkit/ | n. | a market where cheap or used goods are sold | 跳 蚤市场 | Flea markets are frequented by bargain hunters. | 喜欢拣便宜货的人经常会去跳蚤市场。 |
| 8 | label | /ˈleibl/ | n. | a card， paper， etc. marked and attached to an object to indicate its contents， owner， destination， etc. | 标签； 标记 | By using plain labels， generic food brands can afford to charge less because of the money saved on packaging. | 由于使用简单的标签， 没有品牌的食物能低价出售， 因为省下了大笔的包 装费。 |
| 9 | modify | /ˈmɔdifai/ | vt./vi. | to change partially in character， form， etc. | 修 改； 变更 | I need to find a CD player that can be modified to fit my old stereo set. | 我需要找一个改装后能配合我的旧音响组合的 CD 播放机。 |
| 10 | patron | /ˈpeitrən/ | n. | a regular customer； one who sponsors and supports some organization， activity， etc. （ | 老） 顾客； 赞助人 | Each patron of our business should feel welcome in our store. | 我们公司的每个顾客到我们店里都应该觉得受到欢迎。 |
| 11 | purchase | /ˈpəːtʃəs/ | n. | （1） n. the act of buying； an article bought vt. to buy | 购买； 购得之物 （2） 购买 | We will deliver your purchases to your home in three days. | 我们3 天之内会将您所购买的物品送到府上。 |
| 12 | quantity | /ˈkwɔntiti/ | n. | an amount or number | 量； 数量 | Upon ordering， please specify the quantity in the appropriate space on the order form. | 订货的时候， 请在订货单的适当空格注明你要的数量。 |
| 13 | refund （1） | /riːˈfʌnd/ | n. | a sum of money refunded riˈfʌnd/ vt. /vi. to give back money in return for loss or damage | 退款 （2） / 退还； 赔偿 | If there are any defects or problems with our products， just bring them back and we will give you a full refund. | 如果我们的产品有任何瑕疵或问题， 你都可以退货， 我们会全额退款。 |
| 14 | replacement | /riˈpleismənt/ | n. | one who or which takes the place of another； substitution | 代替； 替换 | Would you please stop by the hardware store and pick up a replacement filter for the air conditioning unit？ | 可否请你出去时顺便帮我到五金商店买个空调机的替换过滤网？ |
| 15 | retailer | /riːˈteilə/ | n. | one who sells goods in a store to consumers | 零售商 | Sam Walton， who single-handedly built Wal-Mart， is the largest retailer in the United States. | 山姆·沃顿独立建立了 Wal-Mart， 成为美国最大的零售商。 |
| 16 | shopping mall | /ˈʃɔpiŋˌmɔːl/ | n. | a shopping center | 大型购物中心 | The shopping mall is always packed with shoppers during Christmas season. | 那个大型购物中心在圣诞节期间总是挤满了购物的人潮。 |
| 17 | thrift | /θrift/ | n. | wise and careful use of money and goods； avoidance of waste | 节俭； 节约 | By thrift， he managed to get along on his small salary. | 他省吃俭用， 虽然薪水微薄， 但是还可以度日。 |
| 18 | vender | /ˈvendə/ | n. | a seller of small articles that can be carried about or pushed on a cart | 小贩 | The sidewalk vender on Wall St. sells the best hot dogs I have ever tasted. | 华尔街路边那个小贩卖的热狗是我吃过的最好吃的热狗。 |
| 19 | warranty | /ˈwɔrənti/ | n. | an assurance by a seller that goods or property will be repaired or replaced if not as represented； a written guarantee | 保证； 保证书 | The printer comes with a one-year full service warranty， which covers both parts and labor. | 这台打印机附有一年完全维修的保证书， 零件和修复的人工费用都包括 在内。 |
| 20 | workmanship | /ˈwəːkmənʃip/ | n. | skill in making things； the quality of the work done | 技巧； 手艺 | The workmanship on these dining tables and matching chairs is extraordinary. | 这些餐桌和配套的椅子的手工不同凡响。 |

## Unit 13　Dining Out 在外用餐

### Unit 13 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | alcohol | /ˈælkəhɔl/ | n. | a colorless， limpid， volatile， flammable， watermiscible liquid， used chiefly as a solvent in the extraction of specific substances， in beverages， medicines， lotions， tonics， colognes， etc.； drinks containing this | 酒精； 含酒精之饮料 | When we go out， my friends usually drink alcohol and I end up being the designated driver. | 当我跟朋友外出时， 他们通常会喝酒， 所以我最后都被指派开车载他们 回家。 |
| 2 | appetite | /ˈæpitait/ | n. | a desire or wish to have something， esp. food | 胃 口； 食欲 | If you don’t really have an appetite for food， we should just go to a coffee shop and relax. | 如果你不是真的想吃东西， 我们可以到咖啡店去轻松一下。 |
| 3 | artificial | /ˌaːtiˈfiʃl/ | adj. | made by humans， esp. as a copy of something natural | 人造的； 仿真的 | I do not like many of the prepared foods you can buy in the stores because of all the artificial ingredients the manufacturers put in them. | 我不喜欢许多店里可以买到的现成食物， 因为制造厂商在里面放了各种人工 添加物。 |
| 4 | bland | /blænd/ | adj. | （of food） without much taste； （of people） showing no strong feelings or opinions； mild and soothing | 淡而无味的； 温和的； 无刺激性的 | The food here is normally really tasty， but today it all seems so bland and unappetizing. | 这里的食物通常都很好吃， 但是今天似乎平淡无味， 引不起胃口。 |
| 5 | chef | /ʃef/ | n. | a cook， especially a chief cook | 主厨； 大厨 | I would really like to meet the chef and give him my personal compliment for this wonderful six-course meal. | 我很想见见这里的大厨， 并向他表达我个人对这六道菜的赞美。 |
| 6 | crave | /kreiv/ | vt./vi. | to have an intense desire for | 渴望 | Opponents of the tobacco industry believe that the use of nicotine allows consumers to crave for and thus become addicted to cigarettes. | 反对烟草业的人认为， 尼古丁会使人渴望抽烟并因而上瘾。 |
| 7 | delicacy | /ˈdelikəsi/ | n. | a choice food | 佳肴 | Even though escargot is considered a delicacy by most， it’s still just snails to me. | 虽然法国蜗牛被大部分人视为珍馐， 但对我来说它只是蜗牛罢了。 |
| 8 | digest | /diˈdʒest/ | vt./vi. | to change food into an absorbable form that the body can use | 消化 | Those who are lactose intolerant find it difficult to digest dairy products. | 患有乳糖不耐症的人很难消化奶制品。 |
| 9 | fragrant | /ˈfreigrənt/ | adj. | having a pleasant odor； sweet-smelling | 有香味 的； 芳香的 | The most fragrant and flavorful wines seem to be the ones that come from France. | 最香最有味道的酒好像都来自法国。 |
| 10 | ingredient | /inˈgriːdiənt/ | n. | something that is an element in a mixture or compound； component； constituent | 成分； 配料； 配方 | She just won’t tell me the secret ingredients in her fried chicken. | 她就是不肯告诉我她的独门炸鸡里加了什么特殊配方。 |
| 11 | liquor | /ˈlikə/ | n. | a strong alcoholic drink， such as whisky | 烈酒 （如威士 忌） | He likes to drink liquor， such as vodka. | 他喜欢喝像伏特加那样的烈酒。 |
| 12 | munch | /mʌntʃ/ | vt./vi. | to chew with a strong movement of the jaw， making a noise | 用力嚼； 大声咀嚼 | In order to abate his hunger， Joe munched on a candy bar. | 为了减低他的饥饿感， 乔大口嚼食糖果棒。 |
| 13 | palatable | /ˈpælətəbl/ | adj. | pleasant to the taste； agreeable to the mind or sensibilities | 美味的； 顺耳的； 怡人的 | Although others had reported the beef to be chewy and lacking in flavor， I found it to be very palatable. | 虽然别人说这牛肉很韧， 也没有什么味道， 但是我却觉得味很美。 |
| 14 | pass up | /ˈpaːsʌp/ | v. | to refuse or neglect to take advantage of an opportunity | 放过； 错过 | Thanks for the offer， but I’m starting to watch my weight. I had better pass up on dessert this time. | 谢谢你的甜点， 但是我正在开始注意自己的体重， 所以这一次最好还是不 要吃。 |
| 15 | portion | /ˈpɔːʃən/ | n. | a part of a whole； a share of something | 部分； 一份 | That piece of meat is huge， and I’m certain I can only eat a portion of it. | 这块肉很大， 我想我只能吃下一部分。 |
| 16 | ravenous | /ˈrævinəs/ | adj. | extremely hungry； rapacious | 极饿的； 贪婪的 | He has been exercising non-stop all day， so he must have worked up a ravenous appetite. | 他已经运动了一整天， 所以他现在一定饿扁了。 |
| 17 | refreshment | /riˈfreʃmənt/ | n. | （pl. ） food or drink or both | 茶点 （常用复 数） | We are going to just serve light refreshments at the reception rather than a lavish meal. | 我们在招待会上只会提供简单的点心， 而不是豪华的大餐。 |
| 18 | silverware | /ˈsilvəwɛə/ | n. | articles， esp. tableware， made of or plated with silver | 银器； 镀银餐具 | Could you please put out the napkins and the silverware next to each plate？ | 可以请你在每个盘子旁边帮忙摆上餐巾和餐具吗？ |
| 19 | specialty | /ˈspeʃəlti/ | n. | a special study， interest， quality， feature， etc. | 专攻； 专业； 专长； 特制品 | Baked chicken is his specialty， and he has won several prestigious awards for it. | 他的专长是烤鸡， 他已经为此获得多项大奖。 |
| 20 | starve | /staːv/ | vt./vi. | to die from lack of food； to suffer from hunger | 饿 死； 挨饿 | We were only given two meals a day when we were on our trip and I thought we would starve！ | 在旅行的时候， 我们一天只被供应两餐， 我还以为我们会饿死！ |

### Unit 13 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | apéritif | /əˌperiˈtiːf/ | n. | an alcoholic drink taken before a meal | 饭前酒 | Many of the finer restaurants will give you an apéritif before the meal to help stimulate your appetite. | 很多较高级的餐厅都会给你饭前酒来促进你的食欲。 |
| 2 | appetizer | /ˈæpitaizə/ | n. | a food or drink taken before a meal to increase the desire for food | 开胃菜 | Would you like to order an appetizer first， or would you just like to wait for the main entrée to arrive？ | 你要先点个开胃菜吗， 或是你要等主菜上来？ |
| 3 | beverage | /ˈbevəridʒ/ | n. | any liquid for drinking， esp. one other than water | 饮料 （除水之外） | This café is for people to sit and drink cold beverages rather than having large meals. | 这间小餐馆主要是提供人们一个坐下喝冷饮而不是吃大餐的地方。 |
| 4 | buffet | /ˈbufei/ | n. | a meal at which guests serve themselves from dishes arranged on a table or sideboard | 自助餐 | Because of his seemingly bottomless stomach， Erik loved to go to all-you-can-eat style buffets. | 因为艾瑞克的胃像无底洞， 他喜欢去 “吃到饱” 的自助式餐厅。 |
| 5 | chopsticks | /ˈtʃɔpstiks/ | n. | a pair of slender sticks held together in one hand and used， chiefly in the Orient， to lift food to the mouth | 筷子 | I love Chinese food， but I always feel embarrassed because I can’t use chopsticks very well. | 我喜欢吃中国菜， 但是我常为不会用筷子而感到不好意思。 |
| 6 | cuisine | /kwi（ː）ˈziːn/ | n. | a style of cooking or preparing food； the food prepared | 烹调； 烹调之食品 | The delicious cuisine of the Indian restaurants makes everyone want to use curry in all their dishes. | 印度餐厅的美食使每个人都想在他们所有的菜里加上咖喱。 |
| 7 | dessert | /diˈzəːt/ | n. | a usually sweet food served as the last course of a meal | 餐后甜点 | After finishing the main course， the waiter asked us if we would like some cheesecake for dessert. | 吃完主菜之后， 侍者问我们是否要一些起司蛋糕当甜点。 |
| 8 | entrée | /ˈɔntrei/ | n. | the main course of a meal | 主菜 | The meal includes a soup， a salad， an appetizer， the main entrée， and a dessert. | 这顿饭包括汤、 沙拉、 开胃菜、 主菜， 还有饭后甜点。 |
| 9 | hors d’oeuvre | /ˌɔːˈdəːv/ | n. | an appetizer served before a meal | 开胃小菜 | Their party was very extravagant； there was full catering with the most delicate hors d’oeuvres I have ever had. | 他们的宴会非常豪华， 不但有全套酒席， 还有我所吃过的最精致的开胃 小菜。 |
| 10 | leftovers | /ˈleftˌəuvəz/ | n. | food remaining uneaten after a meal， esp. when served at a later meal | 剩饭剩菜 | The greatest part of Thanksgiving dinner is that there are leftovers in the fridge for the next several weeks. | 感恩节大餐最棒的地方就是留在冰箱的剩菜够吃好几个星期。 |
| 11 | menu | /ˈmenjuː/ | n. | a list of dishes to be served or available for a meal or a series of meals | 菜单 | I’m still waiting for my friends to arrive， but I’d like to look over the menu while I wait. | 我还在等朋友， 但我想在等时先看看菜单。 |
| 12 | napkin | /ˈnæpkin/ | n. | a piece of cloth or absorbed paper used while eating to protect one’s clothes and wipe one’s lips | 餐巾 | It is customary for flight attendants to hand out wet napkins for passengers to wipe their hands with. | 飞机上的服务人员通常会递湿纸巾给旅客擦手。 |
| 13 | parched | /ˈpaːtʃt/ | adj. | completely dry； very thirsty | 焦干的； 极渴的 | While walking across the Sahara Desert， we felt very parched and couldn’t wait to find some water. | 在我们穿越撒哈拉沙漠时， 觉得极度干渴， 迫不及待想找水喝。 |
| 14 | pastry | /ˈpeistri/ | n. | a small sweet cake with crust baked from flour dough made with shortening | 用面团和油酥烤成的小甜点心 | The new bakery has some good bread， but the pastries seem to be their specialty. | 新开的面包店有一些很棒的面包， 但是小点心好像才是他们的特色产品。 |
| 15 | preservative | /priˈzəːvətiv/ | n. | a chemical substance that is used to prevent things from decaying | 防腐剂 | This store sells only products that contain no artificial preservatives. | 这家店只卖不含人工防腐剂的产品。 |
| 16 | recipe | /ˈresipi/ | n. | a list of materials and instructions for preparing a dish or drink； a way to achieve something | 食谱； 烹饪法； 秘诀 | She didn’t follow the recipe and the soup came out all wrong. | 她没按食谱去做， 结果做出来的汤一点都不对味。 |
| 17 | seasoning | /ˈsiːzəniŋ/ | n. | anything that adds zest to or improves the flavor of food | 调味品； 佐料 | The seasoning on these steamed vegetables tastes almost like parsley mixed with orange juice， but it’s hard to tell. | 这些清蒸蔬菜上所加的调味料吃起来几乎像是香菜和柳橙汁的混合， 但我无 法确定。 |
| 18 | snack | /snæk/ | n. | （1） n. a light meal between regular meals vi. to eat a snack | 小吃； 点心 （2） 吃点心 | Dinner is two hours away； you can have some snacks if you’re hungry. | 离晚餐还有两个小时， 如果你肚子饿的话， 可以先用些点心。 |
| 19 | spice | /spais/ | n. | an aromatic or pungent plant substance， as cinnamon or pepper， used to season food | 香料； 调味品 | In Indian cuisine spices and seasonings always seem to blend perfectly with the food. | 印度菜里所使用的香料和调味品似乎都和食物搭配得很完美。 |
| 20 | vegetarian | /ˌvedʒiˈtɛəriən/ | n. | one who eats no meat | 素食者 | Some people become vegetarians due to religious beliefs. | 有些人因为宗教信仰而成为素食者。 |

## Unit 14　Social Contact 社交

### Unit 14 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | acceptance | /əkˈseptəns/ | n. | the act of taking or receiving something offered； approval | 接受； 赞同 | Many people change their personality or lower their personal standards in search of what they call “acceptance”. | 很多人改变他们的个性或降低标准来寻求他们所谓的 “认同感”。 |
| 2 | acquaintance | /əˈkweintəns/ | n. | a person whom one knows slightly | 相识 但不很熟之人 | I need to find some friends in Taiwan because the people at work are really only acquaintances. | 我必须在台湾交一些朋友， 因为工作上认识的人只能称为点头之交。 |
| 3 | anniversary | /ˌæniˈvəːsəri/ | n. | the yearly return of the date of some event | 周年纪念日 | They are celebrating their 25 th wedding anniversary by taking a trip to Hawaii. | 他们到夏威夷度假， 以庆祝结婚25 周年。 |
| 4 | associate | /əˈsəuʃieit/ | n. | （1） n. a person connected with another， esp. in work； partner vt. to connect in thought， memory， or imagination； to bring into relation as partner， etc. | 同事； 伙伴 （2） 联想； 交往 | It seems that people from Asian countries automatically associate Americans with blond hair and blue eyes. | 似乎亚洲人总是很自动地把美国人联想成金发蓝眼。 |
| 5 | banquet | /ˈbæŋkwit/ | n. | an elaborate feast； a formal dinner honoring a guest or occasion | 酒席； 正式的宴会 | The banquet will be held at 8∶ 00 p. m. ， during which the mayor will personally hand out all the awards. | 宴会将在8 点开始， 期间市长将亲自颁发所有奖项。 |
| 6 | commitment | /kəˈmitmənt/ | n. | a responsibility or promise to follow certain beliefs or a certain course of action | 承诺； 许诺 | In modern society many people seem to have forgotten that marriage is a lifelong commitment. | 现代社会里许多人似乎已经忘记婚姻是终身的承诺。 |
| 7 | courtesy | /ˈkəːtisi/ | n. | polite behavior； good manners； a polite or kind action or expression | 礼貌； 礼仪； 好意； 殷勤 | It is common courtesy not to use your cell phone in a restaurant. | 在餐厅内不使用手机是一种普通的礼貌。 |
| 8 | distinguished | /disˈtiŋgwiʃt/ | adj. | characterized by excellence； eminent； famous | 卓越的； 杰出的； 著名的 | Some of the most distinguished authors will be attending the awards-giving ceremony this Saturday night. | 一些最著名的作家会来参加这个星期六晚上的颁奖典礼。 |
| 9 | embrace | /imˈbreis/ | n. | （1） n. an act of embracing vt. /vi. to clasp or hold in the arms lovingly； to encircle， to include | 拥抱 （2） 拥抱； 围绕； 包括 | It is common to see people embrace each other in America. | 在美国常见人们相互拥抱。 |
| 10 | etiquette | /ˈetiket/ | n. | the forms， manners， etc. conventionally acceptable or required in society， a profession， etc. | 礼节； 规范 | It is important， when traveling abroad， to become acquainted with the local etiquette and customs. | 到国外旅游时， 熟悉当地的礼仪和风俗习惯很重要。 |
| 11 | farewell | /fɛəˈwel/ | n. | an expression of good wishes at parting； goodbye； leave-taking | 一路平安； 再见； 告别 | We stayed two days in Norway and then had to bid farewell to the friends we made there. | 我们在挪威停留了两天， 但之后就得跟我们在那里认识的朋友们告别。 |
| 12 | get-together | /get təˈgeðə/ | n. | an informal social gathering （ | 非正式的社 交） 聚会 | We decided to have a little get-togehter for our new neighbors， so they could meet all the people in the building. | 我们决定帮新来的邻居办个小聚会， 让他们认识一下住在同一栋大楼的人。 |
| 13 | greeting | /ˈgriːtiŋ/ | n. | a gesture or word of welcome or salutation； （pl. ） a message of regards or a good wish | 致敬； 问候； （复数） 致意； 赞词 | I just talked to Rich last night on the phone， and he asked how you were and sent his greetings. | 我昨晚才跟李奇通过电话， 他问到你的近况并要我跟你问好。 |
| 14 | hug | /hʌg/ | n. | （1） n. an act of hugging （2） vt. /vi. to clasp or hold closely and fondly in the arms； to embrace | 拥抱； 紧抱 | Before she left， she gave her father a hug. | 走之前， 她拥抱了一下她父亲。 |
| 15 | lavish | /ˈlæviʃ/ | adj. | very generous or wasteful； extravagant； profuse | 过 分大方的； 浪费的； 丰富的 | Not only is their house the biggest one in the neighborhood， but it also has the most lavish furnishings inside. | 他们的房子不但是附近最大的， 屋内也拥有最奢华的家具摆设。 |
| 16 | nosy | /ˈnəuzi/ | adj. | interested in things that do not concern one； prying； inquisitive | 好管闲事的； 爱打听的 | Their nosy neighbors seem to be constantly watching them. | 他们那些好管闲事的邻居似乎无时无刻不在监视他们。 |
| 17 | offensive | /əˈfensiv/ | adj. | causing displeasure； disgusting； insulting | 令 人不快的； 讨厌的； 冒犯的 | Burping in public is not only rude but also very offensive. | 在公共场合打嗝不但粗鲁， 而且相当令人讨厌。 |
| 18 | protocol | /ˈprəutəkɔl/ | n. | the customs and regulations dealing with diplomatic formality， etiquette， etc.； an original draft of a document， etc. | 外交礼节； 草约 | Protocol demands that the Prime Minister attend the ceremony in person. | 依外交礼节， 首相应当亲自出席这项典礼。 |
| 19 | regards | /riˈgaːdz/ | n. | good wishes | 问候； 致意 | I won’t be able to make it to their wedding； please give them this gift as well as my best regards and congratulations. | 我无法去参加他们的婚礼， 请帮我将此礼物交给他们， 并代我向他们致最诚 挚的敬贺。 |
| 20 | salute | /səˈluːt/ | vt./vi. | to greet； to recognize （a military superior） with a prescribed gesture； to honor formally | 向……打招呼； 向……行军礼； 向……表示敬意 | The President and Commander in Chief was saluted by the officers as he emerged from the plane. | 身兼三军统帅的总统走出飞机时受到军官们的行礼致敬。 |

### Unit 14 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | acknowledge | /əkˈnɔlidʒ/ | vt. | to admit the validity， authority， or truth of； to recognize the existence， authority or claims of； to express or show appreciation or gratitude for | 承认； 承认……之存在、 权力； 表示感谢 | After the decision of the Supreme Court was announced， Vice President Al Gore acknowledged defeat. | 在最高法院判决宣布之后， 副总统艾尔·戈尔承认失败。 |
| 2 | aggressive | /əˈgresiv/ | adj. | quick to attack or act in a hostile fashion； determined and forceful； bold and active； enterprising | 挑衅的； 有侵略性 的； 有进取心的； 积极的 | Although he was usually considered mild-mannered， he became very aggressive behind the wheel. | 虽然一般人觉得他温和有礼， 但是一旦坐上驾驶座， 他就变得侵略性十足。 |
| 3 | apologize | /əˈpɔlədʒaiz/ | vi. | to say one is sorry | 道歉 | Billy’s mom made him return to the store and apologize for pilfering. | 比利的妈妈要他回到商店里为偷东西而道歉。 |
| 4 | attractive | /əˈtræktiv/ | adj. | arousing interest， admiration or pleasure； appealing； charming | 引人入胜的； 有吸引力的； 迷人的 | I think what makes a person attractive includes a lot more than just outward appearance. | 我想使一个人有魅力的原因很多， 不只是外表。 |
| 5 | barrier | /ˈbæriə/ | n. | an obstruction； obstacle； anything that blocks or hinders | 障碍； 障碍物； 隔阂 | Many people living abroad find that the language barrier is very difficult to get around. | 很多住在外国的人都觉得， 语言障碍是很难克服的。 |
| 6 | compliment | /ˈkɔmplimənt/ | n. | an expression of praise， admiration or respect | 赞扬； 恭维 | His compliments rarely seemed genuine and often insulted those he was trying to praise. | 他给人的赞美很少让人觉得很真心， 反而经常侮辱到他想赞美的人。 |
| 7 | criticize | /ˈkritisaiz/ | vt./vi. | to judge the merits and faults of； to judge with disapproval | 评论； 批评 | He has no friends because he likes to criticize everyone. | 他之所以没有朋友， 是因为他喜欢批评每个人。 |
| 8 | eloquent | /ˈeləkwənt/ | adj. | able to express ideas and opinions readily and well； fluent and persuasive in discourse | 口才好的； 雄辩的 | Adolph Hitler was a passionate orator and was known for his eloquent speeches. | 阿道夫·希特勒是一个激昂的演说家， 他以雄辩著称。 |
| 9 | encounter | /inˈkauntə/ | n. | （1） n. a meeting， either unexpected or dangerous vt. /vi. to meet unexpectedly； to be faced with | 相遇； 遭遇 （2） 偶然碰见； 遭遇； 面临 | We had an accidental encounter with three Russian scientists on our expedition to the North Pole. | 在到北极探险的途中我们偶遇3 位来自俄国的科学家。 |
| 10 | extend | /iksˈtend/ | vt./vi. | to stretch or continue； to enlarge or prolong； to give or offer | 延伸； 延续； 扩大； 延长； 给予； 提供 | He extended an invitation to everyone in the office to come to his birthday party. | 他邀请办公室里的每个人去参加他的生日派对。 |
| 11 | favorable | /ˈfeivərəbl/ | adj. | characterized by approval； winning approval； helpful； advantageous | 令人满意的； 博得赞许的； 有帮助的； 有利的 | Between the rented apartment and the hotel room， he found the accommodations in the latter to be much more favorable. | 在出租公寓和旅馆之间做比较， 他发现旅馆的设备好多了。 |
| 12 | gossip | /ˈgɔsip/ | n. | （1） n. idle talk or rumor， esp. about the personal or private affairs of others vi. to talk idly， esp. about the affairs of others | 闲话； 流言 （2） 说闲话 | I don’t enjoy working here because everyone seems to be gossiping about everyone else all the time. | 我不喜欢在这里工作， 因为每个人似乎都一直在讲别人的闲话。 |
| 13 | hilarious | /hiˈlɛəriəs/ | adj. | noisily merry； boisterously funny | 狂欢的； 令 人捧腹的 | The hilarious Jim Carrey has entertained audiences with his madcap antics and funny facial expressions. | 滑稽的金·凯利利用他疯狂古怪的举动和好玩的表情使很多观众感到快乐。 |
| 14 | intrude | /inˈtruːd/ | vt./vi. | to enter unwanted or unasked； to forced upon others | 闯入； 侵入； 把……强加于 | It’s better not to enter a door without knocking so as not to intrude on someone’s privacy. | 在进门之前最好先敲门， 免得不小心侵犯他人的隐私。 |
| 15 | modest | /ˈmɔdist/ | adj. | having or expressing a lower opinion of one’s ability than is probably deserved； not large in quantity， size， value， etc.； not vain； decorous | 谦逊的； 适度的； 朴实的； 端庄的 | Compared with those in America， houses in Mexico seem a lot more modest. | 跟美国的房子比起来， 墨西哥的房子看来简朴多了。 |
| 16 | nuisance | /ˈnjuːsns/ | n. | a person， thing， or situation that causes annoyance or inconvenience | 讨厌之人、 事、 物 | Taking my nine-year-old brother out with my friends is a nuisance. | 带着我9 岁的弟弟和朋友去玩是件很麻烦的事。 |
| 17 | prompt | /ˈprɔmpt/ | adj. | arriving at the right time； punctual； done without delay； quick to take action | 准时的； 按时的； 迅速的； 立刻行 动的 | The seminar is going to start at eight o’clock on the dot， so please be prompt and be in your seat by a quarter to eight. | 这个研讨会将在8 点整开始， 请准时到场， 并请在7∶ 45 分时就座。 |
| 18 | quarrel | /ˈkwɔrəl/ | n. | （1） n. an angry argument； a dispute vi. to have a quarrel | 争吵； 争论 （2） 吵架； 争论 | It is quite normal for younger siblings to quarrel with one another. | 年幼的兄弟姐妹互相争吵是很正常的。 |
| 19 | run into | /rʌn ˈintu/ | v. | to meet by chance； to bump into | 偶遇； 碰上 | Who would have thought that we would run into so many old classmates all on the same day！ | 谁会想得到， 我们会在同一天中碰到这么多老同学！ |
| 20 | scandal | /ˈskændl/ | n. | anything that offends moral feelings and leads to disgrace | 丑闻； 引人愤慨之事 | The new corporate president was involved in some illegal smuggling scandal， which resulted in his resignation. | 新的公司总裁卷入了某个非法走私丑闻， 并因此辞职下台。 |

## Unit 15　Entertainment & Mass Media 娱乐及大众传播媒体

### Unit 15 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | amuse | /əˈmjuːz/ | vt. | to keep pleasantly occupied； to entertain； to cause to laugh or smile | 使欢乐； 使愉快； 使发笑 | If it is a long trip， just give him a pen and paper and he will amuse himself. | 如果旅途很长， 只要给他纸和笔， 他就会自得其乐。 |
| 2 | audience | /ˈɔːdjəns/ | n. | the people listening to or watching a performance， speech， television show， etc. | 听众； 观众 | Tonight’s audience was barely awake， much less attentively watching the play. | 今晚的观众简直就快睡着了， 更别提专心看戏剧了。 |
| 3 | ballet | /bæˈlei/ | n. | an artistic dance form of graceful， precise gestures and movements used to tell a story | 芭蕾舞 | One of the most physically demanding performance arts， ballet， is known for its grace and beauty. | 芭蕾舞是一种需要极大体力的表演艺术， 它以优雅和美丽著称。 |
| 4 | cable TV | /ˈkeibl ˈtiːviː/ | n. | a commercial television system in which signals are received by a single antenna and delivered to subscribers’ receivers by means of a cable | 有线电视 | When there was no cable TV， everyone was limited to just a few channels like CCTV1， CCTV2. | 还没有有线电视的时候， 每个人只有少数几个频道可以观看， 例如中央 1 台、 中央2 台等。 |
| 5 | celebrity | /siˈlebriti/ | n. | a famous person； the state of being famous | 名人； 名声 | I hope that Tiger Woods won’t let his celebrity status interfere with how he treats his fans. | 我希望老虎·伍兹不会让他的名声影响到他对待球迷的态度。 |
| 6 | cinema | /ˈsinimə/ | n. | movies； films； a movie theater； the art or industry of making movies | 电影； 影片； 电影院； 电影艺术； 电影业 | They say the new movie， a cinema breakthrough， is a must-see for all avid moviegoers. | 据说那部新影片是影史上的大突破， 所有的热心影迷都一定要去观赏。 |
| 7 | composer | /kəmˈpəuzə/ | n. | a person who writes music | 作曲家 | John Williams is a very famous composer and symphony conductor who has written many movie soundtracks. | 约翰·威廉姆斯是一个非常有名的作曲家和交响乐团指挥家， 他曾经写了 许多电影配乐。 |
| 8 | conductor | /kənˈdʌktə/ | n. | one who directs the playing of a group of musicians | 指挥家 | As the audience cheered and applauded， the conductor slowly lowered his baton， pivoted and graciously bowed to the crowd. | 在观众的欢呼鼓掌下， 这个指挥家慢慢地放下他的指挥棒， 原地转身， 然后 优雅地向观众行礼。 |
| 9 | cue | /kjuː/ | n. | a word or signal to begin or enter， as in a play； a hint or reminder | 暗示； 提示 | The singer missed her cue from the conductor and started too late in the song. | 这个歌手错过了指挥给她的提示， 起头太慢了。 |
| 10 | drama | /ˈdraːmə/ | n. | a piece of writing to be performed by actors； play for the theater， television， radio， etc. | 戏剧； 剧本 | Everyone likes Shakespeare’s drama； I especially enjoy his light comedies， like A Midsummer Night  s Dream and As You Like It. | 每个人都喜欢莎士比亚的戏剧， 我特别喜欢他的轻松喜剧， 例如 《仲夏夜之 梦》 和 《皆大欢喜》。 |
| 11 | fare | /fɛə/ | n. | money paid for transportation | 车费； 票价 | The fare for a taxi usually depends on how far you are traveling. | 出租车费的多少通常是依你坐车的距离长短而定。 |
| 12 | headline | /ˈhedlain/ | n. | the heading printed in large letter above a story in a newspaper （ | 报纸的） 标题 | I don’t read every article in the newspaper； I just skim over all the headlines. | 我不看报纸上的每一篇文章， 我只浏览所有标题。 |
| 13 | newscast | /ˈnjuːzkaːst/ | n. | a broadcast of news on radio or television | 新 闻广播 | Jonathan watched the local newscast every night at six o’clock without fail. | 乔纳森每天晚上6 点一定观看当地的新闻报道。 |
| 14 | periodical | /ˌpiəriˈɔdikl/ | n. | a magazine or other journal that is issued at regular times | 杂志； 期刊 | If you want to find that kind of information， you should try the periodical section. | 如果你要找那类的资讯， 应该到期刊部门去试试。 |
| 15 | premiere | /ˈpremiə/ | n. | a first public presentation of a play or film | 首次公 演； 首映 | Famous people love to attend movie premieres in order to get publicity and hobnob with movies stars. | 名人喜欢参加电影首映会， 如此既可以得到宣传效果， 也可以和电影明星 交好。 |
| 16 | rate | /reit/ | vt. | to place in a certain rank， class， etc. | 分级； 分等 | Before we go to see the movie， we’d better check to see what it’s rated. | 去看电影之前， 我们最好先查看一下它属于哪一级。 |
| 17 | scenario | /siˈnaːriəu/ | n. | an outline of a dramatic work； the script of a film | 戏剧的情节； 电影的脚本 | I really enjoyed reading your script， but I feel that some of the scenarios do not fit in the storyline very smoothly and probably should be changed. | 我真的很喜欢读你的剧本， 但是我觉得有些情节跟整个故事的发展不太搭 调， 可能需要改变一下。 |
| 18 | scriptwriter | /ˈskriptraitə/ | n. | one who writes the text of a play， film， etc. | 剧作家； 编剧 | Many movie companies purchase scripts from professional scriptwriters， and then adapt them for motion pictures. | 很多电影公司从职业剧作家那里买下剧本， 然后改编成电影。 |
| 19 | symphony | /ˈsimfəni/ | n. | a piece of music for an orchestra， usually having four movements | 交响曲 | The New York Philharmonic will be performing Beethoven’s 9th Symphony this Saturday to celebrate the President’s 55 th birthday. | 纽约爱乐交响乐团将在这个星期六演奏贝多芬的 《第九交响曲》， 以庆祝总 统的55 岁生日。 |
| 20 | ticket office | /ˈtikit ˈɔːfis/ | n. | a place in a theater， concert hall， etc. where tickets are sold； box office | 售票处 | Jon went up to the window of the ticket office， got the money out to buy the tickets， but was told that they were all sold out. | 强走到售票窗口前， 拿出钱要买票， 却被告知票已全部售完。 |

### Unit 15 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | applaud | /əˈplɔːd/ | vt./vi. | to express approval （of） by clapping the hands | 鼓掌； 赞同 | The audience began to applaud wildly as the diva finished her heartfelt solo. | 当歌剧中的女主角结束她感人的独唱时， 观众开始疯狂地鼓掌。 |
| 2 | audition | /ɔːˈdiʃən/ | n. | a hearing to try out a singer actor， etc. | 试听； 试演 | I just heard they are having auditions for the musical Cats； maybe I can try too. | 我刚听说他们现在正在办 《猫》 歌舞剧的演员试听会， 或许我也可以试试。 |
| 3 | broadcast | /ˈbrɔːdkaːst/ | n./vt./vi. | to transmit by radio or television； to make known over a wide area | 广播； 宣传 | Her performance will be broadcast on Channel 20 everyday this week from 8∶ 00 p.m. to 9∶ 30 p.m. | 她的表演会在这个星期每天晚上8 点到9 点半在20 号频道播出。 |
| 4 | carnival | /ˈkaːnivəl/ | n. | （a festival marked by） merrymaking and feasting； a traveling amusement show | 狂欢； 欢宴； 嘉年华会； 巡回娱乐表演 | I saw a poster today which says that the carnival will start this Thursday at the fairgrounds. | 我今天看到一张海报， 上面说嘉年华会将在这个星期四在露天市场举办。 |
| 5 | choreography | /ˌkɔːriˈɔgrəfi/ | n. | the art of creating and arranging ballets or dances | 舞蹈设计； 编舞 | Their MTV won so many awards not only because of the song but also because of the outstanding choreography. | 他们的 MTV 赢了这么多奖项不只是因为歌曲动听， 也因为编舞非常出色。 |
| 6 | circus | /ˈsəːkəs/ | n. | a traveling show of acrobats， clowns， trained animals， etc. | 马戏团 | His gymnastics routine looks more like a circus act than any rehearsed athletic feat. | 他的体操动作看来比较像是马戏团表演， 而不像排练过的体育技巧。 |
| 7 | concert | /ˈkɔnsət/ | n. | a public musical performance | 音乐会 | I have heard that they are putting on a benefit concert at the Kennedy Center on Saturday night to help fight the war against cancer. | 我听说， 他们将在星期六晚上在肯尼迪中心举办一场爱心音乐会， 来筹募对 抗癌症的基金。 |
| 8 | critic | /ˈkritik/ | n. | one who forms and expresses judgements， esp. of literary or artistic works； one who finds fault | 批评家； 评论家； 吹毛求疵的 人 | Despite the critic’s positive review of the movie， the people who have gone to see it don’t really like it. | 尽管影评人的评价是正面的， 但是去看过那部电影的人并不是那么喜欢它。 |
| 9 | curtain call | /ˈkəːtən ˌkɔːl/ | n. | the return of the actors to the stage after a performance to acknowledge the audience’s applause | 谢幕 | The actors gave such an outstanding performance that the enthused response from the crowd prompted not one but two curtain calls after the play. | 这些演员的表现非常精彩， 在表演结束之后， 观众热情的回应促使他们二度 出来谢幕。 |
| 10 | editorial | /ˌediˈtɔːriəl/ | n. | an article， as in a newspaper， expressing the opinion of its editors or publishers | 社论 | His specialty was writing scathing editorials that aggravate and anger everyone who reads them. | 他的专长是写能激怒每一个读者的措辞严厉的社论。 |
| 11 | finale | /fiˈnaːli/ | n. | the concluding part esp. of a musical composition | 结 局； 乐曲的最后部分 | The fireworks were spectacular， especially during the grand finale when they shot hundreds of fireworks non-stop for almost ten minutes straight. | 烟火非常壮观， 特别是当最后的压轴好戏出现时， 成千上万的烟火连续发 射几乎有10 分钟之久。 |
| 12 | media | /ˈmiːdiə/ | n. | all the means of mass communication （ | 大众传播） 媒 体 | I don’t think the media today all focused on bad things like killings and robberies instead of anything positive and uplifting. | 我并不认为今日的媒体只专注杀人、 抢劫之类的坏事， 而不报道任何正面或 振奋人心的事情。 |
| 13 | performance | /pəˈfɔːməns/ | n. | a presentation before an audience； the action or manner of carrying out an activity； the ability of a person or machine to do something well | 演出； 表现； 性能 | Michael Crawford’s performance in The Phantom of the Opera was widely acclaimed. | 迈克尔·克劳福德在 《歌剧魅影》 一剧中的表现广受好评。 |
| 14 | playwright | /ˈpleirait/ | n. | one who writes plays | 剧作家 | Neil Simon is one of the greatest playwrights on Broadway， whose works include hits The Odd Couple and The Sunshine Boys. | 尼尔·西蒙是百老汇最伟大的剧作家之一， 他的作品包括大受欢迎的 《怪夫 妻》 和 《阳光男孩》 等。 |
| 15 | preview | /ˈpriːvjuː/ | n. | an advance showing， as of a motion picture； a showing of scenes from a movie to advertise it | 试映； 预告片 | This Saturday night they are showing a sneak preview of MI2 at the Chinese Theatre in Hollywood. | 这个星期六晚上他们将会在好莱坞的中国戏院非公开试映 《碟中谍2》。 |
| 16 | rehearsal | /riˈhəːsəl/ | n. | the act or process of practicing， especially in preparation for a public performance | 预演； 排练 | It is common for the cast of a play to do a dress rehearsal before the opening of a show. | 通常戏剧的演员会在正式开演之前先作一场服装彩排。 |
| 17 | screen | /skriːn/ | n. | something that serves to divide， conceal， or protect， as a moveable room partition； the surface on which a picture is projected for viewing； the front glass surface of a television on which pictures appear | 隔板； 荧幕； 荧光屏 | During take-off， the plane’s projector was jostled， causing the image on the screen to distort and waver. | 在升空时， 飞机的发射器受到碰撞， 导致荧幕上的影像因而扭曲摇摆。 |
| 18 | stunt | /stʌnt/ | n. | an often dangerous act of skill； an action which gains attention， esp. in advertising | 特技； 噱头 | Some mountain climbers pulled the stunt of climbing up the Empire State Building only to find the police were waiting for them at the top. | 几个登山好手拿攀登帝国大厦当特技表演， 但是当他们到楼顶的时候， 却发 现警察就在那里等着他们。 |
| 19 | theater | /ˈθiətə/ | n. | a building for the presentation of motion pictures or dramatic performances | 戏院； 剧院 | One aspect I really don’t like about this theater is that the seats are awfully uncomfortable. | 我对这家电影院很不满意的一点是它们的椅子太不舒服。 |
| 20 | violin | /ˌvaiəˈlin/ | n. | any of the modern family of four-stringed musical instruments played with a bow， specifically the smallest and highest-pitched one of this family | 小提琴 | She plays the violin so well that she has been offered a job as the concertmaster of the Paris Symphony. | 因为她的小提琴拉得非常好， 巴黎交响乐团已经邀请她当首席小提琴手。 |

## Unit 16　Sports & Recreational Activities 运动及休闲活动

### Unit 16 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | aerobics | /ɛəˈrəubiks/ | n. | exercises that condition the heart and lungs by increasing efficient intake of oxygen by the body | 有氧运动 | They are starting a free aerobics class at the community recreation center during the afternoon for housewives who want to get more exercise but can’t find the time at night. | 他们将在下午的时间于社区活动中心为那些想要运动却无法在晚上抽出时间 的家庭主妇们开办一堂免费的有氧运动课。 |
| 2 | coach | /kəutʃ/ | n. | an instructor or trainer | 教师； 教练 | Our coach must be the most heartless man in the world because he made us run three miles outside in the freezing cold with no break. | 我们的教练一定是全世界最没有良心的人， 天寒地冻的， 他居然强迫我们到 外面连续跑了3 英里， 连口气都没得喘。 |
| 3 | compete | /kəmˈpiːt/ | vi. | to strive to outdo another or others in a contest | 竞 争； 比赛 | Whether it is in sports or grades， he loves to compete with others. | 不管是在运动方面或在成绩方面， 他都喜欢与别人竞争。 |
| 4 | defeat | /diˈfiːt/ | n. | （1） n. a defeating or being defeat vt. to win victory over | 失败； 击败 （2） 战胜； 战败 | In ancient Rome， when gladiators battled in the coliseum， defeat sometimes meant death. | 在古罗马时代， 斗士们在竞技场上搏斗时， 失败有时就意味着死亡。 |
| 5 | fitness | /ˈfitnis/ | n. | the quality of being suitable； the state of being physically fit | 适合； 恰当； 身体健康 | In the health conscious Nineties， physical fitness was a big concern. | 在健康意识抬头的90 年代， 身体健康是人们关注的重点。 |
| 6 | gym | /dʒim/ | n. | （short for “gymnasium”） a room or building equipped for physical training and sports | 健身房； 体育馆 | I’m going to the gym tonight to work out and swim. | 我今晚要到体育馆做健身运动并游个泳。 |
| 7 | league | /liːg/ | n. | a group of teams organized to play one another； an association of nations， groups， etc. for promoting common interest | 联 盟； 同盟 | The league has once again emphasized that using illicit drugs is strictly prohibited and will result in penalties， including being banned permanently. | 联盟再一次强调使用非法药品是绝对禁止的， 使用者将遭处罚， 包括永远禁 止参赛。 |
| 8 | marathon | /ˈmærəθən/ | n. | a race on foot， 26 miles and 385 yards in length； any endurance contest | 马拉松； 耐力比赛 | He is in such good shape because he runs marathons almost on a weekly basis. | 因为他几乎每个星期都跑马拉松， 所以他的体能状态好得不得了。 |
| 9 | mountaineering | /ˌmauntiˈniəriŋ/ | n. | the sport of climbing mountains | 登山 运动 | Jeff is much more into mountaineering than I am， even though we both live by the Alps. | 虽然我们都住在阿尔卑斯山旁， 但杰夫比我还要热衷于爬山。 |
| 10 | participate | /paːˈtisipeit/ | vi. | to take part or have a share in an activity or event | 参与； 参加 | Joseph really needs to participate more in the other kids’ activities to make friends. | 乔瑟夫真的必须更积极参与其他孩童的活动才能交到朋友。 |
| 11 | recreation | /ˌrekriˈeiʃən/ | n. | （a form of）amusement and enjoyment； way of spending free time | 娱乐； 消遣 | Lately I’ve been volunteering as a basketball coach for middle school students at the recreation center. | 最近我自愿在活动中心担任中学生的篮球教练。 |
| 12 | relaxation | /ˌriːlækˈseiʃən/ | n. | the act of making or becoming less stiff or severe； rest and amusement | 松弛； 缓和； 休息； 娱乐 | Bungee jumping and sky diving are not my ideas of relaxation. | 高空弹跳和高空跳伞对我而言并不是所谓的 “放松” 活动。 |
| 13 | rigor | /ˈrigə/ | n. | strictness or severity； a trying condition； hardship | 严 格； 严苛； 艰苦 | He enjoyed the rigor of marathons and that was why he won the race. | 他喜欢马拉松赛跑的严苛考验， 那也是他为什么会赢得这个比赛的原因。 |
| 14 | soccer | /ˈsɔkə/ | n. | a game in which two11-member teams seek to propel the ball into the opposing team’s goal by kicking it or by using any part of the body except the arms and hands | 足球 | Soccer is relatively new to Asia； in Europe it is the hottest sport. | 相对而言， 足球对亚洲来说是新兴运动， 在欧洲它却是最受欢迎的运动。 |
| 15 | stadium | /ˈsteidiəm/ | n. | a large structure as for football or baseball， with tiers of seats for spectators | 体育场 | They are going to build a new stadium and will name it after the president. | 他们将盖一座新体育场， 并以总统的名字命名。 |
| 16 | stroll | /strəul/ | vt./vi. | to walk at a leisurely pace | 散步； 闲逛 | It’s a beautiful night； let’s stroll through the park. | 今晚夜色很美， 我们到公园走走吧。 |
| 17 | surrender | /səˈrendə/ | vt./vi. | to give up or give in to the power （esp. of an enemy）， as a sign of defeat； to give up possession or control of （ | 向敌人） 投降； 放弃 | He is so determined to win that he will never surrender or give up as long as there is still hope. | 他下定决心追求胜利， 只要还有希望， 就绝对不会投降或放弃。 |
| 18 | time-out | /ˈtaimˈaut/ | n. | any temporary suspension of play during a game | 暂停 | Down by two points with a few seconds left on the clock， the coach decided to call a time-out. | 在终场前几秒仍然落后两分， 教练决定叫暂停。 |
| 19 | trophy | /ˈtrəufi/ | n. | a prize given for winning a race， competition， etc.， esp. a cup； something taken or gained after much effort， esp. in war or hunting | 奖品； 奖杯； 战利品 | His room is filled with the trophies that he has won over the years. | 他的房间里摆满了多年来获得的奖杯。 |
| 20 | usher | /ˈʌʃə/ | n. | one who shows people to their seats in a theater， church， etc. | 引座员； 招待员 | You follow the usher to our seats， and I will go get some drinks and popcorn. | 你跟着领位员到我们的位子， 我去买些饮料和爆米花。 |

### Unit 16 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | billiards | /ˈbiljədz/ | n. | a game played with hard balls moved by a cue on a table with raised， cushioned edges | 撞球 | A game of pool is called billiards in England. “ | 撞球” 在英国叫做 “桌球”。 |
