| 2 | combative | /ˈkɔmbətiv/ | adj. | ready and eager to fight or argue | 好战的； 好 斗的 | What makes him such a good football player is his unrelenting combative nature. | 使他成为一个好足球队员的原因正是他冷酷无情的好战天性。 |
| 3 | default | /diˈfɔːlt/ | n. | （1） n. failure to perform a duty； failure to appear vt. /vi. to fail to fulfill a contract， duty， etc | 违约； 弃权 （2） 不履行 （契约书） | If John doesn’t show up soon， he is going to lose the competition by default. | 如果约翰不赶快出现， 他将会被视为弃权而输掉比赛。 |
| 4 | defense | /diˈfens/ | n. | the act or process of guarding or protecting against attack | 保护； 防卫 | In American football， just like in all other ball games， the defense is as important as the offense. | 美式足球就像其他所有球类比赛一样， 防守和进攻同等重要。 |
| 5 | forfeit | /ˈfɔːfit/ | n. | （1 ） n. a fine or penalty； what must be lost because some agreement or rule has been broken vt. to lose or be deprived of | 罚款； 丧失 （2） 丧失； 被没收 | Upon seeing that our basketball team didn’t have enough players to fill the lineup， the referee declared the game a forfeit. | 看到我们的篮球队没有足够的球员下场， 裁判判定我们丧失比赛资格。 |
| 6 | intensity | /inˈtensiti/ | n. | great energy， strength， concentration， etc. as of activity， thought， or feeling | 强度； 强烈 | Although off the court he is a really nice guy， Karl Malone plays with an intensity that often intimidates other players. | 虽然在球场外他是个大好人， 可打起球来卡尔·马龙经常是威势惊人。 |
| 7 | leisure | /ˈleʒə/ | n. | time when one is free from work or duties； free time | 空 闲； 闲暇时间 | In my leisure time， I like to work out in the gym and then come home and sleep. | 有空闲时， 我喜欢去健身房做运动， 然后回家大睡一觉。 |
| 8 | massage | /ˈmæsaːʒ/ | n. | （an act of） pressing and rubbing parts of the body to aid circulation or to relax the muscles | 按摩； 揉捏 | A nice massage can help you relax. | 舒适的按摩可以帮助你放松。 |
| 9 | muscle | /ˈmʌsl/ | n. | a tissue composed of fibers that can be contracted and expanded to produce bodily movement； strength or power | 肌肉； 力量 | I ran so many miles yesterday that the muscles in my legs are killing me now. | 我昨天跑了好几英里， 所以现在腿部肌肉疼痛不已。 |
| 10 | pastime | /ˈpaːstaim/ | n. | something done to pass one’s time in a pleasant way | 娱乐； 消遣 | His only pastime is sitting at home and reading books. | 他的唯一消遣是坐在家里看书。 |
| 11 | referee | /ˌrefəˈriː/ | n. | an official who enforces the rules in certain sports contests； a person who is asked to settle a disagreement | 裁判员； 仲裁 者 | They would have won the game if the referee had been fair enough. | 如果当时裁判够公正的话， 他们就会赢得那场比赛。 |
| 12 | replacement | /riˈpleismənt/ | n. | someone or something that takes the place of another； substitution | 代替的人或物 | Now that Rick is injured， we will have to find a suitable replacement for him. | 既然瑞克受了伤， 我们就得找到一个合适的人来替换他。 |
| 13 | runner-up | /ˈrʌnəˈʌp/ | n. | a person or team that finishes second in a contest | 第二名； 亚军 | He was the runner-up in that rigorous marathon race. | 在那场严峻的马拉松比赛中， 他赢得了亚军。 |
| 14 | spectator | /spekˈteitə/ | n. | a person who watches an event or sport without taking part | 观众； 旁观者 | As a spectator only， Michael Jordan has a hard time watching the Chicago Bulls play. | 身为观众不能下场， 只能看着芝加哥公牛队打球， 迈克尔·乔丹简直坐立 难安。 |
| 15 | stretch | /stretʃ/ | vt./vi. | to reach out； to draw out to full extent； to extend the body or limbs to full length | 伸张； 伸开； 伸展 | The best way to prepare for a competition is a warm-up by stretching out all of your muscles. | 为比赛做准备的最好方式就是伸展你的全身肌肉做做暖身运动。 |
| 16 | surfing | /ˈsəːfiŋ/ | n. | the sport of riding in toward shore on the crest of a wave esp. on a surfboard | 冲浪 | I love surfing in Hawaii because the waves are high and the water is warm. | 我喜欢在夏威夷冲浪， 因为浪头很高， 且海水很温暖。 |
| 17 | tactic | /ˈtæktik/ | n. | （often pl. ） a plan or method that is intended to gain a desired result （ | 常用复数） 战略； 策略 | The loss today makes me question the coach’s tactics. | 今天的失败让我怀疑教练的战略技巧。 |
| 18 | tournament | /ˈtuənəmənt/ | n. | a series of contests in competition for a championship | 比赛； 锦标赛 | The historic tennis tournament at Wimbledon is played on courts of grass. | 历史悠久的温布尔登网球赛是在草皮场地上打的。 |
| 19 | umpire | /ˈʌmpaiə/ | n. | an official who administers the rules in certain sports； a person chosen to judge a dispute | 裁判； 仲裁 | On the baseball field， whatever the umpire says stands and there is no use arguing. | 在棒球场上， 不管裁判说什么都成立， 怎么抗议都是无效的。 |
| 20 | volleyball | /ˈvɔlibɔːl/ | n. | a team game played by hitting a ball back and forth over a net with hands while attempting to score by grounding the ball on the opposing team’s court | 排球 | Our high school’s girl volleyball team was ranked number one in the nation last year. | 我们的高中女子排球队去年是全国第一名。 |

## Unit 17　Medicine & Insurance 医疗及保险

### Unit 17 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | alleviate | /əˈliːvieit/ | vt. | to reduce pain， suffering， etc.； to lessen or relieve | 减轻； 缓和 | Aspirin helps to alleviate the pain brought on by headaches. | 阿司匹林可以帮助减轻头痛带来的痛苦。 |
| 2 | bacteria | /bækˈtiəriə/ | n. | （plural of bacterium） very small， sometimes parasitic， unicellular organisms， some of which cause disease | 细菌 | When visiting foreign countries， be sure to filter or boil the water to avoid ingesting bacteria that could make you sick. | 到外国玩的时候， 一定要将水过滤或煮沸， 以免吃进致病细菌。 |
| 3 | collision | /kəˈliʒən/ | n. | the act of crashing； a clash or conflict | 碰撞； 抵触； 冲突 | Princess Diana was killed in an automobile collision in France. | 戴安娜王妃在法国因撞车丧生。 |
| 4 | compensation | /ˌkɔmpənˈseiʃən/ | n. | something （esp. money） given as a way of payment for some lost， damage， etc.； recompense | 补偿； 赔 偿； 报偿 | The accident left him confined to a wheel chair for the rest of his life； I don’t think any monetary compensation can make up for it. | 那场意外使他终身必须坐轮椅， 我觉得没有任何金钱赔偿能弥补他的损失。 |
| 5 | contagious | /kənˈteidʒəs/ | adj. | （of a disease） that can be passed from one person to another by touch | 接触传染的 | His illness is severe， but the doctor assured us that it is not contagious. | 他的病很严重， 但是医生跟我们保证， 这病是没有传染性的。 |
| 6 | crash | /kræʃ/ | n. | a violent vehicle accident； a collision； a sudden loud noise， as of something being violently smashed or struck | 相撞； 碰撞； 突 然发出的巨响 （如打碎、 撞击等） | Because of the crash of her friend’s plane into the mountain， she said she will never fly again. | 因为她的朋友坐的飞机撞山， 她说她将永远不再坐飞机。 |
| 7 | emergency room | /iˈməːdʒənsi ruːm/ | n. | a hospital area equipped and staffed for the prompt treatment of acute illness， trauma， etc. | 急诊室 | The emergency room is always crowded during the New Year holidays because even people with simple colds would come here for help. | 春节期间急诊室总是挤满了人， 因为连只是患普通感冒的人都会到这里求助。 |
| 8 | expiration date | /ˌekspaiəˈreiʃən deit/ | n. | the last date that a product， as food， should be used before it is considered spoiled or ineffective， usually specified on the label or package | 有效期限 | Check the expiration date before you drink that milk to make sure it hasn’t gone bad yet. | 在你喝牛奶之前， 查一下有效日期看它坏了没有。 |
| 9 | general hospital | /ˈdʒenərəl ˈhɔspitl/ | n. | a hospital not limited to any special field of medicine | 综合医院 | Elise decided not to go to the general hospital because her illness required a specialist. | 伊利丝决定不去综合医院， 因为她的病需要看专科医生。 |
| 10 | immune | /iˈmjuːn/ | adj. | exempt from or protected against； resistant to a specific disease | 免除……的； 豁免的； 免疫的 | If a child is injected with the polio vaccination， he / she will hopefully become immune to the disease. | 如果一个小孩注射了小儿麻痹疫苗， 他/ 她将可望对此疾病免疫。 |
| 11 | infection | /inˈfekʃən/ | n. | invasion of a bodily part by pathogenic microorganisms | 感染 | Because of the infection in your kidney， you have to take these antibiotics and drink lots of water. | 因为你的肾脏受到了感染， 你必须服用这些抗生素并多喝水。 |
| 12 | investigator | /inˈvestigeitə/ | n. | one who tries to find out more information about something | 调查员 | An investigator has been sent to make sure that the claim is legitimate and that there is no possibility of fraud. | 一个调查员已被派来确定这个请求合法并且没有诈骗之可能。 |
| 13 | mandatory | /ˈmændətəri/ | adj. | authoritatively ordered； compulsory； obligatory | 命令的； 强制的； 义务的 | In order to go through the application process at many universities， an SAT score is mandatory. | 要通过许多大学里的申请， SAT 成绩是必备的。 |
| 14 | option | /ˈɔpʃən/ | n. | the right of choosing； freedom to choose something available as a choice | 选择权； 选择之自由； 可选择的东西 | From the things he has said， I don’t think he has left us any other option but to sue him for the damages done. | 照他所说的话来看， 我认为除了控告他所造成的伤害之外， 我们别无选择。 |
| 15 | outpatient | /autˈpeiʃənt/ | n. | a patient who receives treatment at a hospital without being hospitalized | 门诊病人 | The surgery to repair my broken arm was done on an outpatient basis and I left the hospital three hours after the start of the operation. | 接合我的断臂的手术是以门诊的方式进行的， 我在手术开始后3 个小时就离 开医院了。 |
| 16 | premium | /ˈprimjəm/ | n. | a reward or prize； an additional amount paid or charged； an amount paid， often in installments， for an insurance policy | 奖品； 额外费用； 保费 | Every time you get a ticket or are involved in a car accident， your insurance premium goes up. | 每次你收到罚单或出车祸， 你的保费都会提高。 |
| 17 | severe | /siˈviə/ | adj. | rigidly accurate and demanding； unsparing and harsh in treating others； serious； grave； intense | 严苛的； 严格的； 严重的 | His fall was so severe that he went unconscious for a long time. | 他摔得非常重， 因此昏厥过去很久一段时间。 |
| 18 | stroke | /strəuk/ | n. | a sudden loss of consciousness with paralysis， caused by a cerebral hemorrhage， blood clot， etc.； apoplexy | 脑血管 破裂或阻塞； 中风 | Because both his father and grandfather suffered serious strokes， he is afraid that he might have one， too. | 因为他的父亲和祖父都有严重中风， 他很担心自己也可能会中风。 |
| 19 | terms | /təːmz/ | n. | the conditions of an agreement， contract， etc.； the stated conditions concerning payment， prices， etc. | 条文； 条款； 付款 条件； 价钱 | If you agree to the terms outlined in the contract， please sign below on the dotted line. | 如果你同意合约上的条文， 请在下方虚线上签名。 |
| 20 | vital | /ˈvaitl/ | adj. | very necessary； of the greatest importance； essential to life； full of life； crucial； energetic； crucial | 非常必要的； 极其重要的； 生死攸关的； 生气勃勃的 | The patient went into a cardiac arrest and his vital signs began to drop. | 病人的心脏突然停止跳动， 而且生命迹象开始消失。 |

### Unit 17 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | antibiotic | /ˌæntibaiˈɔtik/ | n. | any of various substances， such as penicillin and streptomycin， produced by certain fungi， bacteria， and other organisms， that inhibit the growth of or destroy microorganisms and are widely used to prevent and treat diseases | 抗生素 | The antibiotic penicillin has been used to fight various bacterial infections， such as a staph infection. | 抗生素盘尼西林已被用来对抗各种细菌感染， 如葡萄球菌感染。 |
| 2 | claim | /kleim/ | n. | demand or request for something which one has a right to have； a statement that something is true or real | 要求； 声称 | It was two weeks after the accident before he filed a claim for damages to his car. | 在车祸发生后两个星期他才提出车子损坏的赔偿要求。 |
| 3 | commission | /kəˈmiʃən/ | n. | money paid to a salesman or agent for services rendered | 佣金 | Car salesmen work on commission and thus act as if they were your best friends in order to sell you a car. | 车辆推销员靠佣金赚钱， 所以他们表现得好像是你最好的朋友， 为的是把车 子卖给你。 |
| 4 | comprehensive | /ˌkɔmpriˈhensiv/ | adj. | including a lot or everything； large in scope or content | 全面的； 广泛的 | They are going to do a comprehensive test to determine the exact cause of his illness. | 他们将做一次全面的检查， 以便判断导致他患病的确切原因。 |
| 5 | coverage | /ˈkʌvəridʒ/ | n. | the extent or degree to which something is observed， analyzed， and reported； the amount of protection given by insurance | 报道范围； 保险范围 | I don’t know if the coverage of my insurance includes as much as that of yours. | 我不知道我的保险涵盖的项目是不是跟你的保险一样多。 |
| 6 | diagnose | /ˈdaiəgnəuz/ | vt./vi. | to discover the nature of a disease or fault by making a careful examination | 诊断 （疾病）； 判断 （问题） | Because the symptoms resemble many different illnesses， it is hard to diagnose what exactly is wrong with him at this point. | 因为他的症状类似很多不同的疾病， 目前很难诊断出他究竟患的是什么病。 |
| 7 | excruciating | /ikˈskruːʃieitiŋ/ | adj. | intensely painful or distressing； intense or extreme | 极痛苦的； 极度的 | He said that after he had his surgery， he had the most excruciating pain that he had ever felt in his entire life. | 他说， 在手术后他所感受到的痛苦是他一辈子没有经历过的。 |
| 8 | fraud | /frɔːd/ | n. | deliberate deception for unfair or unlawful gain； a swindle or trick； an imposter | 诈骗； 骗局； 骗子 | Many people get caught and arrested for insurance fraud. | 有很多人因诈骗保险金被逮捕。 |
| 9 | handicap | /ˈhændikæp/ | n. | a physiological or mental deficiency that prevents or restricts normal achievement； any condition or situation likely to cause disadvantage or difficulty； any hindrance | 残障； 不利条件； 障碍 | When applying for a driver’s license， wearing eyeglasses or corrective lenses of any kind is considered a type of handicap. | 申请驾照时， 戴眼镜或任何矫正视力的镜片都被视为一种残障。 |
| 10 | indemnity | /inˈdemniti/ | n. | insurance against damage or loss； repayment for damage or loss | 损害保障； 损害赔偿 | In accordance with the 1871 Treaty of Versailles， France was forced to pay a large indemnity and accept German occupation. | 根据1871 年的 《凡尔赛条约》， 法国被迫付大笔的损害赔偿金并接受德国 的占领。 |
| 11 | intensive care unit | /inˈtensiv ˈkɛə ˌjuːnit/ | n. | （ = ICU） a hospital unit where very attentive care is given to patients right after surgery | 加护 病房 | After the severe heart attack， Jerry was in the intensive care unit for two days fighting for his life. | 那次严重的心脏病发作后， 杰瑞在加护病房待了两天， 跟死神搏斗。 |
| 12 | liable | /laiəbl/ | adj. | legally bound or responsible； likely to ； subject to | 有义务的； 应负责的； 易于……的； 倾向于……的 | The insurance company found the driver liable for the damage done to his neighbor’s car. | 保险公司发现， 这个司机必须对邻居车子的损害负责。 |
| 13 | nutrition | /njuːˈtriʃən/ | n. | the process of giving or getting food； anything that gives what is needed in order to live， grow， and stay healthy | 营养 之供给或摄取； 营养 | Proper nutrition can prevent illnesses and give you more energy. | 适当的营养可以预防疾病， 并带给你更多活力。 |
| 14 | organ donor | /ˈɔːgən ˌdəunə/ | n. | one who gives part of his/her body to be put into someone else for medical purposes | 器官捐赠者 | You can indicate on this card whether or not you want to be an organ donor after you die. | 你可以在这张卡片上注明你是否愿意在死后捐出器官。 |
| 15 | pharmaceutical | /ˌfaːməˈsjuːtikəl/ | adj. | connected with （the making of） medicine | 制药的； 药品的 | We will be receiving a new shipment of Tylenol from the pharmaceutical company at the end of the week. | 我们将会在这个周末收到从药品公司寄来的一批新的 Tylenol （止痛药名）。 |
| 16 | purification | /ˌpjuərifiˈkeiʃən/ | n. | the act or process of making pure | 洗 净； 净化 | Water purification is one way to protect people from harmful bacteria. | 净化水质是保证人们不受有害细菌伤害的一种方法。 |
| 17 | side effect | /said iˈfekt/ | n. | any effect of a drug， chemical， or other medicine that is in addition to its intended effect， esp. an effect that is harmful or unpleasant； any accompanying or consequential and usually detrimental effect （ | 药物等之） 副作用 | His organ transplant went smoothly， but we still need to wait and see if there are going to be any side effects. | 他的器官移植手术非常成功， 但是我们仍需要等待一段时间才能知道是否会 有任何副作用产生。 |
| 18 | surgery | /ˈsəːdʒəri/ | n. | the treatment of disease， injury， etc. by manual or instrumental operations （ | 外科） 手术 | The technology and equipment used to perform surgery today are much more advanced than those of a few decades ago. | 今日外科手术所使用的科技和设备比几十年前进步多了。 |
| 19 | trauma | /ˈtrɔːmə/ | n. | a bodily injury； an emotional shock， often having a lasting psychic effect | 外伤； 心灵的伤 | The worse part of the whole accident was the emotional trauma that it placed on the two people and their families. | 整件意外最糟的部分是这两个人和他们家人所承受的心灵创伤。 |
| 20 | waive | /weiv/ | vt. | to give up （a right， legal claim， etc.） | 放弃； 弃权 | You need to sign here if you want to waive your right. | 如果你愿意放弃你的权利， 你必须在这里签名。 |

## Unit 18　Housing 住房

### Unit 18 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | appliance | /əˈplaiəns/ | n. | an apparatus， instrument， or tool for a particular purpose， esp. an electrical machine that is used in the house | 设备； 器具； 工具； 家用电器 | Gil was glad that he and Sheri were given so many kitchen appliances as wedding gifts. | 吉尔很高兴他和雪丽收到这么多厨房家用电器当结婚礼物。 |
| 2 | burglar | /ˈbəːglə/ | n. | a thief who breaks into houses， shops， etc. with the intention of stealing， esp. during the night | 窃贼； 夜间入屋行窃的小偷 | Last night the police caught the burglar who broke into all of our apartments. | 警察昨晚逮捕到偷遍了我们附近所有公寓的窃贼。 |
| 3 | community | /kəˈmjuːniti/ | n. | a group of people living together and/or united by shared interests， religion， nationality， etc. | 社区 | The main reason we moved here is that this community seems to be a nice place and the neighbors are friendly. | 我们搬到这里最主要的原因是这个社区似乎是一个很美好的地方， 邻居们都 很和善。 |
| 4 | electronics | /iˌlekˈtrɔniks/ | n. | the science dealing with the development and application of devices and systems involving the flow of electrons in a vacuum， in gaseous media， and in semiconductors | 电子学 | Thanks to the progress made in electronics， now every household in America has so much convenient equipment. | 由于在电子学方面的进步， 如今美国的每个家庭都拥有许多的电器用品。 |
| 5 | fire extinguisher | /ˈfaiə ikˈstiŋgwiʃə/ | n. | a smallish metal container with water or chemicals inside for putting out a fire | 灭火器 | I just bought two new fire extinguishers ， one for the kitchen and one for the garage. | 我刚买了两个新的灭火器， 一个厨房用， 一个车库用。 |
| 6 | furniture | /ˈfəːnitʃə/ | n. | moveable articles such as beds， chairs， tables， etc. that are placed in a house | 家具 | Your new furniture makes the ambience of the room so different. | 你的新家具使房间的气氛大为改观。 |
| 7 | garbage disposal | /ˈgaːbidʒ disˈpəuzəl/ | n. | an electrical device in the drain of a sink， for grinding up garbage to be washed down the drain | 垃 圾处理机 | When we bought this house， we had a garbage disposal installed in order to reduce the amount of garbage. | 当我们买这栋房子时， 我们就安装了垃圾处理机以减少垃圾量。 |
| 8 | landlord | /ˈlændlɔːd/ | n. | one who owns and leases land， house， etc. to others | 地主； 房东 | If you’re not happy with the leaking tap， just call the landlord and have him fix it. | 如果你不满意水龙头漏水， 就打电话给房东， 要他来修。 |
| 9 | laundry | /ˈlɔːndri/ | n. | a place or business where clothes， etc. are washed and ironed； clothes， etc. that need to be washed or have been washed and ironed | 洗衣店； 要洗或已经洗好的衣物 | Why is there dirty laundry all over your bedroom floor every time I come in here？ | 为什么每次我到这儿来都会看到你卧室地板上到处堆满了脏衣服呢？ |
| 10 | lease | /liːs/ | n. | （1 ） n. a contract by which a landlord rents land， buildings， etc. to others for a specified time vt. to give or take the use of on a lease； to rent or hire | 租约 （2） 出租； 租用 | You can lease a new car if you don’t have the budget to purchase one. | 如果你没有预算金额可以买的话， 你可以去租一辆新车。 |
| 11 | mattress | /ˈmætris/ | n. | a pad of heavy cloth filled with soft material， used on a bed | 床垫 | It’s important to select a mattress that provides excellent back support. | 选择能提供背部最佳支撑的床垫非常重要。 |
| 12 | mortgage | /ˈmɔːgidʒ/ | n. | a legal pledge of property to a creditor as security for the payment of a loan or debt | 抵押贷 （借） 款 | Our main expense each month is the mortgage payment that exceeds $1， 500 a month. | 我们每个月最大的一笔支出就是超过1500 美金的房子抵押贷款。 |
| 13 | ornament | /ˈɔːnəmənt/ | n. | something that is added to make something else more beautiful； decoration； embellishment | 装饰 （品） | Why don’t you and the children hang the ornaments on the Christmas tree while I cook dinner？ | 你和孩子们何不在我做晚餐时把装饰品挂到圣诞树上呢？ |
| 14 | plumbing | /ˈplʌmiŋ/ | n. | the pipes and water supply system in a building | 水管设施 | The plumbing in the house has many problems， such as clogged drains and leaking taps. | 家里的水管设施有很多问题， 例如堵塞的排水管和漏水的水龙头。 |
| 15 | possession | /pəˈzeʃən/ | n. | the state of having， holding， or owning something； a piece of personal property | 所有； 个人财产 | I put all of my valuable possessions in the safe， which is hidden behind the books in the bedroom. | 我把所有贵重的物品都放在保险箱里， 而保险箱则藏在卧室图书的后面。 |
| 16 | real estate | /riːl iˈsteit/ | n. | property in the form of land and houses | 不 动产 | Though the local market had its share of fluctuation， lately real estate prices were high and holding. | 虽然当地市场有所波动， 但最近不动产的价格却居高不下。 |
| 17 | renovate | /ˈrenəveit/ | vt. | to put back into good condition by repairing， rebuilding， etc.； to restore | 更新； 修复； 恢复 | Jan loved the idea of buying the old barn， renovating it， and turning it into a modern home. | 简非常喜欢这个将老旧的谷仓买下来， 重新装修， 然后把它变成一所现代化 的房子的主意。 |
| 18 | rural | /ˈrurəl/ | adj. | of or pertaining to the country， as opposed to the city | 乡下的； 田园的 | Since I have lived in New York for most of my life， it is going to take me a little while to get used to rural life here on the farm. | 因为我已经在纽约度过了大半辈子， 要适应此地农场上的乡村生活将需要一 段时间。 |
| 19 | suburb | /ˈsʌbəːb/ | n. | （often pl. ） an outer area of a city or town， where people live （ | 常用复数） 郊区； 近郊 | Nowadays more and more people are moving into the suburbs， where there is less pollution. | 现今越来越多的人搬到郊区， 因为那儿污染较少。 |
| 20 | urban | /ˈəːbən/ | adj. | of， in， or constituting a city； characteristic of cities | 都市的； 都市式的 | Since relocating from Idaho to New York， he has been struggling to get accustomed to the fast-paced urban lifestyle. | 从爱达荷州搬到纽约后， 他一直很努力地去适应快节奏的都市生活方式。 |

### Unit 18 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | balcony | /ˈbælkəni/ | n. | a platform that projects from the wall of a building and is surrounded by a railing ； an upper floor of seats in a theater， often projecting over the main floor | 阳台； 包厢 | Please watch that small child so he doesn’t fall from the balcony. | 请你看着那个小孩， 以免他从阳台上跌下去。 |
| 2 | chore | /tʃɔː/ | n. | a daily or routine task in a house； a piece of uninteresting， difficult， or unpleasant work | 家务难事； 困难、 烦琐、 无趣 的工作 | It seems that as the children grow older， it becomes more of a chore to keep this house clean. | 好像随着小孩越长越大， 使这个家保持干净的工作就越来越繁重。 |
| 3 | down payment | /ˌdaun ˈpeimənt/ | n. | a part of the full price paid at the time of buying something， with the rest to be paid later； advance payment； deposit | 头期款； 预付金； 订金 | With all the money we’ve saved， we are finally able to make a down payment on a new house. | 有了存下来的钱， 我们终于可以付新房子的头期款了。 |
| 4 | fire alarm | /ˈfaiə əˈlaːm/ | n. | an apparatus ， such as a bell， that gives a signal to warn people of fire | 火灾警报器 | I feel much safer now that we have installed a new fire alarm in the house. | 在屋内装了新的火灾警报器之后， 我觉得安心多了。 |
| 5 | furnishings | /ˈfəːniʃiŋz/ | n. | articles of furniture or other articles， like curtains， etc. in a room | 家具； 室内陈设 | The apartment can be rented by the month and it is complete with TV， beds and all the other furnishings. | 这间公寓可以包月承租， 且电视、 床铺以及其他家具一应俱全。 |
| 6 | garage | /ˈgæraːʒ/ | n. | a building where motor vehicles can be kept； a commercial establishment where motor vehicles are repaired and serviced | 车库； 修车厂 | The right taillight of your car is broken； you should take the car down to the garage and have it fixed. | 你车子右边的车尾灯坏了， 你该开车到修车厂把它修一下。 |
| 7 | kitchenware | /ˈkitʃinwɛə/ | n. | cooking equipment or utensils | 厨房用具 | My kitchenware is old； I am looking forward to replacing it. | 我的厨房用具旧了， 我很期待可以买新的。 |
| 8 | laundromat | /ˈlaːndrəˌmæt/ | n. | self-service or automatic laundry facility | 自 助洗衣店 | Our apartment does not have washer and a dryer， so we have to use the laundromat down the street. | 我们的公寓没有洗衣机和烘干机， 所以我们得到街上的自助洗衣店去洗 衣服。 |
| 9 | lawn | /lɔːn/ | n. | a stretch of grass covered land next to a house | 草坪； 草地 | You won’t be allowed to go out and play until you mow the lawn. | 除非你把草坪修剪好， 否则就不准出去玩。 |
| 10 | loan shark | /ˈləunˌʃaːk/ | n. | one who lends money at illegal rates of interest | 放高利贷者 | When you go down to Vegas， be very wary of those loan sharks and don’t get yourself into more trouble than you can possibly handle. | 当你到拉斯维加斯时， 小心那些放高利贷的人， 不要让自己惹上不能处理的 麻烦。 |
| 11 | messy | /ˈmesi/ | adj. | needing a lot of cleaning up； untidy； dirty； disordered | 杂乱的； 肮脏的 | I clean the house twice a day， but with three children it still gets messy. | 我每天打扫家里两次， 但有3 个小孩在， 它还是一样杂乱。 |
| 12 | neighborhood | /ˈneibəhud/ | n. | a group of people and their houses forming a small area within a larger place； the area around a point or place | 街坊； 住宅区； 临近地区 | We like to have picnics and other activities each month with the other people in the neighborhood to get to know them better and allow our children to meet new friends. | 我们喜欢每个月和街坊邻居举办野餐和其他活动， 以便更好地了解他们， 也 可以让我们的小孩认识新朋友。 |
| 13 | playground | /ˈpleigraund/ | n. | a place， often at or near a school， for outdoor play or recreation； any place， environment or facility used for recreation or amusement | 运动场； 游戏场； 游乐场 | There is a nice playground near our house， which is rather safe since many families are there throughout the day. | 在我们家附近有一个游乐场， 由于整天都有许多家庭在那个地方玩， 所以 蛮安全的。 |
| 14 | porch | /pɔtʃ/ | n. | a roofed entrance built out from a house； an open area with a floor and a roof at the side of a house | 门廊； 游廊 | Why don’t you go sit out on the porch and watch the fire flies while we clean up the dishes？ | 你何不趁着我们清洗碗盘时到游廊上坐坐， 看看萤火虫呢？ |
| 15 | property | /ˈprɔpəti/ | n. | something， especially land， owned by someone | 财 产； 地产 | Every time they climb over the fence and cut through our yard， they are trespassing on our property. | 每次他们爬过围篱穿过我们的院子时， 都是在侵入私人所有地。 |
| 16 | relocate | /riːˈləukeit/ | vt./vi. | to move to a different place | 重新安置； 迁徙 | My family decided to relocate to Texas while I was overseas， so I don’t really know anyone in the neighborhood. | 我在国外期间， 家人决定搬到得克萨斯州， 所以街坊邻居我都不熟。 |
| 17 | residence | /ˈrezidəns/ | n. | the place where one lives； a house， esp. a large important one ； living in a place | 住所； 公馆； 居住 | His permanent residence is here， but we hardly ever see him because he is away at college. | 他的户籍地址在这里， 但因为他一直都住在大学里， 所以我们很少看到他。 |
| 18 | storage | /ˈstɔridʒ/ | n. | the act of keeping a supply of something for future use；a place where things are kept | 储藏； 藏室 | Though the house was beautiful， the storage space in the attic was nowhere near adequate. | 虽然这房子很漂亮， 但是阁楼上的储藏室的空间根本不够。 |
| 19 | tenant | /ˈtenənt/ | n. | a person who pays rent for the use of a room， building， land， etc. | 房客； 承租人； 佃户 | We should talk to the tenant living on the fifth floor； he is always making noise. | 我们应该跟住5 楼的房客谈谈， 他老是制造一些噪音。 |
| 20 | utility | /juːˈtiliti/ | n. | a useful service for the public， such as supplies of water or electricity to the home， the bus service， the telephone service， etc.； the degree of usefulness | 公共事业； 实用 | They probably shut off the electricity because you have failed to pay the utility bill for three months in a row. | 因为你已经连续3 个月没有付水电费， 所以他们可能把电力切断了。 |

## Unit 19　Product Research and Development 产品调研与开发

### Unit 19 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | adaptability | /əˌdæptəˈbiləti/ | n. | the ability to change so as to be suitable for new needs， different conditions， etc. | 顺应性； 可用性； 灵活性； 适 应性 | The adaptability of youth to new surroundings is one of their good qualities. | 年轻人适应新环境的能力是他们的一种优点。 |
| 2 | analysis | /əˈnælisis/ | n. | examination of something by dividing it into its separate parts | 分解； 分析 | Our analysis shows that the company’s failure was caused by lack of investment. | 我们的研究表明， 该公司之所以失利， 是由于投资不足。 |
| 3 | bandwagon effect | /ˈbændˌwægən iˈfekt/ | n. | the observation that people often do （or believe） things because many other people do （or believe） the same； the phenomenon of a popular trend attracting even greater popularity | 花 车效应； 攀比效应； 跟风效应 | In periods of high merger activity there is a bandwagon effect with more and more firms seeking to engage in takeover activity. | 当合并非常活跃时， 会出现一种跟风效应， 使越来越多的企业寻求参与并购 活动。 |
| 4 | classify | /ˈklæsifai/ | vt. | to arrange data into classes or groups according to a definite plan or method | 把……分类； 分为同一等级 | The secretary will classify these two documents into different groups. | 秘书会把这两份文件进行分类， 归到不同的组别。 |
| 5 | collect | /kəˈlekt/ | vt. | to come or bring together in one place so as to form a group or mass | 收集； 搜集； 采集 | Our task is to analyze the data that have been collected. | 我们的任务是分析收集到的资料。 |
| 6 | diversify | /daiˈvəːsifai/ | vt. | to make （more） diverse； to spread into new habitats and produce variety or variegate | 使成形形色色； 使多样化； 使 变化 | Our factory is trying to diversify its range of products. | 我们厂正设法增加产品的品种。 |
| 7 | design | /diˈzain/ | n. | （1） n. the act of working out the form of something （as by making a sketch or outline or plan） ； an arrangement scheme vt. to formulate a plan for； to devise | 设 计； 构思； 绘制 （2） 计划； 设计 | These are the houses specifically designed for old people. | 这些是专门为老年人设计的房子。 |
| 8 | empathy | /ˈempəθi/ | n. | understanding and entering into another’s feelings | 移情作用； 神入 | In all his actions and decisions， the American people sensed a deep empathy for the poor and the powerless. | 在他的所有行动和决定中， 美国人民感到了对穷人和无权无势者的深切 同情。 |
| 9 | enquire | /inˈkwaiə/ | vi. | to make a search or inquiry into， in order to discover information | 询问； 打听； 调查 | They enquire closely into the merits of the case. | 他们仔细调查了事情的是非曲直。 |
| 10 | fad | /fæd/ | n. | an interest followed with exaggerated zeal | 时尚， 一时流行 的狂热 | His interest in photography is only a passing fad. | 他对摄影的兴趣只是一时的爱好罢了。 |
| 11 | globalization | /ˌgləubəlaiˈzeiʃən； -liˈz/ | n. | growth to a global or worldwide scale | 全球化 | Globalization does not pose equal interests and risks to all nations. | 全球化对所有的国家并不构成同等的利益和风险。 |
| 12 | hierarchy | /ˈhaiəraːki/ | n. | a series of ordered groupings of people or things within a system | 等级制度； 统治集团； 阶级组织 | The lack of importance attached to human-resource management can be seen in the corporate hierarchy. | 对人力资源管理的重视的缺乏可以从公司的组织层级结构上看出来。 |
| 13 | imitate | /ˈimiteit/ | vt. | to copy （the behaviour， appearance， speech， etc.） typical of （a person）； to take as an example or model | 模仿； 仿 效； 仿制； 伪造 | His handwriting is difficult to imitate. | 他的笔迹很难模仿。 |
| 14 | lifestyle | /ˈlaifstail/ | n. | a manner of living that reflects the person’s values and attitudes | 生活方式 | Travelling in France gave me an idea of their lifestyle. | 在法国的旅行让我对他们的生活方式有所了解。 |
| 15 | mastermind | /ˈmaːstəmaind/ | n. | someone who creates new things； someone who has exceptional intellectual ability and originality | 优秀的策 划者； 幕后操作的人； 智囊 | Three cobblers with their wits combined equal Zhuge Liang the mastermind. | 三个臭皮匠， 抵个诸葛亮。 |
| 16 | originality | /ˌəridʒəˈnæliti/ | n. | the ability to think and act independently； the quality of being new and original （not derived from something else） | 创意； 新奇； 原始 | His design shows great originality. | 他的设计颇具独创性。 |
| 17 | pioneer | /paiəˈniə/ | n. | （1） n. someone who helps to open up a new line of research or technology or art vt. to open up an area or prepare a way | 先锋； 开辟者 （2） 提倡； 开辟 | Henry Ford was a pioneer in the auto industry. | 亨利·福特是汽车工业的先驱。 |
| 18 | positioning | /pəˈziʃəniŋ/ | n. | the act of putting something in a certain place | 定位 | In order to begin positioning a product， two questions need to be answered. First， what is your marketing environment？ Second， what is your competitive advantage？ | 要开始定位一个产品， 有两个问题需要回答： 第一， 你们的营销 环境是什么？ 第二， 你们的竞争优势在哪里？ |
| 19 | strategy | /ˈstrætidʒi/ | n. | an elaborate and systematic plan of action； the branch of military science dealing with military command and the planning and conduct of a war | 策略； 战略 | Profits motivate this strategy along with ethics. | 利润和伦理是这种商业策略的动机。 |
| 20 | version | /ˈvəːʃən/ | n. | something a little different from others of the same type； an interpretation of a matter from a particular viewpoint | 版本； 翻译 | We upgrade over an earlier version of Windows 2000. | 我们正在对 Windows 2000 的早期版本升级。 |

### Unit 19 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | aesthetic | /esˈθetik/ | adj. | concerning or characterized by an appreciation of beauty or good taste； relating to or dealing with the subject of aesthetics | 有美感的； 审美的； 美学的 | The product also shows the aesthetic values of the time. | 这款产品也显示出当时的社会审美价值。 |
| 2 | audience | /ˈɔːdjəns/ | n. | a gathering of spectators or listeners at a （usually public） performance； the part of the general public interested in a source of information or entertainment | 受众； 观众； 读者 | In order to achieve the purpose of promoting products， sometimes the advertiser deliberately sets language traps in advertisements to mislead the audience. | 有的广告发布者为了达到推销商品的目的， 故意在广告中设置 “文字陷阱”， 以误导消费者。 |
| 3 | awareness | /əˈwɛənis/ | n. | having knowledge of； state of elementary or undifferentiated consciousness | 意识； 明白； （产品） 知晓度； 知名度 | It is advisable to give voluntary lectures to popularize Olympic knowledge and environmental awareness in some residential areas. | 在一些住宅区做义务讲座， 普及奥林匹克知识和环保意识， 这是非常可取的 做法。 |
| 4 | communication | /kəˌmjuːniˈkeiʃən/ | n. | the activity of communicating； the activity of conveying information | 通讯； 传达； 传播 | Telegraph communication was broken off. | 电报通讯已经中断。 |
| 5 | comment | /ˈkɔment/ | n. | a statement that expresses a personal opinion or belief； a written explanation or criticism or illustration that is added to a book or other textual material | 注解； 评论； 意见 | His pithy comments knocked the bottom out of my argument. | 他精辟的评论彻底驳倒了我的论点。 |
| 6 | convenient | /kənˈviːniənt/ | adj. | suited to your comfort or purpose or needs； large and roomy | 便利的； 方便的 | He found a simple and convenient way to solve the problem. | 他找到了解决那一问题的简便方法。 |
| 7 | determinant | /diˈtəːminənt/ | n. | a determining or causal element or factor | 决定因素 | Social finesse， willingness to relocate and language capacity are also determinant factors in assessing job qualifications. | 社交手腕、 愿意调动以及语言能力也是评估工作资格的决定性因素。 |
| 8 | domestic | /dəˈmestik/ | adj. | of or relating to the home； of concern to or concerning the internal affairs of a nation | 家里的， 家庭的； 本国的， 国 内的 | Soon they produced enough to satisfy domestic demands. | 不久他们就生产了足够的产品来满足国内的需求了。 |
| 9 | door-to-door | /ˈdɔːrtˈəˈdɔːr/ | adj. | （of e.g. journeys or deliveries） direct from point of origin to point of destination； omitting no one； from the door of one house to that of the next | 按户访问的； 按户送达的 | The limousine offers door-to-door service. | 该豪华轿车提供门到门的服务。 |
| 10 | end user | /end ˈjuːzə（r）/ | n. | the ultimate user for which something is intended | 最后用户； 终端用户 | The purpose of channel marketing is to satisfy the end users in the market. | 分销的目的是满足市场上终端用户的需要。 |
| 11 | forecast | /ˈfɔːkaːst/ | vt./vi. | to predict in advance； to judge to be probable | 预想； 预测； 预计 | How much quantity do you forecast to sell in the first year？ | 贵公司预计在第一年销售多少数量呢？ |
| 12 | localization | /ˌləukəlaiˈzeiʃən/ | n. | a determination of the place where something is； the principle that specific functions have relatively circumscribed locations in some particular part or organ of the body | 本 土化； 本地化 | As the globalization and localization market continues to expand and increase in complexity， we’ve made it our mission to provide customers with maximum choice in finding the globalization solution that best fits their unique needs. | 随着全球化与本土化市场的继续扩大和日趋复杂， 我们要将为顾客提供最多 选择作为使命， 使他们寻找到最适合其独特需求的全球化解决方案。 |
| 13 | mature | /məˈtjuə/ | adj. | （1 ） adj. having reached full natural growth or development vt. to develop and work out fully in one’s mind | 成熟的； 充分考虑的 （2） 使成熟 | He matured his design by constant revision. | 他不断修改自己的设计使之完善。 |
| 14 | observe | /əbˈzəːv/ | vt./vi. | to discover or determine the existence， presence， or fact of； to make mention of | 观察， 注意到； 注意， 评论 | It is difficult to observe any change in the overseas market. | 很难看出国外市场有什么变化。 |
| 15 | orient | /ˈɔːriənt/ | vt. | to determine one’s position with reference to another point | 使适应， 使熟悉情况或环境； 使朝向 | It took quite a long time for a freshman to orient himself. | 大学新生要经过好长一段时间才能适应新环境。 |
| 16 | presentation | /ˌprezenˈteiʃən/ | n. | the act of making something publicly available； presenting news or other information by broadcasting or printing it | 介绍； 陈述； 演示 | He prepared his presentation carefully in advance. | 他预先精心准备了陈述。 |
| 17 | psychology | /saiˈkɔlədʒi/ | n. | the science of mental life | 心理状态， 心 理学 | The psychology of customer decisions will play an important part in your sales efforts as unspoken customer requirements. | 顾客决定的心理可以作为潜在的客户需求， 在你的销售工作中扮演了重要的 角色。 |
| 18 | questionnaire | /ˌkwestʃəˈnɛə/ | n. | a form containing a set of questions； submitted to people to gain statistical information | 调查表， 问卷 | We can use a questionnaire to survey attitudes to smoking. | 我们可以使用问卷方式调查对吸烟的意见。 |
| 19 | symbolize | /ˈsimbəlaiz/ | vt. | to express indirectly by an image， form， or model； to be a symbol； to represent or identify by using a symbol； to use symbols | 采用象征； 用符号表现 | A wedding ring symbolizes the union of the two partners. | 结婚戒指象征着男女双方的结合。 |
| 20 | workable | /ˈwəːkəbl/ | adj. | capable of being done with means at hand and circumstances as they are | 可使用的； 可操作的； 可行的 | Your quoted price is out of line with the prevailing level. That is why we feel confident that our suggestion of three percent off your present one is workable. | 你方报价与市价有差异， 所以我们相信减价百分之三的建议是可行的。 |

## Unit 20　Quality Control 质量管理

### Unit 20 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | abnormal | /æbˈnɔːməl/ | adj. | not normal； not typical or usual or regular or conforming to a norm | 不正常的； 不规则的 | We do not think such an abnormal phenomenon will last long. | 我们认为， 这样的反常现象不会持续很久。 |
| 2 | attribute | /əˈtribju（ː）t/ | n. | a construct whereby objects or individuals can be distinguished； an abstraction belonging to or characteristic of an entity | 属性； 特征 | Her greatest attribute was her kindness. | 她最大的特点是与人为善。 |
| 3 | adjust | /əˈdʒʌst/ | vt./vi. | to alter or regulate so as to achieve accuracy or conform to a standard； to place in a line or arrange so as to be parallel or straight | 调整； 调节 | This electric pot can adjust the temperature automatically. | 这种电饭锅能自动调节温度。 |
| 4 | ageing | /ˈeidʒiŋ/ | n. | acquiring desirable qualities by being left undisturbed for some time； the organic process of growing older and showing the effects of increasing age | 成熟； 老化 | A severe industry-wide lack of investment in training means the long-term skills base is both ageing and shrinking. | 全行业在训练手段上严重的投资不足意味着长期技能基础的老化和萎缩。 |
| 5 | defect | /diˈfekt；ˈdiːfekt/ | n. | an imperfection in a bodily system； a failing or deficiency | 缺陷； 不足 | This made up the defect of the equipment to some extent. | 这在一定程度上弥补了设备的缺欠。 |
| 6 | efficiency | /iˈfiʃənsi/ | n. | the ratio of the output to the input of any system； skillfulness in avoiding wasted time and effort | 效率； 效能 | Our goal is to achieve the maximum of efficiency. | 我们的目标是取得最高的效率。 |
| 7 | formalization | /ˌfɔːməlaiˈzeiʃən/ | n. | the act of making formal （as by stating formal rules governing classes of expressions） | 规范化； 形式化 | It uses cluster analysis to explore the relationship between quality management and different forms of work formalization. | 它采用群集分析来探索质量管理和不同形式的工作规范之间的关系。 |
| 8 | grade | /greid/ | n. | a relative position or degree of value in a graded group | 等级 | Grade A potatoes are the best in quality. | 甲等马铃薯是质量最好的。 |
| 9 | improve | /imˈpruːv/ | vt./vi. | to make better； to get better | 改善； 改进； 提高 | Everybody is welcome to make suggestions on how to improve. | 欢迎大家对于怎么改善提出建议。 |
| 10 | ingredient | /inˈgriːdiənt/ | n. | a component of a mixture or compound； an abstract part of something | 要素， 成分 | One ingredient of Venetian success was national unity. | 威尼斯获得成功的要素是国民团结一致。 |
| 11 | measurement | /ˈmeʒəmənt/ | n. | the act or process of assigning numbers to phenomena according to a rule | 测量； 尺寸 | The committee also said that liquid medicines should all use the same measurement terms. | 该委员会还表示， 液体药品也应该使用相同的量度。 |
| 12 | nonconforming | /ˈnɔnkənˈfɔːmiŋ/ | adj. | not conforming to established customs or doctrines especially in religion | 不一致的； 不合格的 | The incoming quality submitted was 4% nonconforming， and the outgoing quality after inspection is 3. 6% nonconforming. | 呈递的进料质量水平是4% 不合格， 检查后出厂的质量水平是3. 6% 不合格。 |
| 13 | probability | /ˌprɔbəˈbiliti/ | n. | a measure of how likely it is that some event will occur； a number expressing the ratio of favorable cases to the whole number of cases possible | 几率； 概率 | There’s no probability that he’ll come back in a few days. | 他不可能几天之内就回来。 |
| 14 | quality gate | /ˈkwɔliti geit/ | n. | a review after key activities or phases of a project to verify work has been performed | 质量门； 质量阀 （即在达到 下一道工序前， 本工序的产品必须严格达到特定的品质标准） | It is your department’s responsibility to carry out quality gates for all mobile devices for all key milestones to enable management to take fast and stable decisions. | 为所有移动设备的一切关键指标实施质量把关， 使管理部门能够做出快速 而稳健的决定， 这是你们部门的责任。 |
| 15 | reliability | /riˌlaiəˈbiliti/ | n. | the quality of being dependable or reliable | 可 靠性， 可信度， 可信赖性 | Online brands want to be seen and want to be part of the real world to add some reliability and realness to their brands. | 网络品牌希望能被购买者看见， 并成为现实世界的一部分， 以此来为他们自 己的品牌增添一些可靠性和真实性。 |
| 16 | responsibility | /riˌspɔnsəˈbiliti/ | n. | the social force that binds you to the courses of action demanded by that force | 职责， 责任 | All rights carry with them corresponding responsibilities. | 所有的权利都带有相应的责任。 |
| 17 | sampling | /ˈsaːmpliŋ/ | n. | （statistics） the selection of a suitable sample for study； items selected at random from a population and used to test hypotheses about the population | 抽样； 提供样品 | Probably he had come down in the world and this sampling was all that was left him from the time when he could afford to come and select his favorite pudding. | 很可能他已经家道败落， 从前他是有钱来选购他最喜爱的布丁的， 而今却只 能靠试吃样品来品尝一下布丁的味道了。 |
| 18 | supervisor | /ˈsjuːpəvaizə/ | n. | one who supervises or has charge and direction of ； a program that controls the execution of other programs | 监督人； 管理人； 检查员 | Why don’t you take the problem up with the factory supervisor？ | 你为什么不把问题告诉工厂检察员呢？ |
| 19 | unacceptable | /ˈʌnəkˈseptəbl/ | adj. | not acceptable； not welcome | 不可以 接受的 | I’m afraid your suggestion is unacceptable. | 恐怕您的建议是不可取的。 |
| 20 | yardstick | /ˈjaːdstik/ | n. | anything which serves as a test or standard of measurement or comparison | 标准； 准绳； 码尺 | Durability is one yardstick of quality. | 耐久性是质量好坏的一个尺度。 |

### Unit 20 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | aberration | /ˌæbəˈreiʃən/ | n. | a state or condition markedly different from the norm | 色差； 偏差 | This reliable temperature adjust drying room avoiding aberration is a necessary facility. | 这个可靠的温度调节烘房可以避免色差， 是一个必要的设施。 |
| 2 | accuracy | /ˈækjurəsi/ | n. | the quality of being near to the true value； （mathematics） the number of significant figures given in a number | 准确 度， 准确性 | Audrey was famous for her accuracy in accountancy. | 奥德丽在会计工作中因精确而著名。 |
| 3 | airless | /ˈɛəlis/ | adj. | lacking fresh air | 不通风的； 密闭的 | In a hot， airless room in Africa， surrounded by customs officials， I filled out forms to get a residency permit. | 在非洲一间闷热、 密闭的房间里， 周围都是海关官员， 我填写了表格， 以获 得居留许可。 |
| 4 | average | /ˈævəridʒ/ | n. | a statistic describing the location of a distribution | 平均数； 平均水平； 一般标准 | The Dow Jones industrial average soared to 9850 points. | 道·琼斯工业平均指数飙升至9850 点。 |
| 5 | durable | /ˈdjuərəbl/ | adj. | existing for a long time | 耐用的 | This raincoat is made of very durable material. | 这件雨衣是用非常耐用的料子做的。 |
| 6 | experiment | /iksˈperimənt/ | n. | the act of conducting a controlled test or investigation | 实验 | Experiments indicate the new drug will cure infections. | 实验证明， 这种新药可以治传染病。 |
| 7 | failure | /ˈfeiljə/ | n. | an act that fails； an event that does not accomplish its intended purpose | 失效； 缺陷； 故障 | Business failure registered a postwar record this year. | 今年厂商的倒闭达到战后最高纪录。 |
| 8 | fatigue | /fəˈtiːg/ | n. | temporary loss of strength and energy resulting from hard physical or mental work | 疲劳， 疲惫 | Working overtime caused him great fatigue. | 加班加点的工作使他疲惫不堪。 |
| 9 | genre | /ˈʒaːŋrə/ | n. | a kind of literary or artistic work； a style of expressing yourself in writing | 种类； 属类； 流派 | Digital graphics and special effects gave the genre a new lease of life in the 1990s. | 数字化图形和特殊效果使这种类型在20 世纪90 年代获得了新生。 |
| 10 | intrinsic | /inˈtrinsik/ | adj. | belonging to a thing by its very nature； situated within or belonging solely to the organ or body part on which it acts | 内部的； 固有的； 真正的 | We should distinguish between extrinsic and intrinsic motivation. | 我们应当区分外部激励和内部激励。 |
| 11 | job shop | /dʒɔb ʃɔp/ | n. | a typically small manufacturing operation that handles specialized manufacturing processes such as small customer orders or small batch jobs | 加工车间 | It is basically a flexible job shop problem， with some added complexities due to the special characteristics of the machines involved in the production process. | 它基本上是一个可以通融的工作车间问题， 只是由于生产过程中的机器的特 殊性质， 显得有些复杂。 |
| 12 | prevention | /priˈvenʃən/ | n. | the act of preventing | 预防； 阻止； 妨碍 | An ounce of prevention is better than a pound of cure. | 一分预防胜于十分治疗。 |
| 13 | reject | /riˈdʒekt/ | vt. | to refuse to accept or acknowledge | 拒收； 抵 制； 否决 | The official is entitled to accept and reject any materials and products. | 官员有权批准或否定一切物料及车间产品的使用和产品出厂。 |
| 14 | repair | /riˈpɛə/ | vt. | to put something in working order again； to restore by replacing a part or putting together what is torn or broken | 修理； 补 救； 修复 | He repairs radios for his friends during his spare time. | 他利用业余时间给朋友们修理收音机。 |
| 15 | standardization | /ˌstændədaiˈzeiʃən/ | n. | the condition in which a standard has been successfully established； the imposition of standards or regulations | 标准化 | The standardization of nuts and bolts had saved industry millions of dollars. | 标准化的螺母和螺栓可以为工业节省百万美元。 |
| 16 | shrinkage | /ˈʃriŋkidʒ/ | n. | process or result of becoming less or smaller； the amount by which something shrinks | 减少； 收缩； 损耗 | There has been some shrinkage in our export trade. | 我们的出口贸易已有些萎缩。 |
| 17 | survey | /səːˈvei/ | n. | （1） n. a detailed critical inspection vt. to consider in a comprehensive way | 调查， 检查 （2） 调查； 测量； 检查 | We were pleased with our first survey of the house. | 我们对那幢房子的第一次检查感到满意。 |
| 18 | tighten | /ˈtaitn/ | vt./vi. | to make tight or tighter； to become tight or tighter | 加严； 勒紧， 使变紧 | He’s just lost his job so he has to tighten his belt. | 他刚失去工作， 所以他必须节衣缩食。 |
| 19 | validation | /ˌvæliˈdeiʃən/ | n. | the act of validating； finding or testing the truth of something； the cognitive process of establishing a valid proof | 确认 | Is this award a validation of to what you do？ | 这个奖励是对您所做成就的证明吗？ |
| 20 | variation | /ˌvɛəriˈeiʃən/ | n. | an instance of change； the rate or magnitude of change； an activity that varies from a norm or standard | 变异 | Variations outside the range given are not unlikely. | 变化超出规定范围并非不可能。 |

## Unit 21　Marketing and Customer Service 市场营销与客户服务

### Unit 21 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | alteration | /ˌɔːltəˈreiʃən/ | n. | an event that occurs when something passes from one state or phase to another； the act of making something different （as e.g. the size of a garment） | 退换； 变更； 修改 | There have been a few alterations to the timetable. | 时间表上有一些变动。 |
| 2 | behavior | /biˈheiviə/ | n. | manner of acting or controlling yourself； the action or reaction of something （as a machine or substance） under specified circumstances | 行为； 举止； 状态 | I must explain that the reason for my dissatisfaction is the waiter’s inconsiderate behavior. | 我必须解释， 我之所以心有不满， 是因为服务员的态度不周到。 |
| 3 | clearance | /ˈkliərəns/ | n. | the rate at which a substance is removed or cleared from the body by； the distance by which one thing clears another； the space between them； vertical space available to allow easy passage under something | 清仓； 批准； 许可 | All types of shoes and handbags on clearance. Here you’ll find shoes and handbags marked down to $29. 95！ | 所有款式的鞋子和手提包清仓大甩卖。 你在本店可以发现低至$29. 95 的鞋 和手提包！ |
| 4 | complain | /kəmˈplein/ | vi. | to express complaints，discontent，displeasure， or unhappiness； to make a formal accusation； to bring a formal charge | 抱 怨； 投诉； 不满 | Some workers complain about this way of management. | 一些工人对这种管理方法怨声载道。 |
| 5 | deferred payment | /diˈfəːd ˈpeimənt/ | n. | a debt which has been incurred and will be paid back at some point in the future | 延期付款； 分期付款 | If you can’t afford the purchase， you can go with a deferred payment plan. | 如果您一时购买不起， 可以考虑采用延期付款的计划。 |
| 6 | elite | /eiˈliːt/ | n. | a group or class of persons enjoying superior intellectual or social or economic status； selected as the best | 精英， 中坚分子； 精华 | The only thing a non-elite market can offer that likely won’t be matched by an elite market is the security of a long-term deal. | 非精英市场能够提供而精英市场很可能无法提供的唯一东西就是长期贸易的 安全性。 |
| 7 | expectation | /ˌekspekˈteiʃən/ | n. | belief about （or mental picture of） the future； wishing with confidence of fulfillment | 期待； 预料； 期望值 | We should justify the ardent expectations of our customers. | 我们要不辜负顾客的殷切期望。 |
| 8 | follow-up | /ˈfɔləu ʌp/ | n. | an activity that continues something that has already begun or that repeats something that has already been done | 后续 服务工作 | The kind of follow-up service is determined by your product type and your type of certification. | 什么样的后续服务取决于你的产品类型和你的认证类型。 |
| 9 | feedback | /ˈfiːdbæk/ | n. | the process of giving and receiving input in the form of comments， opinions， observations， and analysis to either validate or invalidate the customer service system | 反馈； 回复， 反应 | This calls for close monitoring and rapid feedback. | 这需要对业务的紧密关注和快速反应。 |
| 10 | impulse | /ˈimpʌls/ | n. | an instinctive motive； a sudden desire | 冲动； 刺 激， 推动 | Up to 60% of all items bought in malls are purchased on impulse. | 顾客在商场购买的所有物品中高达60% 是出于一时的冲动。 |
| 11 | intimate | /ˈintimit/ | adj. | marked by close acquaintance， association， or familiarity； having or fostering a warm or friendly and informal atmosphere | 亲密的； 隐私的 | He is intimate with those customers. | 他与那些顾客关系密切。 |
| 12 | lookism | /ˈlukizəm/ | n. | discrimination against or prejudice towards others based on their appearance | 容貌歧视 | Lookism is also a factor in areas other than employment. | 除了就业， 容貌歧视在其他领域也是一个因素。 |
| 13 | loyalty | /ˈlɔiəlti/ | n. | the quality of being loyal； feelings of allegiance | 忠 诚， 忠贞， 忠实 | Loyalty shines out in time of woe. | 艰难的时候方能显出忠诚。 |
| 14 | motive | /ˈməutiv/ | n. | the reason for the action； that which gives purpose and direction to behavior | 动机， 目的； 主题 | The man privately admits that his motive is to make profit. | 那人私下承认， 他的动机是为了谋利。 |
| 15 | offset | /ˈɔːfset/ | vt. | to compensate for or counterbalance | 弥补； 抵销 | The pay raise will be offset by inflation. | 增加的工资会被通货膨胀所抵销。 |
| 16 | privilege | /ˈprivilidʒ/ | n. | a special advantage or immunity or benefit not enjoyed by all； a right reserved exclusively by a particular person or group （especially a hereditary or official right） | 特权； 特殊待遇 | The store extended credit privileges to us. | 该商店给予了我们信贷特权。 |
| 17 | preference | /ˈprefərəns/ | n. | a strong liking； a predisposition in favor of something | 偏好 | We’ve granted that country special trade preferences. | 我们给了那个国家特殊的贸易优惠。 |
| 18 | remittance | /riˈmitns/ | n. | a payment of money sent to a person in another place | 汇款 | We’ll notify you as soon as your remittance come. | 您的汇款一到， 我们就通知您。 |
| 19 | self-concept | /selfˈkɔnsept/ | n. | the mental and conceptual understanding and persistent regard that sentient beings hold for their own existence | 自我 概念 | The exercise involving the beliefs to foster self-concept will provide participants with the required tools to develop a plan. | 这项练习可以促进自我概念， 为参加者提供制定计划的必要工具。 |
| 20 | tie-in sale | /ˈtaiˌin seil/ | n. | a kind of sale results from a contractual arrangement between a consumer and a producer whereby the consumer can obtain the desired good （tying good） only if he agrees also to purchase a different good （tied good） from the producer | 搭售， 搭卖 | The so-called “tie-in sales ” provisions and deceptive or misleading warranty terms are the two prohibitions under the act. | 所谓的 “搭卖” 规定和欺骗性或误导性的保修条款是该法案禁止的两种行为。 |

### Unit 21 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | annual | /ˈænjuəl/ | adj. | occurring or payable every year； completing its life cycle within a year | 一年一次的； 每年的 | We’d like to see your annual report for the last two years. | 我们想看看贵公司过去两年的年度报告。 |
| 2 | atmosphere | /ˈætməusfiə/ | n. | a particular environment or surrounding influence | 气氛， 氛围 | The talk was conducted in a cordial atmosphere. | 会谈是在热情友好的气氛中进行的。 |
| 3 | conflict | /ˈkɔnflikt/ | n. | an open clash between two opposing groups （or individuals）； opposition between two simultaneous but incompatible feelings | 冲突； 争执 | I check to make sure our schedule does not conflict. | 我查看以确定我们的日程没有冲突。 |
| 4 | customary | /ˈkʌstəməri/ | adj. | in accordance with convention or custom； commonly used or practiced； usual | 习惯性的； 惯例的 | It is customary to tip the waiter. | 给侍者小费是一种习俗。 |
| 5 | discrimination | /disˌkrimiˈneiʃən/ | n. | unfair treatment of a person or group on the basis of prejudice | 歧视 | She sued the company for racial discrimination. | 她以种族歧视为由对公司起诉。 |
| 6 | experience | /iksˈpiəriəns/ | n. | the accumulation of knowledge or skill that results from direct participation in events or activities； the content of direct observation or participation in an event | 经验， 经历； 感受 | He illustrated his point by relating his own experiences. | 他用自己的经历说明他的观点。 |
| 7 | feel | /be inclined to/ |  | fiːl/ biː inˈklaind tuː/ having a preference， disposition， or tendency | 倾向……的 | I am inclined to query the accuracy of that statement. | 我颇怀疑该声明的正确性。 |
| 8 | guarantee | /ˌgærənˈtiː/ | vt. | to give surety or assume responsibility； to make certain of | 保证， 担保 | The maker guarantees this hair dye to stay on three weeks. | 制造商保证染发剂使用后的有效期为3 个星期。 |
| 9 | handle | /hændl/ | vt. | to be in charge of， act on， or dispose of | 处理； 操作； 运用 | We will handle this case with discretion. | 我们会谨慎地处理这个案子。 |
| 10 | intangible | /inˈtændʒəbl/ | adj. | not having physical substance or intrinsic productive value | 无形的； 切实的 | The trademark is our company’s intangible asset. | 商标是我们公司的无形资产。 |
| 11 | perception | /pəˈsepʃən/ | n. | the representation of what is perceived； basic component in the formation of a concept | 知觉； 感觉 | His analysis of the problem showed great perception. | 他对该问题的分析显示出敏锐的洞察力。 |
| 12 | personality | /ˌpəːsəˈnæliti/ | n. | the complex of all the attributes— behavioral， temperamental， emotional and mental—that characterize a unique individual | 个性 | I wish I had a more confident personality. | 我希望我的个性更有自信。 |
| 13 | popularity | /ˌpɔpjuˈlæriti/ | n. | the quality of being widely admired or accepted or sought after | 普及； 流行； 声望 | Cyber bars have gained in popularity in recent years. | 网吧近年来越来越流行。 |
| 14 | potential | /pəˈtenʃəl/ | adj. | existing in possibility； expected to become or be； in prospect | 潜在的； 可能的 | The product has even more potential in export markets. | 这种产品在出口市场上甚至会有更大的销售潜力。 |
| 15 | retention | /riˈtenʃən/ | n. | the act of retaining something； the power of retaining and recalling past experience | 维系； 保留； 保持 | The goal of customer retention marketing is to convert first-time or occasional buyers into loyal， long-term customers. | 顾客维系营销的目标就是将第一次或偶然购物的买方成为长期忠实的客户。 |
| 16 | rumor | /ˈruːmə/ | n. | gossip （usually a mixture of truth and untruth） passed around by word of mouth | 风评； 谣言； 传闻 | The rumor is afloat that there is a run on the bank. | 银行挤兑的谣言四起。 |
| 17 | supplementary | /ˌsʌpliˈmentəri/ | adj. | functioning in a subsidiary or supporting capacity； added to complete or make up a deficiency | 附加 的； 补足的 | The new students received supplementary instruction. | 新生接受了补课。 |
| 18 | stereotype | /ˈstiəriətaip/ | n. | a conventional or formulaic conception or image | 典范； 老套 | He’s the stereotype of an army officer. | 他属于那种老派的军官。 |
| 19 | subculture | /ˈsʌbkʌltʃə/ | n. | a social group within a national culture that has distinctive patterns of behavior and beliefs | 亚文化 | Probably one of the most misunderstood subcultures in our generation is the skinhead subculture. | 或许我们这一代人最容易让人误解的就是光头亚文化。 |
| 20 | telemarketing | /ˈtelimaːkitiŋ/ | n. | the use of the telephone as an interactive medium for promotion and sales | 电话销售 | Telemarketing is the most interactive marketing medium available nowadays. | 电话销售是目前最具有互动性的营销媒介。 |

## Unit 22　Job Application & Interview 工作申请与面试

### Unit 22 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | trainee | /treiˈniː/ | n. | someone who is employed at a low level in a particular job in order to learn the skills needed for that job | 新兵； 练习生， 实习生 | My first job was as a graduate trainee with a bank. | 我的第一份工作是在一家银行做大学毕业实习生。 |
| 2 | reference | /ˈrefrəns/ | n. | a formal recommendation by a former employer to a potential future employer describing the person’s qualifications and dependability | 介绍信； 证明书 | A reference letter can boost employees’ confidence in a depressing economy. | 一封推荐信能在经济低迷时为员工的自信心打上一剂强心针。 |
| 3 | solid | /ˈsɔlid/ | adj. | characterized by good substantial quality | 固体的； 可靠 的； 结实的 | At 40， he had solid experience as an online marketing exec， but no one was hiring him. | 作为一个40 岁的人， 他有着丰富的在线营销管理经验， 但却始终没被录用。 |
| 4 | foundation | /faunˈdeiʃən/ | n. | the basis on which something is grounded | 基础； 地基； 根据 | Job evaluation is the foundation of the whole designation on position and compensation system. | 工作评估是整个职位薪酬体系设计的基础。 |
| 5 | quit | /kwit/ | vt./vi. | to choose to leave a job； to give up or retire from a position | 离开； 辞职 | It would be folly to quit your job before finding a new one. | 在你找到新的工作前就辞职是不明智的。 |
| 6 | apply for | /əˈplai fɔː/ | v. | to ask （for something） | 申请 | If you want to apply for a job， you have to prepare for interview， during which there are always some difficult questions that you have to answer. | 你若要申请工作， 就得作好面试的准备， 到时常常得回答一些棘手的问题。 |
| 7 | available | /əˈveiləbl/ | adj. | obtainable or accessible and ready for use or service | 可获得的； 可找到的； 有空的 | In the final paragraph mention any dates that you won’t be available for an interview and remember to thank the employer and say that you look forward to hearing from them. | 最后一段要指出不能参加面试的时间， 并对雇主表示感谢， 期望从他们那里 得到回复。 |
| 8 | qualified | /ˈkwɔlifaid/ | adj. | meeting the proper standards and requirements and training for an office or position or task | 合格的； 有资格的 | It also means that your “guanxi” or connections can be very important as a tiebreaker when a hundred equally-qualified applicants are after the same job. | 这也意味着当有一百名资质相同的申请者竞争同一份工作时， 你的关系或人 脉将成为制胜的决定性因素。 |
| 9 | character | /ˈkæriktə/ | n. | all the qualities that make people distinct from other people or places | 性格， 品质； 特性 | Requests for character references are all too often answered evasively. | 品行推荐信的请求总是会被人避免回答。 |
| 10 | description | /diˈskripʃn/ | n. | an account which explains what someone or something is or what they look like | 描述； 形容 | Thank you for sending me the job description for the post of Assistant Personnel Officer. | 感谢您寄来的有关人事助理一职的工作说明。 |
| 11 | diploma | /diˈpləumə/ | n. | a document certifying the successful completion of a course of study | 毕业证书， 学位证书； 奖状 | If you have a lot of knowledge and practical experience in your field but no diploma to show for it， don’t worry because you can prove yourself with your performance. | 如果你在你的专业领域有丰富的知识和很多的实践经验， 但是这些却不能通 过证书来反映出来， 也不要担心， 因为你可以通过你的工作表现来证明 自己。 |
| 12 | expected | /ikˈspektid/ | adj. | considered likely or probable to happen or arrive | 预期的； 预料的 | Please email or fax your resume （both in English and Chinese）， diploma， training certificates and expected salary to the following address within two weeks. | 请在两周内发电子邮件或传真你的简历 （中英文）、 毕业证书、 培训证书以 及期望薪水到下列地址。 |
| 13 | graduate | /ˈgrædjueit，-dʒueit/ | vi. | （1） vi. to receive an academic degree upon completion of one’s studies n. a person who has successfully completed a degree at a university or college | 毕业 （2 ） 大学毕业生 | I want to give them every opportunity to graduate with skills that allow them to have fulfilling careers — careers where they contribute to the field. | 我想给他们机会， 让他们毕业时能够获得满足职业要求的技能———他们能在 自己的职业生涯领域作出贡献。 |
| 14 | interpersonal | /ˌintəˈpeːsənl/ | adj. | relating to relationships between people | 人际的； 人与人之间的 | The result of the survey clearly indicates that many employees were well prepared in business knowledge and computer skills， but lacked interpersonal skills to interact gracefully with customers. | 该调查结果清楚地显示， 很多员工在商业知识和计算机技能方面准备充足， 但是缺乏与客户得体沟通的人际交往能力。 |
| 15 | skills set | /ˈskilz ˌset/ | n. | a particular combination of skills that a person has developed， especially ones that can be used in a job | 一套技能 | If we were fully participating in the moment and sharing in solving the problem， we probably could have found a way to assist within our talents and skills set. | 如果我们马上完全参与并且分担解决问题， 那么我们就可能会以我们的才能 和技能找到援助的方法。 |
| 16 | requirement | /riˈkwairmənt/ | n. | something that is required in advance | 要求； 必要条件； 必需品 | In some cases， companies will post highly detailed job requirements in want ads， despite the fact that the job may actually require a generalist candidate with a broad set of skills. | 在某些情况下， 企业会在招聘广告中列出十分具体的职位要求， 尽管该职位 实际上可能需要一位拥有广泛技能的全才应聘者。 |
| 17 | practical | /ˈpræktikəl/ | adj. | guided by actual experience and observation rather than theory | 实际的； 实用性的 | That is why I believe that knowledge gained from practical experience is more important than that acquired from books. | 那就是为什么我认为从实际经验中获得的知识比从书本中所得到的知识更为 重要。 |
| 18 | qualification | /ˌkwɔlifiˈkeiʃən/ | n. | an attribute that must be met or complied with and that fits a person for something | 资格； 条件； 限制 | Responsibility and reliability are necessary qualifications， as well as a friendly and outgoing personality. | 责任感和可靠性是必需的素质， 友善和外向的性格也必不可少。 |
| 19 | vacancy | /ˈveikənsi/ | n. | a job or position that has not been filled | 空缺； 空位； 空白 | One experienced headhunter told me she had never been asked for a lawyer to fill a board vacancy. | 一位经验丰富的猎头曾告诉我， 从未有人委托她物色一位律师， 来填补董事 会空缺。 |
| 20 | working experience | /ˈwəːkiŋ ikˈspiəriəns/ | n. | any experience that a person gains while working in a specific field or occupation | 工作经验 | Some fresh graduates who have little or no working experience may face a similar problem， in that they cannot get experience until they get a job， but cannot get a job as they have no experience. | 一些缺乏社会工作经验的应届大学毕业生往往都会面临同样的两难困境， 那 就是除非找到一份工作， 否则没有工作经验； 然而他们却找不到工作， 恰恰 又是因为没有工作经验。 |

### Unit 22 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | contact | /ˈkɔntækt/ | vt./vi. | to be in or establish communication with （ | 使） 接触， 联系 | Secondly， you may contact Human Resource and other employment agencies in California to find out what jobs are available to fit your interest and qualification. | 其次， 你可以联系加州的人力资源和其他就业机构来发现那些适合你的兴趣 和资质的工作。 |
| 2 | recruitment | /riˈkruːtmənt/ | n. | enlisting people for the army （or for a job or a cause etc.） | 补充； 征募新兵 | Employment interview is a subjective evaluation process concerning the interviewer and the interviewee， and it also is a vital method for recruitment. | 招聘面试是考官对求职者的主观评价过程， 是大部分企业招聘新员工的一种 重要方法。 |
| 3 | promotion from within （PFW） | /prəˈməuʃən frəm wiˈðin/ |  | a commitment to consider current employees for promotion opportunities before hiring candidates from outside of the organization | 内部提升 | Best Buy actively encourages all its employees to suggest improvements to the company’s work processes， much as Toyota does， and favors promotion from within. | 百思买 （Best Buy） 积极鼓励其所有员工像丰田那样对公司的工作流程提出 改进建议， 并从中提拔优秀员工。 |
| 4 | posting | /ˈpəustiŋ/ | n. | sign posted in a public place as an advertisement | 公布； 邮递； 布置 | Each job posting will stress different qualities over others， so rework each résumé to highlight the experience and skills that correspond to that particular employer. | 每个工作岗位公告对不同要求都有不同程度的强调， 因此你要修改每份简 历， 使它突出特定雇主所需要的经验和技能。 |
| 5 | degree | /diˈgriː/ | n. | an award conferred by a college or university signifying that the recipient has satisfactorily completed a course of study | 学位 | After obtaining a specific college degree， a job applicant can be competitive for previously unavailable jobs. | 获得了大学的某个学位之后， 求职者就在先前不可能得到的工作上有了竞 争力。 |
| 6 | agency | /ˈeidʒənsi/ | n. | a business that serves other businesses | 代理， 中 介； 代理处 | “The research demonstrates how motivational it can be to gain a prestigious job title，” said Paul Rapacioli， director of employment agency Reed’s Internet Service， which carried out the survey. | 调查是由职业介绍所里兹网络服务公司主持的， 该公司主管保罗·拉帕西奥 利说： “这次调查表明， 得到一个令人尊敬的头衔会产生多大的鼓舞作用。” |
| 7 | internship | /ˈintɜːnʃip/ | n. | a position held by an intern， or the period of time when someone is an intern | 实习生的职位； 实习期 | First I would just send my resume to every game studio’s human resource department asking for an internship regardless of if I saw one posted or not. | 首先我会给每个游戏工作室的人力资源部门发我的简历， 询问是否需要实 习生， 不管他们是否宣称正在招募实习生。 |
| 8 | selection | /siˈlekʃən/ | n. | the act of choosing or selecting | 选择， 挑选； 精 选品 | One advantage of this strategy is to encourage self-selection among candidates since some will decide they are not fitted for a particular role or employer. | 这种战略的一个优点就是， 鼓励求职者进行自我选择， 因为一些人将判定他 们不适合某个特定的职位或公司。 |
| 9 | validity | /vəˈlidəti/ | n. | the quality of having legal force or effectiveness | 有 效性； 正确； 正确性 | Human resource strategy is significantly linked to many aspects of leadership validity， such as， organization commitment， job satisfaction， and organization performance. | 人力资源战略与领导效能的许多方面， 如组织承诺、 工作满意感和组织绩 效等存在着显著的关系。 |
| 10 | objectivity | /ˌɔbdʒekˈtivəti/ | n. | judgment basedonobservablephenomena anduninfluencedbyemotionsor personal prejudices | 客观； 客观性 | The recruitment conclusion relies entirely on the record of the interview， the authenticity and objectivity of which can’t be guaranteed. | 得出的招聘结论完全依赖面试时的谈话记录， 但面试记录的真实性和客观 性则无法得到保证。 |
| 11 | norm | /nɔːm/ | n. | a standard or model or pattern regarded as typical | 标 准， 规范 | The changes include hiring external fund administrators， particularly in the US where this has not been the norm， and increasing the frequency and depth of reporting to clients. | 相关的变化包括聘用外部基金管理人———特别是在这种做法尚未成为规范的 美国、 提高客户报告的频率和深度等。 |
| 12 | cutoff score | /ˈkʌtɔːf skɔː/ | n. | the minimum number of points that someone must score on a test in order to be considered for a job， etc. | 录用分数线 | If you have large applicant pools， using a hard cutoff score is a great way to efficiently filter your candidates， and to screen out those who lack the basic aptitude or skills required for the job. | 如果你有很多申请者， 那么使用录取分数线是一个很好的方式来有效地过滤 你的应征人员， 并筛选出那些缺乏该工作所需的基本能力或技能的人员。 |
| 13 | job knowledge | /dʒɔb ˈnɔlidʒ/ | n. | one’s mastery of the concepts needed to perform certain work | 业务知识 | Doing most of the work to prepare a performance profile ahead of time demonstrates solid job knowledge and will earn you some potential partnership points. | 事先花大量时间准备绩效概况， 可证明你的业务知识扎实， 也会为你潜在 的团队合作加分。 |
| 14 | employment | /imˈplɔimənt/ | n. | the state of being employed or having a job | 使用； 职业； 雇用 | All too often， novice interviews don’t have a good technique for probing the “inner being” of the managerial candidate in the employment interview. | 很常见的是， 在面试过程中， 面试新手缺乏试探经理候选人 “内心世界” 的技巧。 |
| 15 | unstructured | /ʌnˈstrʌktʃəd/ | adj. | lacking definite structure or organization | 无社会组织的； 松散的； 非正式组成的 | Unstructured interviews allow questions based on the interviewee’s responses， and proceed like a friendly， non-threatening conversation. | 非结构化面试允许问题建立在被面试人的反应上， 并且以一种友好的、 不具 威胁性的对话方式进行。 |
| 16 | structured | /ˈstrʌktʃəd/ | adj. | having a distinct physical shape or form | 有 结构的； 有组织的 | We have introduced a more structured training programme. | 我们已经推出了一门更系统的培训课程。 |
| 17 | group | /gruːp/ | n. | any number of entities （members） considered as a unit | 组； 团体 | Sometimes for introverted people the group interview can be very daunting. | 有时候， 对于内向性格的求职者而言， 小组面试可能会让他们望而却步。 |
| 18 | vocational | /vəuˈkeiʃənəl/ | adj. | of or relating to a vocation or occupation； especially providing or undergoing training in special skills | 职业的， 行业的 | Vocational interest test is one of the approaches to select a proper job as vocation counselor. | 职业兴趣测试是在从事职业辅导员工作时选择适合工作的指标之一。 |
| 19 | interviewee | /ˌintəvjuːˈiː/ | adj. | a person who is interviewed | 被接见者； 被访问者 | If only you could choose your seats， you should have seated besides one side of the interviewee， not opposite. | 要是可以选择座位的话， 要坐在面试者桌子的侧面， 而不要坐在他的正 对面。 |
| 20 | interviewer | /ˈintəvjuːə（r）/ | n. | a person who conducts an interview | 面试 官； 会见者； 面谈者 | Once your interview is done， address the interviewer by name， smile and offer genuine appreciation for the investment of his or her time in your future with the company. | 当你的面试结束后， 面带微笑叫出面试官的名字， 然后向他/ 她表达自己真 挚的感谢， 感谢他/ 她抽出时间给你提供一个工作机会。 |

## Unit 23　Pay & Promotion 薪酬与晋升

### Unit 23 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | payroll | /ˈpeirəul/ | n. | a list of employees and their salaries | 工资册； 在职人 员工薪名册 | I heard she was off the payroll. | 我听说她被解雇了。 |
| 2 | wage | /weidʒ/ | n. | the amount of money that is regularly paid to people for the work that they do； something that remunerates | 工资； 代价； 报偿 | Take out a blank sheet of paper， and write down the “time budget” you prepared yesterday， and the approximate hourly wage you calculated earlier. | 拿出一张空白的纸， 写下你昨天准备好的 “时间预算” 以及你早先算好的你 每小时的实际工资。 |
| 3 | raise | /reiz/ | n. | an increase in your wages or salary | 上升； 加薪 | “Unfortunately， I got lies，” he replied. “Every year my employer reminds me that my hard work is being appreciated by the management and that I am due for a huge salary raise. ” “ | 很不幸， 我被骗过，” 他回答， “每年我的老板都骗我说我的辛勤劳动受到 管理层的赏识， 应该得到大幅加薪。” |
| 4 | windfall | /ˈwindfɔːl/ | n. | a sudden happening that brings good fortune （as a sudden opportunity to make money） | 外快； 意外之财 | Whenever you have a little windfall or make a good frugal choice， immediately save it for an extra debt repayment. | 无论什么时候你发了横财或是节约了开支， 立即把这些钱存起来作为额外债 务偿还资金。 |
| 5 | year-end | /ˈjəːrˌend，ˈjiə-/ | adj. | taking place at the close of a fiscal year | 年 终的 | Employees are urged to be discreet in their spending and not to flaunt their wealth. This reminder normally comes during the year-end bonus time. | 一般在年底发奖金时， 公司会提醒员工花钱要有尺度， 不要浪费自己的 财富。 |
| 6 | overtime pay | /ˈəuvətaim pei/ | n. | additional financial compensation for any hours worked by nonexempt staff over the amount of forty hours per week | 加班费 | In the letter， the five employees asked for the overtime pay they said is owed them and for compensation for health damages. | 在信中， 这五名员工要求支付所欠的加班工资和他们的健康损失补偿。 |
| 7 | punch | /pʌntʃ/ | n. | making holes in something | 打孔 | When you get to work， you put your card in the punch clock， and it will print your starting time on the card. | 你来上班的时候， 把你的卡片放进考勤表， 它就会打出你的上班时间。 |
| 8 | paycheck | /ˈpeitʃek/ | n. | a check issued in payment of wages or salary | 付 薪水的支票， 薪水 | All of us know how important work is not just for the paycheck， but for the peace of mind that comes with knowing you can provide for your family. | 我们所有人都知道工作的重要性， 不仅仅是为了一份薪水， 也为了心灵的平 静， 这份平静是随着你能供养自己的家庭而来。 |
| 9 | merit pay | /ˈmerit pei/ | n. | extra pay awarded to an employee on the basis of merit | 绩效工资 | Microsoft Thursday announced it would do away with merit pay increases for employees in the next fiscal year. | 微软周四宣布下一个财年将不会提高员工的绩效工资。 |
| 10 | unpaid | /ʌnˈpeid/ | adj. | without payment | 未付款的； [税收] 未缴纳的； 无 报酬的 | Around 15% of employees said they had either accepted a pay freeze or deliberately not requested a pay rise， while 14% had taken unpaid leave. | 大约15% 的员工表示， 他们要么接受薪水冻结， 要么刻意不提加薪要求， 而14% 已经接受无薪假期。 |
| 11 | income tax | /ˈinkʌm tæks/ | n. | a personal tax levied on annual income | 所得税 | Everyone with an income should pay income tax equal to a modest percentage of his income. | 任何有收入的人都应当支付一笔与之相称的适当百分比的所得税。 |
| 12 | take-home | /ˈteikˈhəum/ | adj. | （of salary or wages） remaining after all deductions including taxes （ | 工资扣税等后） 实得的 | These calculators will show you your gross pay， how much goes to taxes and how much you’ll be left with， which is also known as net， or take-home pay. | 这些计算器会告诉你有多少工资总额、 交多少税、 剩下多少， 或者说工资净 额、 实得工资。 |
| 13 | managerial | /ˌmænəˈdʒiəriəl/ | adj. | of or relating to the function or responsibility or activity of management | 经理的； 管理上的 | He has also had managerial experience on every level. | 他还有各个层级的管理经验。 |
| 14 | net | /net/ | adj. | remaining after all deductions | 纯粹的； 净余的 | This report offers a handy guide to how long it takes a worker on the average net wage to earn the price of a Big Mac in 73 cities. | 这份报告提供了一种简单的说明， 统计了在 73 个城市里， 一般净工资收入 水平的工人需要工作多长时间才能赚到一个巨无霸的钱。 |
| 15 | fine | /fain/ | n. | money extracted as a penalty | 罚款 | He was fined ￥ 500 and banned from driving for one month. | 他被罚款500 元， 而且一个月禁止开车。 |
| 16 | minimum wage | /ˈminiməm weidʒ/ | n. | the lowest wage that an employer is allowed to pay， determined by contract or by law | 最低工资 | In less developed countries， employers frequently pay workers below the minimum wage， and some employers do not pay their workers at all. | 在不发达的国家， 雇主常付给工人低于最低工资的薪水， 而且有些雇主根 本不给他们的工人支薪。 |
| 17 | hourly | /ˈauəli/ | adj. | occurring every hour or payable by the hour | 每小时 的， 以钟点计算的 | hourly wage | 小时工资， 时薪 You’re just spreading a slightly higher average hourly wage over a longer period， so it seems like you’re taking a lower wage in exchange for more days off. 只是将你的略微高的平均小时工资分布在一段较长的时间， 所以看起来你用 较低的工资换得更多的休假。 |
| 18 | monthly | /ˈmʌnθli/ | adj. | occurring every month or payable by the month | 每月的， 每月一次的； 有效期为一个月的 | monthly wage | 月工资， 月薪 I get a monthly wage of ￥ 5000. 我每月工资是5000 元。 |
| 19 | piecework | /ˈpiːswək/ | n. | work paid for according to the quantity produced | 计件工作 | The quality rank piecework wage system which means wage depends on the key performance indication， such as output， quality and so on， was carried out in the production workshop. | 生产车间执行的是与产量、 质量等主要业绩指标挂钩的质量等级计件工 资制。 |
| 20 | ceiling | /ˈsiːliŋ/ | n. | an upper limit on what is allowed； the overhead upper surface of a room | 上限； 天花板 | U. S. should copy Switzerland and consider a “wage ceiling ” ratio， too. The ratio means to cap executive pay at 12 times what the lowest-paid worker at a company makes. | 美国应该仿效瑞士， 也考虑 “最高工资” 比例。 这个比例意味着， 高管的收 入被限制在公司最低工资员工收入的12 倍以内。 |

### Unit 23 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | probation | /prəˈbeiʃən/ | n. | a trial period during which your character and abilities are tested to see whether you are suitable for work or for membership； the testing or trial of a person’s conduct， character， qualifications， or the like | 试用； 试用期 | Employee appointment to the council will be subject to a term of probation of 6 months. | 到该理事会任职须经过6 个月的试用期。 |
| 2 | promotion | /prəˈməuʃən/ | n. | advancement or act of raising in rank or position | 提升， 晋升； 推销 | We all know that Mr. Smith is requesting promotion. | 我们都知道史密斯先生在申请升职。 |
| 3 | congratulation | /kənˌgrætjuˈleiʃən/ | n. | an expression of pleasure at the success or good fortune of another | 祝贺； 贺词 | People crowded round the manager with congratulations on his promotion. | 人们簇拥着那个经理， 向他祝贺升职。 |
| 4 | demonstrate | /ˈdemənstreit/ | vt. | to provide evidence for or stand as proof of； to show by one’s behavior， attitude， or external attributes | 证明； 展 示； 论证 | She demonstrated a new car to customers in the department store. | 她在百货公司向顾客示范驾驶一辆新汽车。 |
| 5 | recommend | /ˌrekəˈmend/ | vi. | to present as worthy of confidence， acceptance， use， etc.； to commend； to mention favorably | 推荐； 建议 | I recommend that you ask for promotion as soon as possible. | 我建议你尽快提出升职。 |
| 6 | career | /kəˈriə/ | n. | a person’s progress or general course of action through life or through a phase of life， as in some profession or undertaking | 职业 | The training program will be tailored to an employee’s ultimate career objective. | 此培训课程将有助于员工实现职业生涯的最终目标。 |
| 7 | progress | /ˈprəugres/ | n. | gradual improvement or growth or development | 进步， 发展； 前进 | I will keep you posted about the progress of my next project. | 我将把我的下一个项目的进展情况汇报给你。 |
| 8 | accomplish | /əˈkɔmpliʃ/ | vt. | to gain with effort； to bring to its goal or conclusion | 完成 | It has never been her boast that she alone could accomplish the work. | 她从没有夸口说她能单独完成那项工作。 |
| 9 | elect | /iˈlekt/ | vt. | to select by a vote for an office or membership | 选举； 选 择； 推选 | I’m so glad that you were elected the leader. | 我真高兴你被选为领导了。 |
| 10 | director | /diˈrektə， daiˈrektə/ | n. | the manager or chief executive of certain schools， institutes， government bureaus， etc.； someone who controls resources and expenditures | 主任， 主管； 导演 | I’m glad that you were promoted to director of the department， and you’ve been a great asset to our department. | 我真高兴你晋升为部门总监了。 你一直是我们部门的栋梁之材。 |
| 11 | post | /pəust/ | n. | a job in an organization | 岗位； 职位 | We hope you’re successful in your new post in the treasury. | 我们祝你在新的财务岗位上成功。 |
| 12 | position | /pəˈziʃən/ | n. | situation or condition， especially with relation to favorable or unfavorable circumstances | 职位， 岗位 | Your personality will help you climb up positions in work. You have always been persistent and responsible. | 你的性格有助于你在工作中升职。 你一直很有毅力和责任心。 |
| 13 | persistent | /pəˈsistənt/ | adj. | constantly repeated； persisting， especially in spite of opposition， obstacles， discouragement， etc. | 固执的， 坚持 的； 持久稳固的 | We intend to utilize this knowledge to focus future efforts on overcoming persistent challenges and obstacles in business. | 我们计划利用这一知识， 在今后侧重于努力克服工作上持续的挑战和障碍。 |
| 14 | responsible | /risˈpɔnsəbl/ | adj. | answerable or accountable， as for something within one’s power， control， or management （often followed by to or for） | 负责的， 可靠的； 有责任的 | You are too young for such a responsible position. | 对于这样一个责任重大的职位， 你太年轻了。 |
| 15 | overpay | /ˌəuvəˈpei/ | vt. | to pay more than is necessary or reasonable | 多 付给 （某人） 钱， 给 （某人） 过高报酬 | Has Jack been overpaid for his work？ | 杰克的工作报酬是否过高？ |
| 16 | nominate | /ˈnɔmineit/ | vt. | to propose （someone） for appointment or election to an office； to appoint to a duty or office | 推荐； 提名 | If your company offers recognition for extraordinary contributions， ask your boss to nominate you. | 如果你们公司设有特殊贡献奖的话， 让你的上司提名你参选。 |
| 17 | seniority | /ˌsiːniˈɔrəti/ | n. | higher rank than that of others especially by reason of longer service | 年长； 职位高； 资历 | Influenced by old ideas， some units assign posts according to seniority. | 由于受旧观念的影响， 有些单位分配工作按资排辈。 |
| 18 | acting | /ˈæktiŋ/ | adj. | serving temporarily， especially as a substitute during another’s absence； not permanent | 代理的 | The new acting manager has a reputation of being someone who is independent. | 这位新的临时代理经理有极有主见的名声。 |
| 19 | solution | /səˈluːʃən/ | n. | the act of solving a problem， question， etc. | 解 决方案 | You look for the solution. That is why your promotion does not surprise me. People like you are valuable in all senses. | 你总是寻求解决方案。 这就是为什么你的升职一点儿也不让我惊讶。 从各方 面来说， 像你这样的人是很有价值的。 |
| 20 | devotion | /diˈvəuʃən/ | n. | an assignment or appropriation to any purpose， cause， etc. | 献身， 奉献； 忠诚 | You put passion and devotion to all you do， and all that will make you get even further. Getting a promotion is only a small step. | 你对你所做的一切工作都付出热情和奉献， 这一切将使你更进一步。 获得晋 升只是很小的一步。 |

## Unit 24　Welfare Benefits 员工福利待遇

### Unit 24 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
