| 1 | wellbeing | /ˌwelˈbiːiŋ/ | n. | welfare； a state characterized by health， happiness， and prosperity | 幸福； 福利； 安乐 | And more importantly， this sustainability plan must thread through every area of the organization， including employee wellbeing. | 更重要的是， 可持续发展计划必须贯穿组织的各个领域， 包括雇员的福利 待遇。 |
| 2 | reward | /riˈwɔːd/ | n. | something that you are given because you have behaved well， worked hard， or provided a service to the community | 报 酬； 报答； 酬金 | Current employment laws will be changed to reward effort and punish laziness. | 现行雇佣法要变， 要奖勤罚懒。 |
| 3 | betterment | /ˈbetəmənt/ | n. | a change for the better； progress in development； the act or process of bettering | 改善， 改进； 改良 | His application of Internet technology is for the betterment of mankind. | 他的网络技术应用是为了人类生活的改善。 |
| 4 | vacation | /vəˈkeiʃən； veiˈkeiʃən/ | n. | a period of suspension of work， study， or other activity， usually used for rest， recreation， or travel | 假期； （房屋） 搬出 | She dreamt about paid vacation plans when she should have been working. | 她在本应工作时胡思乱想着带薪度假计划。 |
| 5 | welfare | /ˈwelfɛə/ | n. | financial or other assistance to an individual or family from a city， state， or national government； similar assistance from company | 福利； 幸福； 福利事业 | Their talk ran upon the problems of company welfare and security system. | 他们的谈话涉及公司福利和保障制度问题。 |
| 6 | allowance | /əˈlauəns/ | n. | a sum of money allotted or granted for a particular purpose， as for expenses | 津贴， 零用钱； 允许 | His company provided him with an allowance when he was studying in the English-speaking country. | 他的公司为他在英语国家期间的学习提供了津贴。 |
| 7 | subsidy | /ˈsʌbsidi/ | n. | a direct pecuniary aid furnished by a government to a private industrial undertaking | 补贴； 津贴； 补助金 | Workers in the Los Angeles are planning a massive demonstration against financial subsidy cuts. | 洛杉矶的工人们正在策划一场反对削减财政补贴的大游行。 |
| 8 | insurance | /inˈʃuərəns/ | n. | coverage by contract in which one party agrees to indemnify or reimburse another for loss that occurs under the terms of the contract | 保险； 保险费； 赔偿金 | I balanced the benefits against the costs of medical insurance. | 我权衡了医疗保险的好处和它的花费。 |
| 9 | release | /riˈliːs/ | n. | a freeing or releasing from confinement， obligation， pain， emotional strain， etc. | 释放； 发布 | Under the agreement， release pay will be cut by about 10 percent for the staff who were employed after 2013. | 根据这个协议， 2013 年以后雇佣的员工的遣散费将被削减10% 左右。 |
| 10 | title | /ˈtaitl/ | n. | a word that is used before someone’s name in order to show their status or profession， such as ‘Mr.’， ‘Mrs.’， or ‘Doctor’ | 称呼； 职称 | professional and technical titles | 专业技术职务 She has been awarded the title Professor. 她已被授予教授职称。 |
| 11 | check | /tʃek/ | n. | a written order directing a bank to pay money | 支票 | He wrote his boss a check， but it was bounced. | 他开了一张支票给他的老板， 但是被退票了。 |
| 12 | period | /ˈpiəriəd/ | n. | a round of time marked by the recurrence of some phenomenon or occupied by some recurring process or action | 周期， 期 间； 时期 | The pay period at that company is 7 days. | 那家公司的工资周期是7 天。 |
| 13 | accumulation | /əˌkjuːmjəˈleiʃn/ | n. | the collecting together of things over a period of time | 堆积； 积累； 积聚 | I want to buy a house recently， so I should carry with housing accumulation fund. | 我最近想买房， 要提用住房公积金。 |
| 14 | leave | /liːv/ | n. | permission to be absent， as from work or military duty | 许 可， 同意； 休假 | Why don’t you take a few days’ unpaid leave？ | 你为什么不休几天无薪假？ |
| 15 | tax | /tæks/ | n. | （1） n. a sum of money demanded by a government for its support or for specific facilities or services vt. to pay a part of one’s income or profits to the government | 税金 （2） 征税 | Husband and wife may be taxed separately on their incomes. | 丈夫和妻子可能会分别被征收所得税。 |
| 16 | severance pay | /ˈsevərəns pei/ | n. | compensation paid by an organization to an employee who leaves because， through no fault of his own， the job to which he was appointed ceases to exist | 遣散费 | In Austria severance pay is due to anyone employed for more than three years. | 在奥地利， 任何就业三年以上的雇员都有解雇费。 |
| 17 | endowment | /inˈdaumənt/ | n. | the capital that provides income for an institution | 捐赠； 捐助； 捐款 | The company donates to the school endowment fund every year. | 该公司每年向学校的捐赠基金捐款。 |
| 18 | treatment | /ˈtriːtmənt/ | n. | the management of someone or something； action or behavior toward a person， animal， etc. | 处理； 对待 | We don’t want any special treatment. | 我们不需要任何特殊待遇。 |
| 19 | automobile | /ˈɔːtəməbiːl，ˌɔːtəˈməubil/ | n. | a motor vehicle， with four wheels， usually propelled by an internal combustion engine | 汽车 | Some multinational companies offer automobile subsidies to senior members on the management team. | 有些跨国公司给管理层的成员提供汽车补贴。 |
| 20 | housing | /ˈhauziŋ/ | n. | the providing of houses for a group or community | 房屋； 住房供给 | The headquarters has allocated funds for housing subsidy to the branch. | 总部已为该分公司的住房补贴拨付资金。 |

### Unit 24 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | team | /tiːm/ | n. | a number of persons associated in some joint action | 团队 | All the employees on the team were told to say they had social insurance and to express satisfaction with their residences and environment. | 团队里所有的员工被告知他们要回答有社会保险， 且同时要表达出对住宅和 环境的满意。 |
| 2 | unemployment | /ˌʌnimˈplɔimənt/ | n. | the state of being unemployed or not having a job | 失业； 失业率； 失业人数 | unemployment insurance | 失业保险 Your unemployment insurance will become effective from the third week of your being out of work. 你的失业保险将从你失业的第三周起生效。 |
| 3 | injury | /ˈindʒəri/ | n. | any physical damage to the body caused by violence or accident or fracture etc. | 伤害， 损害； 受伤处 | employment injury insurance | 工伤保险 The results show that education， job security， employment injury insurance and self perception influence their job satisfaction significantly. 结果显示教育状况、 工作环境中的安全状况、 工伤保险和自我认知状况等会 显著地影响工作满意度。 |
| 4 | retirement | /riˈtairmənt/ | n. | the state of being retired from one’s business or occupation | 退休， 退役 | retirement insurance | 退休保险 Social Security is a type of retirement insurance paid by almost all American workers and employers. 社会保险是一种几乎所有美国工人和雇主都会投保的退休保险。 |
| 5 | direct | /diˈrekt， daiˈrekt/ | adj. | proceeding without deviation or interruption； straight and short | 直接的； 直系的 | Direct financial compensation is an employee’s base wage. It can be an annual salary， hourly wage or any performance-based pay that an employee receives， such as profit-sharing bonuses. | 直接经济报酬是一个员工的基本薪水。 它可以是年薪、 时薪或者任何一个员 工会得到的绩效薪水， 比如分红奖金。 |
| 6 | indirect | /ˌindiˈrekt， ˌindaiˈrekt/ | adj. | having intervening factors or persons or influences | 非直接的 | Value-added taxes， sales taxes， and import duties are indirect taxes. | 增值税、 营业税和进口税都是间接税。 |
| 7 | external | /iksˈtəːnl/ | adj. | happening or arising or located outside or beyond some limits or especially surface | 外部的； 表面的； 外面的 | The aim of salary management is to obtain internal and external equity of salary system. | 薪酬管理的目标是达到薪酬体系的内部公平和外部公平。 |
| 8 | internal | /inˈtəːnl/ | adj. | happening or arising or located within some limits or inside | 内部的； 内在的； 国内的 | The subject of our meeting was internal organization and personnel. | 我们谈话的主要内容是内部组织机构和人事安排。 |
| 9 | factor | /ˈfæktə/ | n. | one of the elements contributing to a particular result or situation | 因素； 要素； 因数 | In modern monetary economy， the financial factor is exerting an increasingly greater influence on the development of national economy. | 在现代货币经济中， 金融因素对国民经济发展的影响日益显著。 |
| 10 | earn | /əːn/ | vt. | to receive money in return for work that one does | 赚取， 挣得 | Players should be allowed to earn money from advertising. | 应当允许运动员拍广告挣钱。 |
| 11 | follower | /ˈfɔləuə/ | n. | a person who accepts the leadership of another | 追 随者； 属下； 拥护者 | This means that you must be happy to be a follower rather than a leader， especially in a business setting. | 这就意味着你必须乐于成为一个跟随者， 而不是领导者， 特别是在商务事宜方面。 |
| 12 | classification | /ˌklæsifiˈkeiʃən/ | n. | a group of people or things arranged by class or category | 分类； 类别， 等级 | This kind of classification is not exhaustive. | 这种分类法并不是包罗无遗的。 |
| 13 | comparison | /kəmˈpærisn/ | n. | relation based on similarities and differences | 比较； 对照； 比喻 | factor comparison method | 因素比较法 Approaches for post evaluation mainly include ranking method， classification method， factor comparison method， point scoring method， Hay group， etc. 岗位评价方法主要有排序法、 分类法、 因素比较法、 积点评分法、 海氏法等。 |
| 14 | pay | /pei/ | n. | money given， usually in exchange for goods or services | 工 资， 薪水； 付款 | If this happens to you， or happens to other people in the company who are at your pay grade， this should set off some warning bells. | 如果这一切发生在你身上， 或者是发生在与你同样薪酬等级的其他同事身 上， 这就可以算作某种征兆了。 |
| 15 | range | /reindʒ/ | n. | an area in which something acts or operates or has power or control | 范围； 幅度； 排 | Therefore， I’d expect a salary at the high end of your pay range for the position classification. | 所以， 我希望的是一份属于这个职位工资范围里最高的工资。 |
| 16 | ownership | /ˈəunəʃip/ | n. | the relation of an owner to the thing possessed； possession with the right to transfer possession to others | 所 有权； 物主身份 | Obviously， one way to do it is to actually make them owners， for example， with an employee stock ownership plan. | 很显然， 这样做的一个办法就是真正使雇员成为企业主 （老板）， 例如， 实 施员工入股计划。 |
| 17 | competitive | /kəmˈpetətiv/ | adj. | be less expensive than other goods or services of the same kind | 竞争的； （商品或服务） 具有竞争力的 | Qualified candidates will be offered with highly competitive benefit package. | 对于合格录取者提供优厚的待遇。 |
| 18 | sharing | /ˈʃɛəriŋ/ | n. | using or enjoying something jointly with others | 公 用， 共享 | The company uses profit sharing schemes instead of bonuses to tie people to the organization goals. | 该公司使用分享利润的方式代替个人红利， 以团结大家去完成团队目标。 |
| 19 | cafeteria | /ˌkæfiˈtiəriə/ | n. | a restaurant where you serve yourself and pay a cashier | 自助餐厅 | A “cafeteria ” benefits plan allows employees to withhold a portion of their pretax salary to cover certain medical or child-care expenses. | 自助计划允许员工预留一部分税前收入来支付某些医疗或者育儿开销。 |
| 20 | maternity | /məˈtəːnəti/ | adj. | the state of being pregnant； the period from conception to birth when a woman carries a developing fetus in her uterus | 产科的； 孕妇的 | The new Social Insurance Law consolidates pension， medical， work-injury， unemployment and maternity insurance programs. | 新的 《社会保险法》 将养老、 医疗、 工伤、 失业和生育保险项目整合在 一起。 |

## Unit 25　Rules & Regulations 公司规章制度

### Unit 25 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | confidentiality | /ˌkɔnfiˌdenʃiˈæləti/ | n. | being spoken， written， acted on， etc.， in strict privacy or secrecy | 机密， [计] 机密性 | We clearly conclude that any of authorized staff or third party should obey the confidentiality responsibility established by our company. | 我们议定所有授权经办之同事或第三人均需遵守本公司规定的保密责任。 |
| 2 | disciplinary | /ˈdisiplinəri/ | adj. | relating to discipline in behavior | 训诫的； 规律的； 训练的 | “The disciplinary commission is an independent organ，” said the company spokesman. “It will decide if the case is of interest. ” | 该公司发言人说： “纪律委员会是一个独立的机构， 它会决定一些涉及利益 的问题。” |
| 3 | appeal | /əˈpiːl/ | n. | a serious and urgent request； a formal request for a decision to be changed | 呼吁， 请求； 上诉 | Their appeal for help went unheeded. | 他们求助的呼吁未受到注意。 |
| 4 | referral | /riˈfəːrəl/ | n. | the act of referring （as forwarding an applicant for employment or referring a matter to an appropriate agency） | 参照； 提及； 被推举的人 | Whenever someone gives you a referral， get back to them and let them know the outcome. | 只要有人介绍客户给你， 都要再跟他们联系， 让他们知道结果。 |
| 5 | harassment | /ˈhærəsmənt/ | n. | a feeling of intense annoyance caused by being tormented | 骚扰； 烦恼 | The parent company in the United States will not tolerate harassment or any other form of misconduct. | 美国的母公司不能容忍骚扰或任何其他形式的不正当行为。 |
| 6 | rule | /ruːl/ | n. | something regarded as a normative example [ | 数] 规则； （pl. ） 条例， 规章制度 | But the new rules have been criticized because they do not include punishments for those who choose to ignore them. | 但是这些新规定遭到了批评， 因为它们没有包括对那些忽视规定的人做出 惩处。 |
| 7 | regulation | /ˌregjuˈleiʃən/ | n. | a principle or condition that customarily governs behavior | 条例； 规程； 章则 | As per company regulation， any paid employee won’t have the right of authorship. | 根据公司规定， 拿了薪水的员工， 不得拥有署名权。 |
| 8 | enforce | /inˈfɔːs/ | vt. | to ensure observance of laws and rules | 实施， 执行； 强迫， 强制 | The agency will have broad authority to write and enforce rules on products from mortgages to credit cards to payday loans. | 该局在针对抵押贷款、 信用卡、 短期贷款等一系列产品制定、 实施监管规则 方面， 将拥有广泛的权力。 |
| 9 | violate | /ˈvaiəleit/ | vt. | to act in disregard of laws， rules， contracts， or promises | 违反； 侵犯， 妨碍 | The decision comes just days after the Obama administration announced that it would step up enforcement against companies that violate rules on anti-competitive behavior. | 就在这一处罚决定宣布的几天之前， 奥巴马政府发布了关于加大对违反竞 争法规的公司的执法力度的声明。 |
| 10 | accounting | /əˈkautiŋ/ | n. | a system that provides quantitative information about finances | 会计， 会计学 | In selecting an accounting policy， an enterprise always considers about the contracts with each related groups， especially the contracts with the stockholders， creditors and government. | 企业在进行会计政策选择时， 常常是基于与各利益相关方合约的考虑， 特 别是与股东、 债权人及政府合约的考虑。 |
| 11 | reimbursement | /ˌriːimˈbəːsmənt/ | n. | compensation paid （to someone） for damages or losses or money already spent | 退还， 偿还； 报销 | Other employees care more about advanced training with an associated certificate of accomplishment or diploma or tuition reimbursement. | 其他员工更关心高阶培训， 能获得相关的证书或文凭或学费报销政策。 |
| 12 | misconduct | /misˈkɔndʌkt/ | n. | bad or unacceptable behavior， especially by a professional person | 不端行为； 处理不当 | Although whistle blowers are protected under federal law from employer retaliation for misconduct， there have been cases where punishment for whistle blowing has occurred. | 尽管爆料员工受到雇主报复不当行为的相关联邦法律的保护， 惩罚这些爆 料的人的案例曾有发生。 |
| 13 | defy | /diˈfai/ | vt. | to refuse to obey someone or something and behave in a particular way | 违抗； 反抗； 不服从 | Many people have been arrested for defying the ban on street trading. | 有很多人因为违抗禁止在街上摆摊交易的禁令而被捕。 |
| 14 | expense-report system | /ikˈspens riˈpɔːt ˈsistəm/ | n. | a mechanism to declare business expenses by employees themselves； expenditure declaration system | 报销制度 | Automation has vastly changed the expense-report system， forcing travelers to more-strictly comply with company policies. | 自动化已经让报销制度发生了巨大变化， 迫使出差的人更严格地遵守公司的 规章制度。 |
| 15 | training | /ˈtreiniŋ/ | n. | activity leading to skilled behavior | 培训 | McDonalds expects the DS training system to save money by cutting training time by half compared to traditional methods， partly because of how familiar employees will already be with using a DS. DS | 培训系统比传统培训方式缩短了一半的培训时间， 麦当劳期望能够因此 来节约经费， 也部分是因为员工们已经十分熟悉 DS 的使用了。 |
| 16 | subordinate | /səˈbɔːdinət/ | adj. | （1 ） adj. lower in rank or importance； inferior in status n. a person who has a less important position than you in the organization that you both work for | 级别或职位较低的； 下级的 （2） 部属； 部下， 下级 | If you had a subordinate who failed to delegate authority in his department， what would you do？ | 如果你有一个部属在他的部门中授权失败， 你应该怎么办？ |
| 17 | key account training | /kiː əˈkaunt ˈtreiniŋ/ | n. | an organizational training process used by business-to-business suppliers to manage their relationships with strategically-important customers and， in turn， to produce measurable business benefits | 大客户培训 | Key account training includes the areas of relationship building， business etiquette， face to face selling and HR expertise. | 大客户培训包括大客户关系建设、 商务礼仪、 面对面销售和人力资源知识 等领域。 |
| 18 | budget | /ˈbʌdʒit/ | n. | the amount of money that a person， organization， or country has available to spend on it | 预算 | Once we have a working budget system， we can focus on methods to pay off the deficit. | 一旦我们有了可操作的预算制度， 我们就能把重点放在偿清债务的方法上。 |
| 19 | attendance management | /əˈtendəns ˈmænidʒmənt/ | n. | the act of managing of employees’ presence in a work setting to minimize loss due to employee downtime | 考勤管理 | Attendance Management is an integral part of effective human resources management. | 考勤管理是有效人力资源管理的重要环节。 |
| 20 | structure | /ˈstrʌktʃə/ | n. | the manner of construction of something and the arrangement of its parts | 制度 | If a firm wants to adopt a new wage and salary structure， it is essential that the firm should decide on a method of job evaluation and ways of measuring the performance of its employees. | 公司如果想采用新的工资和薪水制度， 那么确定工作评估的方法和衡量雇员 业绩的办法就必不可少。 |

### Unit 25 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | applicable | /ˈæplikəbl/ | adj. | applying or capable of being applied； relevant； suitable； appropriate | 可适用的； 可应用的； 合适的 | This ensures that the exercise of governance and decision-making conforms to all organizational policies and principles， and also to any applicable regulations and laws. | 这确保了治理和决策制定的实行符合所有的组织政策和原则， 也符合所有适 用的规章和法律。 |
| 2 | conform | /kənˈfɔːm/ | vi. | to act in accord with the prevailing standards， attitudes， practices， etc.， of society or a group | 符合； 遵照 | It is a solid， proven solution for adopting multiple standards — whether your goal is to conform to the law or to improve your business practices. | 它是一个坚固的、 已经证实采用多个标准的解决方案， 不管你的目标是遵守 法律还是改善你的商业实践。 |
| 3 | supervise | /ˈsjuːpəvaiz/ | vt./vi. | to oversee （a process， work， workers， etc.） during execution or performance； to superintend | 监督， 管理； 指导 | They are being rolled out in workplaces， allowing employees in disparate locals to communicate more easily and letting managers supervise employees from afar. | 它们在工作场所推出， 使身在不同地点的员工能够更轻松地交流， 并让管理 者在远处监管员工。 |
| 4 | precaution | /priˈkɔːʃn/ | n. | an action that is intended to prevent something dangerous or unpleasant from happening | 预防， 防备； 预防措施 | Employees should take the necessary safety precautions before attempting to clean the machinery. | 员工在清扫机器之前， 必须采取必要的防护措施。 |
| 5 | affair | /əˈfɛə/ | n. | anything done or to be done； anything requiring action or effort； business； concern | 事情； 事务； 私事 | Since that is the affair in your department， we do not interfere with your problems. | 既然那是你们部门的事情， 那么我们不愿意干涉你们的问题。 |
| 6 | retain | /riˈtein/ | vt. | to secure and keep for possible future use or application | 保持； 雇 | They strive to retain access to the resources that make company tick. | 他们设法获取公司赖以运转的资源。 |
| 7 | stipulate | /ˈstipjuleit/ | vt. | to specify as a condition or requirement in a contract or agreement； to make an express demand or provision in an agreement | 规定； 保证 | The rules do not stipulate， however， precisely which risks must （or can） be assessed， nor how much evidence is sufficient to justify a particular measure. | 然而这些规则并没有明确规定必须 （或可能） 评估哪些风险， 或者多少证 据才能充分证明一个特殊措施的合理性。 |
| 8 | fulfillment | /fulˈfilmənt/ | n. | the process or business of handling and executing customer orders， as packing， shipping， or processing checks | 履行； 实行 | Businessmen prefer to deal with those whose background seemed to increase the likelihood of the successful fulfillment of agreements. | 商人更喜欢和那些背景看起来能增加成功履行合约可能性的人做生意。 |
| 9 | lateness | /ˈleitnəs/ | n. | occurring， coming， or being after the usual or proper time | 晚， 迟 | They said their efficiency at work was reduced by about 40 percent and reported a high frequency of accidents， lateness and absenteeism. | 他们说， 他们的工作效率下降了40% ， 而且意外事故、 迟到和旷工的发生频 率也很高。 |
| 10 | absenteeism | /ˌæbsənˈtiːizəm/ | n. | frequent or habitual absence from work， school， etc. | 旷工； 旷课； 无故缺席 | The benefits to companies of adopting flextime， the report says， “can outweigh the costs by reducing absenteeism， lowering turnover， improving the health of workers， and increasing productivity. ” | 该报告指出： 采用弹性工作时间的公司获得的收益， “会超出其成本支出， 这是由于旷工减少、 人员流动率下降、 员工健康状况改善以及生产力提高。” |
| 11 | detain | /diˈtein/ | vt. | to keep from proceeding； to keep waiting | 拘留； 留住 | No entity or individual shall confiscate or detain any motor vehicle plate. | 任何单位和个人不得收缴、 扣留机动车号牌。 |
| 12 | penalty | /ˈpenəlti/ | n. | a punishment imposed or incurred for a violation of law or rule； a payment required for not fulfilling a contract | 罚款， 罚 金； 处罚 | However， the new rule does not list any penalty provisions for violators， which arouses doubts over how the new regulation will be implemented. | 然而， 该新规定并没有列出任何对违规者的处罚， 这使人们怀疑这项新规定 将如何实施。 |
| 13 | outsourcing | /ˈautˌsɔːsiŋ/ | n. | the action of obtaining goods or services from an outside supplier； the process of contracting work out | 外包； 外 购； 外部采办 | One of the major reasons， after all， that company is outsourcing its operations to Asian countries is for the labor costs. | 毕竟， 这家公司将其业务外包到亚洲的主要原因之一就是为了减少劳动力 成本。 |
| 14 | compensatory | /kəmˈpensətəri/ | adj. | serving to compensate， as for loss， lack， or injury | 补偿的， 赔偿的 | He also suggested that this is one of the compensatory mechanisms. | 他也提出， 这是一种补偿的机制。 |
| 15 | prevail | /priˈveil/ | vi. | to be or prove superior in strength， power， or influence （usually followed by over） | 盛行， 流行； 战胜， 获胜 | The pursuit of maximum profit seems destined to prevail. | 追逐利润最大化似乎注定将会盛行。 |
| 16 | deem | /diːm/ | vt. | to hold as an opinion； to think； to regard | 认为， 视作； 相信 | We do not have any supply chain problems， but more time is needed before we deem the operation a success. | 我们认为供应链本身没有什么问题， 但要确认该项目是否成功还需要一段 时间。 |
| 17 | termination | /ˌtəːmiˈneiʃən/ | n. | a coming to an end of a contract period | 结束， 终止 | The right of the party concerned to claim for a compensation of the loss / damage shall not be affected by any modification， rescission， termination of the contract. | 合同的变更、 解除或终止， 不影响当事人要求赔偿损失的权利。 |
| 18 | hereunder | /hiəˈʌndə/ | adv. | in a subsequent part of this document or statement or matter etc. | 在此之下； 在下面 | No failure by either party to enforce any rights hereunder shall be construed a waiver. | 任何一方不履行其在本协议下的权利不应被视为弃权。 |
| 19 | submission | /səbˈmiʃən/ | n. | presenting sth. for consideration， a decision， etc. | 投降； 提交 （物） | I demand the submission of the signature to an expert. | 我要求把签名提交专家鉴定。 |
| 20 | beforehand | /biˈfɔːhænd/ | adv. | （1） adv. ahead of time； in anticipation adj. being ahead of time or need | 事先； 预先 （2） 提前的； 预先准备好的 | Have you got any examples of very successful marketing tools， marketing campaigns you started from the beginning without knowing beforehand whether they would be successful or not？ | 你有过什么非常成功的营销手段的例子吗？ 那些在一开始的时候并不会事 先知道它们能否成功的例子？ |

## Unit 26　Sighing a Contract 签订合约

### Unit 26 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | secure | /siˈkjuə/ | vt. | to get by special effort | 争取到； 弄到 | Shu also bribed some officials to secure a lucrative contract for a French firm used as an intermediary for his high-tech company. | 舒还向某些官员行贿， 为一家法国公司争取一份有利可图的合同， 而这家法 国公司是他的高科技公司的中间商。 |
| 2 | honor | /ˈɔnə/ | vt. | to accept as pay | 履行； 信守 | If a company does not honor the contract， an employee can report the situation and the company might be removed from the list next year. | 如果哪家公司不履行合同规定， 雇员就可以报告该情况， 那么明年该公司就 可能从名单中删除。 |
| 3 | annul | /əˈnʌl/ | vt. | to declare invalid | 取消； 废除； 宣告无效 | The contract was annulled by both parties. | 该合同被双方取消了。 |
| 4 | abide by | /əˈbaid bai/ | v. | to act in accordance with someone’s rules， commands， or wishes | 遵守； 信守； 承担……的后果 | It goes without saying that both parties must abide by the contract terms that we have agreed upon and signed. | 不用说， 双方都必须遵守已经同意并已签署过的合约条款。 |
| 5 | obligation | /ˌɔbliˈɡeiʃən/ | n. | the state of being obligated to do or pay something | 义务， 责任 | A labour contract is an agreement that defines the labour relations between workers and employers as well as the rights and obligations of the two parties. | 劳动合同是劳动者与用人单位确立劳动关系、 明确双方权利和义务的协议。 |
| 6 | execute | /ˈeksikjuːt/ | vt. | to carry out or perform an action | 实行； 执行 | We require you to compensate us with an amount of losses totaling $ 500， 000 caused by your failure to execute the contract and with all the expenses arising from this arbitration. | 我方要求你方赔偿由于不履行合同义务所造成的损失 50 万美元， 并承担由 于进行仲裁而产生的一切费用。 |
| 7 | break | /breik/ | vi. | to act in disregard of laws， rules， contracts， or promises； to terminate | 打破； 弄坏 | Study the contract thoroughly and carefully， scrutinize your duties and what happens if you break the contract. | 深入细致地研究合同条文， 仔细审读你所承担的责任， 细心审查假若你违 背合同将会产生什么结果。 |
| 8 | sign | /sain/ | vt./vi. | to approve and express assent， responsibility， or obligation； to be engaged by a written agreement | 签署； 签名 | If you sign a contract with the government for millions of dollars， you have guarantee to get your dollars back， plus whatever profits they allowed in the contract. | 如果你和政府签订了合约， 做了一个百万的大单子， 你能保证拿到钱， 和其 他， 在合同里规定的利润。 |
| 9 | breach | /briːtʃ/ | n. | a failure to perform some promised act or obligation | 违 反 | HP sued Hurd on September 7， alleging breach of contract， and threatened misappropriation of trade secrets. | 惠普于9 月7 日控告赫德违反合同， 并指控他盗用商业秘密。 |
| 10 | countersign | /ˈkauntəsain/ | vt. | to add one’s signature to after another’s to attest authenticity | 会签； 副署； 确认 | If you should find any questions， please notify at once. If not， please countersign the contract and return us one copy by next mail for our files. | 如果你们发现有任何问题， 请立即告知， 否则， 请会签合同并立即退回一份 供我方存档。 |
| 11 | authorize | /ˈɔːθəraiz/ | vt. | to give or delegate power or authority to | 授权， 批准， 委托 | She authorized her assistant to sign the papers. | 她授权自己的助手去签这些文件。 |
| 12 | binding | /ˈbaindiŋ/ | adj. | executed with proper legal authority | 有约束力 的； 捆绑的 | The agreement is binding on both parties. | 协议对双方具有约束力 |
| 13 | completion | /kəmˈpliːʃ（ə）n/ | n. | a concluding action | 完成， 结束； 实现 | The completion date of the subcontract works shall be Oct. 1st， 2018. | 转包合同完成的日期为2018 年10 月1 日。 |
| 14 | tear up | /teə ʌp/ | v. | to cancel or annul | 取消 （合同） | Many a foreigner escaped bankruptcy only because he would tear up a contract rather than go into court to enforce his rights. | 许多外国人免于破产的厄运只是因为他愿意撕毁合约， 而不上法庭去主张他 的权利。 |
| 15 | arbitration | /ˌaːbiˈtreiʃən/ | n. | the hearing and determination of a dispute by an impartial referee agreed to by both parties | 公断， 仲裁 | During the arbitration， the contract shall be executed continually by both parties except for the matters in disputes. | 在仲裁过程中， 除双方有争议的部分外， 本合同应继续履行。 |
| 16 | draw up | /drɔː ʌp/ | v. | to make up plans or basic details for | 草拟， 起草 | They agreed to establish a working party to draw up a formal agreement， for instance a contract. | 他们同意成立一个工作组来起草一份正式协议， 比如， 一份合同。 |
| 17 | renewal | /riˈnju（ː）əl/ | n. | an official increase of a document such as a licence or a contract in the period of time for which it remains valid | 更 新， 恢复； 续约 | The contract is up for renewal. | 该合同正考虑续订。 |
| 18 | terminate | /ˈtəːmineit/ | vt./vi. | to bring to an end or halt （ | 使） 终止； （使） 结束 | Either party has the right to terminate the contract by a written notice. | 任何一方有权书面通知对方终止本合同。 |
| 19 | interpretation | /inˌtəːpriˈteiʃən/ | n. | explanation | 解释； 翻译 | This contract is written both in Chinese and English. For purposes of meaning or interpretation of the contract， the English language shall govern. | 本合同以中英文两种文字书写， 但有关本合同的含义及解释以英语合同 为准。 |
| 20 | stipulation | /ˌstipjuˈleiʃən/ | n. | an agreement or concession made by parties in a judicial proceeding （or by their attorneys） relating to the business before the court | 规定； 约定； 条款 | In order to avoid subsequent amendments， please see to it that the L / C stipulations conform to the terms of the contract. | 为避免以后的改正， 请务必使信用证的规定和合同条款相一致。 |

### Unit 26 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | endorsement | /inˈdɔːsmənt/ | n. | the signature， instructions， etc.， placed on the reverse of a commercial document， for the purpose of assigning the interest therein to another | 背书； 签注 （文件） | This insurance contract is consisted of insurance clauses， insurance application， insurance policy， certificate of insurance and endorsement on policy. | 本保险合同由保险条款、 投保单、 保险单、 保险凭证以及批单组成。 |
| 2 | withdraw | /wiðˈdrɔː/ | vt. | to remove something or take it away； to take money out of a bank account | 撤走； 拿走； （从银行） 取 （钱） | We would rather withdraw the draft resolution than amend it. | 与其修改草案， 不如予以撤回。 |
| 3 | amend | /əˈmend/ | vt. | to alter， modify， rephrase， or add to or subtract from by formal procedure | 修改； 改善， 改进 | The contract shall take effect after signed by both parties. During the period of execution， any of party can not amend or cancel the contract at random. | 本合同自双方签字之日起生效， 合同执行期内， 甲乙双方均不得随意变更或 解除合同。 |
| 4 | revoke | /riˈvəuk/ | vt. | to take back or withdraw； to annul， cancel， or reverse | 撤回， 取消； 废除 | The parties with the signing of this contract hereby mutually revoke all previous agreements which affect the contents of this contract. | 双方签署的这项合约撤销所有在此以前双方协议的涉及本合同的内容。 |
| 5 | irrevocable | /iˈrevəkəbl/ | adj. | not to be revoked or recalled； unable to be repealed or annulled； unalterable | 不可改变的； 不能取消的； 不能挽回的 | Coca-Cola said it has accepted irrevocable undertakings from Huiyuan’s three shareholders for acceptance of the offers， in aggregate representing approximately 66 percent of the Huiyuan shares. | 可口可乐称他们已经接受了来自汇源三大股东的不可撤销承诺， 即他们已 经接受了本次出价， 总计代表将近66% 的汇源股权。 |
| 6 | definite | /ˈdefinit/ | adj. | clearly defined or determined； not vague or general | 一定的； 确切的 | The deputy chairman hemmed and hawed without giving a definite answer. | 副主席说话吞吞吐吐， 没有给出明确的答复。 |
| 7 | ratify | /ˈrætifai/ | vt. | to confirm by expressing consent， approval， or formal sanction | 批准； 认可 | The heads of the two governments met to ratify the treaty. | 两国政府首脑举行会晤， 签署这项条约。 |
| 8 | void | /vɔid/ | adj. | having no value or authority | 空的； 无效的； 无用的 | The court ruled that the claim was null and void. | 法庭裁决此项要求无效。 |
| 9 | dimension | /diˈmenʃən/ | n. | the magnitude of something in a particular direction | 方面； [数] 维； 尺寸 | There is a dimension to the problem that we have not discussed. | 这问题还有一方面我们没讨论过。 |
| 10 | hinder | /ˈhində/ | vt./vi. | to cause delay， interruption， or difficulty in； to hamper； to impede | 阻碍， 妨碍； 成为阻碍 | The policy will promote rather than hinder reform. | 这项政策将促进而不是妨碍改革。 |
| 11 | consortium | /kənˈsɔːtjum/ | n. | an association of companies for some definite purpose | 财团； 联合； 合伙 | If a consortium wins the bid， the parties thereto shall jointly enter into a contract with the tenderer and assume joint and several liability over the winning project to the tenderer. | 联合体中标的， 联合体各方应当共同与招标人签订合同， 就中标项目向招标 人承担连带责任。 |
| 12 | original | /əˈridʒənl/ | n. | a work， writing， or the like， as opposed to any copy or imitation | 原件； 正本 | These are two originals of the contract we prepared. We’ll sign two originals， each in the Chinese and English language. | 这是我们准备好的两份合同正本。 我们将要用中文和英文分别签署两份原件。 |
| 13 | jurisdiction | /ˌdʒuərisˈdikʃən/ | n. | the right， power， or authority to administer justice by hearing and determining controversies | 司法权， 审 判权， 管辖权 | In some matters， particularly in contract and tort actions， the jurisdiction of the county courts is unlimited if both parties agree to the action being tried in a county court. | 在某些事务中， 尤其是契约和侵权之诉， 如果双方都同意将案件交到郡法院 审理， 那么郡法院对此案件的管辖权就不受限制。 |
| 14 | counterpart | /ˈkauntəpaːt/ | n. | a person or thing having the same function or characteristics as another； a duplicate copy （ | 契约、 合同） 副本， 复制品； 相对物 | This contract may be executed simultaneously in two or more counterparts via telex or facsimile transmission， each of which shall be deemed as originals and legally binding. | 传真和电传的合同副本 （两份或多份） 均视为有效版本， 与原件同等对待 并具有法律约束力。 |
| 15 | inspection | /inˈspekʃn/ | n. | a formal or official examination | 检查， 检验 | inspection clause | 验收条款 How will the inspection clause be stipulated in the contract is matter of importance. 检验条款在合同中应如何规定， 这一点很重要。 |
| 16 | memorandum | /ˌmeməˈrændəm/ | n. | a short note designating something to be remembered， especially something to be done or acted upon in the future； reminder | 备忘录； 便笺 | Amendments and changes are to be recorded in a memorandum or written document signed by both parties. | 修改和变化应以双方签字的备忘录或书面文件形式记录在案。 |
| 17 | existing | /igˈzistiŋ/ | adj. | being in a specified place or under certain conditions； present | 目前的； 现存的 | This is a good time to discuss issues in the existing contract that need to be addressed， such as remediation in the event of a lapse of service. | 此时也是一个很好的时机去深入讨论现有合同中所存在的问题， 比如服务失 效时的补救措施。 |
| 18 | duration | /djuəˈreiʃən/ | n. | the period of time during which something continues | 持续， 持续的时间， 期间 | Normally， the contract duration is two years and renewable for a further two years if we both agree. | 通常来说， 合同期限为两年， 如双方同意， 到期可再续签两年。 |
| 19 | license | /ˈlaisns/ | n. | （1） n. a legal document giving official permission to do something vt. to give official permission for the person to do something or for the activity to take place | 许可证， 执照； 特许 （2） 同意； 发许可 证 | Under the agreement， the council can license a U. S. company to produce the drug. | 根据协议， 委员会可授权一家美国公司生产这种药。 |
| 20 | clause | /klɔːz/ | n. | a separate section of a legal document （as a statute or contract or will） | 条款 | When the consumers demand a repair， change， or return of large commodities under a warranty clause， the business operators should be responsible for the transportation charges reasonably incurred. | 对包修、 包换、 包退的大件商品， 消费者要求经营者修理、 更换、 退货的， 经营者应当承担运输等合理费用。 |

## Unit 27　Telephone Calls 接打电话

### Unit 27 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | code | /kəud/ | n. | a group of numbers or letters which is used to identify something， such as a mailing address or part of a telephone system | 代 码； 号码 | I’d like to place a person-to-person call to Chicago. The number is 9328647， but I don’t know the area code. | 我想打个叫人电话到芝加哥， 电话号码是9328647， 但我不知道区号。 |
| 2 | conference call | /ˈkɔnʃərəns kɔːl/ | n. | a telephone call in which more than two people participate | 会议电话 | “I think it is something the government and bank industry need to look at， ” he said in a conference call with analysts across the nation. | 他在面向全国的分析师的电话会议上表示： “我认为政府和银行业需要对这 一问题予以关注。” |
| 3 | crossed lines | /krɔst lainz/ | n. | crossed lines happen when the accidental placement of a cable into the wrong plug on the switchboard rather than some static interference | 电话干扰 | We’ve been having trouble with your area because of crossed lines. One moment， and I’ll try and get the number for you. | 您所在的地区的电话经常串线。 请等一会， 我试试给您接通正确的电话。 |
| 4 | exchange | /iksˈtʃeindʒ/ | vt. | to hand over one and receive another， approximately equivalent | 交换； 交易； 兑换 | During the exhibition， they had exchanged mobile phone numbers with the person sitting opposite to you. | 在展会上， 他们与坐在你对面的人交换了手机号码。 |
| 5 | long distance call | /lɔŋ ˈdistəns kɔːl/ | n. | a telephone call made outside a defined local area， usually to another city | 长途电话 | How can I make a direct long distance call from this phone in our office？ | 在咱们办公室， 我用这部电话怎么打直拨长途电话？ |
| 6 | mouthpiece | /ˈmauθpiːs/ | n. | the part of a telephone into which a person speaks | 传话机； 话筒 | She covered the mouthpiece of the phone with her hand. | 她用手遮住电话的话筒。 |
| 7 | engaged | /inˈgeidʒd/ | adj. | （of telephones） unavailable for use by anyone else or indicating unavailability | 使用中的， 忙碌的； 占线的 | I couldn’t get the general manager in that company as the line was engaged. | 因电话占线， 我没能接通那个公司总经理的电话。 |
| 8 | connect | /kəˈnekt/ | vt./vi. | to join by means of communication equipment | 连接； 联合； 关联 | The telephone company finally put in lines to connect the towns in this area. | 电话公司终于把这个地区的所有城镇用电话连接起来了。 |
| 9 | collect call | /kəˈlekt kɔːl/ | n. | a telephone call that the receiving party is asked to pay for | 对方付费电话 | As I had no money with me， I made a collect call. | 因为身上没钱， 所以我打了一个由对方付款的电话。 |
| 10 | return | /riˈtəːn/ | vt./vi. | to answer back | 返回； 回应 | When is a good time to have him return your call？ | 他什么时候回电话给你比较好？ |
| 11 | put through | /put θruː/ | v. | to connect by telephone | 接通 （电话）； 使穿 过； 使从事 | Could you put through a collect call for me？ | 你能为我接一个对方付费电话吗？ |
| 12 | message | /ˈmesidʒ/ | n. | a communication （usually brief） that is written or spoken or signaled | 消息； 启示 | Through the website the youth can see the different videos， comment on them， leave message on the chat board and participate on online polls. | 通过该网站， 年轻人可以观看不同的影片、 评论影片、 在留言板上留信息、 参与线上意见调查。 |
| 13 | outside | /ˌautˈsaid/ | adj. | functioning outside the boundaries or precincts of an organized unit | 外面的， 外部的； 外来的 | Dial 9 for an outside line， and then the number you are calling. If you want to contact anyone in the office， just dial their extension. | 打外线时先拨9， 然后再拨你要拨打的电话号码。 假如你是和公司里的人通 话， 那么只要拨他们的分机号码就行了。 |
| 14 | ring | /riŋ/ | vi. | to get or try to get into communication （with someone） by telephone | 按铃； 敲钟 | Mr. Smith called while you were out and asked if you will ring him back. | 你出去时， 史密斯先生打来电话， 他问你是否能给他回电话。 |
| 15 | correspond | /ˌkɔrisˈpɔnd/ | vi. | to be compatible， similar or consistent； to coincide in their characteristics | 符合， 一致； 通信 | She still corresponds with business partners she met in Hawaii nine years ago. | 她依然和9 年前在夏威夷遇到的商业伙伴保持联系。 |
| 16 | call back | /kɔːl bæk/ | v. | to return or repeat a telephone call | 再打电话； 回电话 | Christopher， I have very limited time. Could you reorganize your points？ And I’ll call back at 4 30. | 克里斯托弗， 我的时间有限， 你能把内容再组织一下吗？ 我 4 30 会给你打 电话的。 |
| 17 | telephone | /ˈtelifəun/ | vt./vi. | to get or try to get into communication （with someone） by phone | 打电话 | I couldn’t telephone my boss because his telephone line was engaged. | 我不能给老板打电话， 因为他的电话占线了。 |
| 18 | emergency | /iˈməːdʒənsi/ | n. | a sudden unforeseen crisis （ usually involving danger） that requires immediate action | 紧急情况； 突发事件； 非常时刻 | Luckily， he really knew what to do in an emergency. | 幸运的是， 他真的知道在紧急情况下要做什么。 |
| 19 | respond | /risˈpɔnd/ | vi. | to show a response or a reaction to something | 回答； 作出反应 | We were quite aware （ of ） how you would respond to our terms. | 我们十分清楚你们对我们提出的条件肯定会有什么反应。 |
| 20 | reach | /riːtʃ/ | vt. | to establish communication with someone | 达到； 抵达； 找到 | For example， you can route calls from family members and colleagues to reach you on your mobile， office or home phone， while sending other callers to your voicemail or blocking them. | 例如， 你可以将家人和同事的电话发送到手机、 办公室或住宅电话上， 而将 其他电话转入语音信箱或屏蔽掉。 |

### Unit 27 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | bother | /ˈbɔðə/ | vt. | to cause inconvenience or discomfort to | 烦扰， 打扰； 使……不安； 使……恼怒 | I have enough to contend with； don’t bother me with your problems. | 我的事都应付不过来了， 别再拿你的事麻烦我了。 |
| 2 | recording | /riˈkɔːdiŋ/ | n. | signal encoding something （e. g. ， picture or sound） that has been recorded | 录音； 唱片 | Do you remember how to operate this recording machine to leave a message？ | 你记得怎样使用这个录音机来留言吗？ |
| 3 | beep | /biːp/ | n. | a short high tone produced as a signal or warning | 哔哔的 声音； 警笛声 | I’m not in the office right now. Please leave a message after the beep. | 我现在不在单位， 请在哔的一声之后开始留言。 |
| 4 | answering | /ˈaːnsəriŋ/ | adj. | replying | 应答的； 回应的 | When Sandra returned from vacation， she told me that she had a message from Jack on her answering machine. | 当桑德拉度假回来， 她告诉我她已经在电话答录机上接到杰克的来电。 |
| 5 | incoming | /ˈinkʌmiŋ/ | adj. | coming in； arriving | 进来的， 到来的； 即将 就任的 | One of its features is to route an incoming call to a succession of numbers until it is answered. | 它的一项功能是将来电按顺序转接至一系列号码， 直到被接听为止。 |
| 6 | outgoing | /ˈautgəuiŋ/ | adj. | leaving a place or a position | 出发的， 外出的； 即将离职的 | When placing an outgoing call on a multi-line phone， many times you will need to choose a line by pushing a button. | 当在多线电话上使用外线时， 常常需要通过按一个键。 |
| 7 | slow down | /sləu daun/ | v. | to lose velocity； to move more slowly； to become slow or slower | 减速， 放慢速度 | You speak a little fast； slow down， please. | 你说得有点儿快， 请说慢点儿。 |
| 8 | get through | /get θruː/ | v. | to be in or establish communication with | 接通 电话； 度过， 熬过 （困难时期等） | Will my email messages get through to the inbox of the clients if I can’t get through on the phone？ | 我的电子邮件信息是否能到达用户的收件箱， 如果我不能在电话上找到 他们？ |
| 9 | signal | /ˈsignl/ | n. | any communication that encodes a message | 信号； 暗 号； 导火线 | I’ve been trying to call Mr. Dickinson， but I keep getting a busy signal. | 我不停地打电话给迪肯森先生， 但是我总是得到电话忙音。 |
| 10 | await | /əˈweit/ | vt. | to look forward to the probable occurrence of | 等候， 等待； 期待 | I’ll await his return call. | 我会等他的来电。 |
| 11 | receiver | /riˈsiːvə/ | n. | earphone that converts electrical signals into sounds | 电话听筒； 接收器 | Lucas picked up the receiver and started to dial the number in Florida， the United States of America. | 卢卡斯拿起听筒， 开始拨打在美国佛罗里达的电话。 |
| 12 | bill | /bil/ | n. | statement of charges for service | 账单 | The shop uses the post office to ship its coffee beans to customers， running up a typical monthly bill of $ 500. | 这家商店会用邮寄的方式将咖啡豆送到顾客手中， 平时一个月的费用大约是 500 美元。 |
| 13 | hang up | /hæŋ ʌp/ |  | to put a telephone receiver back in its cradle | 挂上电 话 | Always say goodbye politely before you hang up the phone. | 别忘了在挂断电话之前要和对方礼貌地说再见。 |
| 14 | voicemail | /ˈvɔismeil/ | n. | a computerized system for answering and routing telephone calls | 语音信箱 | Calls to your number are automatically forwarded to voicemail if you do not answer within 30 seconds. | 如果你三十秒内未接电话， 来电会自动转到语音信箱。 |
| 15 | rental | /ˈrentl/ | n. | the act of paying for the use of something （as an apartment or house or car or venue） | 租金收入， 租金； 租赁 | The meeting room rental fee will be charged as per our contract， right？ | 会议室的租金按照我们的合同算， 对不对？ |
| 16 | line | /lain/ | n. | a telephone connection | 电话线； 路线， 航线 | Hold the line， please. Let me have a check. Yes， there is a single room available. | 请不要挂断电话， 我去查看一下。 我们还有一个单人间。 |
| 17 | arrangement | /əˈreindʒmənt/ | n. | the thing arranged or agreed to | 布置； 整理； 准备 | I’ll make the arrangement with Mr. Lincoln at 412-220-4433. | 我会跟电话是412-220-4433 的林肯先生协调安排的。 |
| 18 | complete | /kəmˈpliːt/ | vt. | to come or bring to a finish or an end | 完成 | To participate， customers must first fill out a personality survey that typically takes an hour or more to complete. | 若要参与， 客户必须先填写一份个性调查表， 一般要花上一个多小时。 |
| 19 | note down | /nəut daun/ | v. | to write something down quickly， so that you have a record of it | 记录某事； 记下某事 | If you note down in the beginning all that you need to perform these promotion activities， you will not have to redo the list every time. | 如果你一开始就把执行营销活动所需要的信息都记录下来， 就不用每次都麻 烦的重列清单了。 |
| 20 | transfer | /trænsˈfəː/ | vt./vi. | to send from one person or place to another （ | 使） 转移； 调任 | I am afraid this is a direct line. We cannot transfer your call to the purchasing department. Could you dial 2234-1156， please？ | 我很抱歉， 这是直接电话。 我们没有办法为您转接采购部。 请您改拨 2234- 1156， 好吗？ |

## Unit 28　Feelings， Emotions， Attitudes， & Sensations

### Unit 28 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | affection | /əˈfekʃən/ | n. | tender feeling towards another； fondness | 感情； 钟爱 | Zack found it hard to show affection towards those for whom he cared. | 扎克发现， 他很难向他关心的人表达他的情感。 |
| 2 | anticipate | /ænˈtisipeit/ | vt./vi. | to think likely to happen； to look forward to； to act in advance of ； to consider or make use of before the proper time | 预见； 期待； 在……之前行动； 预先考虑； 提前使用 | I don’t know what my test score is， but I anticipate that the professor will let us know with a day or two. | 我不知道我考多少分， 但是我预期教授会在一两天之内公布我们的成绩。 |
| 3 | brag | /bræg/ | vt./vi. | to talk too proudly about oneself， one’s possessions， etc.； to boast | 自夸； 吹嘘 | After a while， Jim’s friends couldn’t stand his incessant bragging any more. | 听多了之后， 吉姆的朋友们就受不了他永无止息的吹牛了。 |
| 4 | delighted | /diˈlaitid/ | adj. | very pleased or satisfied | 欣喜的； 愉快的 | I would be delighted to have you and your family over for dinner Friday night. | 如果你和你的家人能在星期五晚上到寒舍用晚餐， 我会非常高兴。 |
| 5 | detest | /diˈtest/ | vt. | to dislike intensely； to hate very much； to loathe | 憎恶； 痛 恨； 嫌恶 | As an animal rights activist， Ryan detested all forms of violence against animals. | 身为一个动物保护积极分子， 莱恩痛恨任何形式的虐待动物。 |
| 6 | disgust | /disˈgʌst/ | n. | （1 ） n. a strong and often sick feeling of dislike， aversion， or repugnance vt. to cause a feeling of disgust in | 作呕； 厌恶； 反感 （2） 导致反感 | I am so disgusted when I see mothers smoking cigarettes while feeding their little babies！ | 每当我看到一些母亲在喂小孩喝奶时还一面抽烟， 就觉得非常反感！ |
| 7 | distress | /disˈtres/ | n. | great suffering of the mind or body； suffering caused by lack of money； a state of danger or great difficulty | 悲痛； 贫困； 危难 | The radio on the ship has been picking up a distress signal non-stop for an hour， but we can’t seem to pinpoint where it is coming from. | 船上的无线电在过去一个小时中不断收到求救信号， 但是我们却无法确切找 到信号来源。 |
| 8 | enthusiastic | /inˈθjuːziˌæstik/ | adj. | having or showing intense interest | 热心 的； 狂热的 | We are looking for an enthusiastic supervisor who can keep the whole team excited about the project. | 我们正在寻找一位满怀热忱能使整个小组对本专案感到兴致盎然的主管。 |
| 9 | esteem | /isˈtiːm/ | n. | （1） n. favorable opinion； respect vt. to value highly； to respect and admire | 好评； 尊敬 （2） 极看重； 敬重 | The fireman told us that if there is ever a fire， we should only take the things we esteem to be of the utmost value. | 救火员告诉我们， 如果有火灾发生， 我们应该只拿我们视为最重要的东西。 |
| 10 | fascination | /ˌfæsiˈneiʃən/ | n. | the state of being attracted or enchanted； powerful attraction； charm | 入迷； 着迷； 强烈吸引力； 魅力 | I don’t understand his fascination with bugs， but if it makes him happy he can sit out in the backyard all day and dig for all the critters that he wants. | 我不了解他为什么对甲虫如此着迷， 但是如果他高兴的话， 他可以整天待在 后院挖他想要的任何生物。 |
| 11 | gloomy | /ˈgluːmi/ | adj. | dark or dim； dismal or depressing； dejected or melancholy； hopeless or despairing | 幽暗的； 令人沮丧的； 忧郁的； 没有 希望的 | Looking out into the overcast and gloomy sky， he grabbed his coat and cursed the coming rain. | 看着外面阴沉晦暗的天色， 他抓起外套并诅咒即将来临的大雨。 |
| 12 | impartial | /imˈpaːʃəl/ | adj. | not giving special favor or support to any one side； without bias； unprejudiced； fair | 不偏袒的； 无偏见的； 公正的 | The judges were impartial to either team， so we had a fair game. | 裁判们对两队都不偏袒， 所以我们的比赛是公平的。 |
| 13 | impulsive | /imˈpʌlsiv/ | adj. | tending to act suddenly without thinking | 冲 动的 | The fact that he just went out and shot the dog showed that he was very impulsive. | 他就这样跑出去将狗射杀的事实说明他很冲动。 |
| 14 | irritated | /ˈiriteitid/ | adj. | angered， provoked， or annoyed； inflamed or made painful， as a part of the body | 被激怒的； 发炎的； 疼痛的 | The skin on the palm of his hand had become red and irritated from the constant friction caused by the rope. | 他手心的皮肤已经因为绳索不停地磨擦而变红发痛。 |
| 15 | mad | /mæd/ | adj. | mentally ill； insane； infatuated； foolish； angry | 发疯的； 疯狂的； 着迷的； 愚蠢的； 生气的 | The naughty children made the nanny very mad. | 调皮的小孩们使得保姆非常生气。 |
| 16 | objective | /əbˈdʒektiv/ | n. | （1） n. an aim or goal adj. uninfluenced by emotion or personal prejudice | 目标 （2） 客观的 | The USA did not have any clear objectives when they engaged in the Vietnam War and thus suffered a gigantic loss. | 美国在参加越战时并没有明确的目标， 所以才会蒙受重大损失。 |
| 17 | panic | /ˈpænik/ | n. | （1 ） n. a sudden， unreasoning， and quick-spreading fear or anxiety vt. /vi. to （cause to ） feel panic （ | 恐慌 （2） 使） 恐慌 | The crowd panicked when the whole building started to shake. | 当整幢楼开始晃动时， 人群产生了恐慌。 |
| 18 | prejudice | /ˈpredʒudis/ | n. | an unfair and unfavorable opinion or feeling formed beforehand or without knowledge， thought， or reason | 偏见； 歧视 | As much as many Americans would like to say that they have overcome racial hatred， there is still a lot of prejudice within their country. | 即使很多美国人喜欢说他们已经克服种族间的仇恨， 但在他们国内其实还是 有很多歧视的情况存在。 |
| 19 | repulsive | /riˈpʌlsiv/ | adj. | very unpleasant； causing very strong dislike； revolting ； disgusting | 使人反感的； 令人厌恶的 | I find such behavior absolutely repulsive； I would like to ask you to leave immediately！ | 我觉得这样的行为极度令人厌恶， 请你马上离开！ |
| 20 | tranquility | /trænˈkwiliti/ | n. | quality or state of being calm， quiet， or peaceful； serenity | 平静； 安宁 | I like hiking in the woods because of the tranquility and peace that I feel there. | 我喜欢到树林里徒步行走， 因为在那儿我感觉平静而安详。 |

### Unit 28 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | ambition | /æmˈbiʃən/ | n. | a strong desire for success， power， wealth， etc. | 雄心； 野心 | With unbridled ambition， Madonna climbed the pop music charts to become one of the most successful female musicians in history. | 有着无限野心的麦当娜在流行音乐榜上屡创佳绩， 成了史上最成功的女音乐 家之一。 |
| 2 | attraction | /əˈtrækʃən/ | n. | the action or power of getting attention； something that draws attention | 吸引 （力）； 吸引人之物 | For a relationship to work out between two people， there has to be social compatibility as well as physical attraction. | 感情要能有结果， 两人不但要门当户对， 身体上还得相互吸引对方。 |
| 3 | contentment | /kənˈtentmənt/ | n. | the state or fact of being satisfied | 满意； 满足 | Ecstatic about his move to Oakland， Billy Joe wrote a song of his newfound contentment called Welcome to Paradise. | 兴高采烈地搬到了奥克兰， 比利·乔写了一首名为 《欢迎来到天堂》 的歌 来表达他对新发现的满意之情。 |
| 4 | despair | /disˈpɛə/ | n. | complete loss of hope； something that causes the loss of hope | 绝望； 使人绝望的事物 | His despair was simply overwhelming after losing his friend and his dog in the same month. | 在一个月内同时失去他的朋友和狗后， 他哀痛欲绝。 |
| 5 | devoted | /diˈvəutid/ | adj. | very loving， loyal， or faithful； zealous or ardent | 挚爱的； 忠实的； 热忱的 | Although her boyfriend was half a world away， Tami remained completely devoted to him and turned down all date invitations. | 虽然她的男朋友跟她相隔半个地球， 泰咪还是对他十分忠实， 谢绝了所有 约会的邀请。 |
| 6 | disposition | /ˌdispəˈziʃən/ | n. | a particular tendency of character， behavior， etc.； nature； temperament | 倾向； 性格； 气质； 性情 | No matter what happens， he always has such a cheery disposition and never seems to get discouraged or disheartened. | 不管发生什么事， 他总是一直保持愉快的态度， 似乎从来不会感到气馁或灰心。 |
| 7 | elegant | /ˈeligənt/ | adj. | having the qualities of grace and beauty； tasteful； excellent | 高雅的； 有品位的； 上等的 | All the elegant evening gowns worn by Princess Diana were made with the most wonderful fabrics. | 戴安娜王妃所穿的高雅的晚礼服都是用最高级的衣料剪裁的。 |
| 8 | entice | /inˈtais/ | vt. | to tempt with hope of reward or pleasure； to lure or seduce | 诱惑； 引诱 | The luxurious lifestyle of the city entices a lot of young people away from the countryside. | 都市里奢华的生活方式诱使许多年轻人离开农村。 |
| 9 | fancy | /ˈfænsi/ | adj. | elaborate in design； of superior grade； decorative or brightly colored； （of a price） higher than usual or reasonable | 精心设计 的； 特级的； 花哨的； 昂贵的 | The Queen Anne chairs that they have in their dining room seem too fancy for their house on the beach. | 他们把安妮皇后时期风格的椅子摆设在海滨度假屋的餐厅里显得太华丽了。 |
| 10 | fondness | /ˈfɔndnis/ | n. | the state or quality of being tender and loving； doting affection； a liking or weakness for something | 钟爱； 溺爱； 嗜好； 癖好 | Will you look back on the time we spent together with fondness？ | 你会以欢喜的心情看待我们过去一起分享的时光吗？ |
| 11 | hideous | /ˈhidiəs/ | adj. | extremely ugly and/or shocking to the senses； repugnant； repulsive | 丑得吓人的； 不忍卒睹的； 丑恶的； 可憎的 | The new skin treatment she was using caused her face to break out with hideous red sores. | 她使用的新皮肤治疗法使她的脸冒出很多可怕的红疹。 |
| 12 | impression | /imˈpreʃən/ | n. | an image or effect that is produced in the mind by a person， event， experience， etc.； a not very clear feeling or idea about something | 意念； 印象； 不太明确的感觉或想法 | Although it wasn’t stated， Jill got the impression that Tom was infatuated with her. | 虽然没有说明， 但是吉儿似乎感觉到汤姆非常迷恋她。 |
| 13 | instinct | /ˈinstiŋkt/ | n. | （a） natural ability or tendency to act in a certain way， without having to learn or think about it | 天性； 本能； 直觉 | Canaries can pick the right building materials for the perfect nest by instinct. | 金丝雀能够靠本能选择最适合用来筑巢的材料。 |
| 14 | jealous | /ˈdʒeləs/ | adj. | feeling resentful because of another’s success， advantage， etc.； wanting to have what someone else has； wanting to keep what one has | 嫉妒的； 羡慕的； 唯恐失去的 | If other men talk to his wife， he gets terribly jealous. | 只要别的男人和他老婆说话， 他就醋劲大发。 |
| 15 | nasty | /ˈnæsti/ | adj. | disgustingly dirty； filthy； morally offensive； indecent； mean； malicious； very unpleasant； severe | 肮脏的； 不道德 的； 恶毒的； 令人不愉快的； 严重的 | The movie critic didn’t like the stars in that film， so his remarks about how they acted were extremely nasty. | 这个电视评论家并不喜欢那部电影里的演员， 所以对他们演技的评论极为 恶毒。 |
| 16 | optimistic | /ˌɔptiˈmistik/ | adj. | disposed to take a favorable view of events or conditions and to expect the most favorable outcome | 乐观的 | According to the doctor， her condition is very serious， but let’s try to be optimistic about things until we know for sure what is going to happen. | 根据医生的说法， 她的情况非常严重， 但我们应该保持乐观的态度， 直到我 们确切知道以后的发展如何。 |
| 17 | perceive | /pəˈsiːv/ | vt./vi. | to become aware of through the senses； to have knowledge of； to understand | 感知； 察觉； 理会 | Illusions are effective in deceiving our senses as they allow our bodies to perceive what is not actually there. | 幻觉很容易混淆我们的感官， 因为它使我们的身体感知到实际上并不存在的东西。 |
| 18 | provoke | /prəˈvəuk/ | vt. | to make angry； to irritate； to stir up action or feeling | 激怒； 引起； 激起 | Although many wild animals may seem cute and harmless， it would be wise not to provoke them in any way. | 虽然很多野生动物看来既可爱又无害， 但最好还是不要招惹它们。 |
| 19 | startle | /ˈstaːtl/ | vt./vi. | to surprise， frighten， or alarm； to cause to make a sudden， involuntary movement， as in fright | 惊吓； 使吓一跳 | The dog’s sudden emergence from under a bush startled the whole band of burglars. | 那只狗突然从灌木丛里窜出来， 吓了这群窃贼一大跳。 |
| 20 | upset | /ʌpˈset/ | vt./vi. | to disturb emotionally or physically； to defeat unexpectedly； to cause to turn over， fall over， or tip over | 使心烦意乱； 使不舒服； 使失败； 打翻 | Rick was upset because Mary turned down his proposal of marriage. | 因为玛丽拒绝他的求婚， 瑞克的心情很不好。 |

## Unit 29　Other Important Nouns & Verbs 其他重要名词及动词

### Unit 29 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | aspect | /ˈæspekt/ | n. | the way one appears； the appearance of something from a specific position or viewpoint； a side facing in a given direction | 面 貌； 方面； 朝向 | The professor said he really liked your thesis， but he never specified which aspect of it interested him most. | 教授说他真的很喜欢你的论文， 但是他没有特别指出他最感兴趣的是哪一个 部分。 |
| 2 | assumption | /əˈsʌmpʃən/ | n. | something taken as a fact or believed to be true without proof | 假定； 假设 | I did this under the assumption that you were going to deliver the package straight to my office. | 我是在认定你会把包裹直接送到我的办公室的前提下做这件事的。 |
| 3 | attempt | /əˈtempt/ | n. | （1） n. an effort made to do something， an attack， as on a person’s life vt. to make an effort at； try to do something， esp. without succeeding | 企图； 攻击 （2 ） 努力尝试； 企图 | He made a very feeble attempt to apologize， which only made things worse in the end. | 他没有诚意的道歉最终只会使得事情越变越糟。 |
| 4 | call waiting | /kɔːlˈweitiŋ/ | n. | a telephone service whereby a person engaged in a telephone call is notified by a tone that a second call is being made to the same number | 电话插播 | You couldn’t have gotten a busy signal because I have call waiting； the call should have gone through. | 我有电话插播， 所以电话不可能占线， 你应该可以打得通呀！ |
| 5 | declaration | /ˌdekləˈreiʃən/ | n. | the act of making known or stating clearly； a formal statement or announcement； proclamation | 宣布； 公布； 宣言 | These events will lead to the declaration of war. | 这些事件将会导致宣战。 |
| 6 | endeavor | /inˈdevə/ | n. | （1） n. an earnest attempt or effort vt. /vi. to make an earnest attempt； to try | 努力； 尽力 （2） 试图； 尝试 | Many scientists have wanted to journey to the center of the earth， but they know that such an endeavor would cost them their lives. | 很多科学家想要到地心去看看， 但他们知道这样的尝试会使得他们丧命。 |
| 7 | exceed | /ikˈsiːd/ | vt. | to be greater than； to surpass； to go or be beyond the limits of | 超过； 超出； 超越 | Their success exceeded all our expectations. | 他们的成功超出我们大家的期待。 |
| 8 | identity | /aiˈdentiti/ | n. | who or what a particular person or thing is； sameness； exact likeness | 身份； 本体； 相同； 同一 | The identity of the murdered man has not been established yet. | 那名遭谋杀的男子身份尚未被确定。 |
| 9 | implication | /ˌimpliˈkeiʃən/ | n. | something suggested or indicated without being explicitly stated； the act of suggesting or indicating indirectly； a possible later effect of an action， decision， etc. | 含义； 暗示； 牵连 | The implications of his recent discoveries have gotten physicians and scientists excited about the possible benefits human beings will gain from his research. | 他的新发现背后所代表的意义使得医生和科学家都为他的研究可能带给人类 的福音感到兴奋不已。 |
| 10 | inspire | /inˈspaiə/ | n. | to encourage or motivate； to stimulate， or impel， as to some creative effort； to arouse （ a thought or feeling） in （someone） | 鼓舞； 激发； 引发 | Martin Luther King Jr. ’s famous I Have a Dream speech inspired many to stand up for the civil rights of African Americans. | 马丁·路德·金的著名演说 《我有一个梦想》 启发了很多人为非裔美籍人 士的民权而奋斗。 |
| 11 | intention | /inˈtenʃən/ | n. | a determination to act in a specified way； purpose | 意图； 目的 | I know he had the best intentions， but what he did was wrong and has caused a lot of problems for the company. | 我知道他是好意， 但是他所做的事仍然是错误的， 而且还给公司制造了不少 问题。 |
| 12 | interrupt | /ˌintəˈrʌpt/ | vt./vi. | to break into （a discussion， etc.） or break in upon （a speaker， etc.）； to break the continuity of | 打断； 中断 | It is really impolite to barge in without knocking and interrupt me while I’m sleeping. | 没有敲门就冲进来并打断我的睡眠是非常无礼的。 |
| 13 | manipulate | /məˈnipjuleit/ | vt. | to operate or manage with skillful use of the hands； to control or influence for one’s own purpose | 操作； 操纵 | He likes to manipulate people into doing things that they don’t want to， and that is why he doesn’t have too many friends. | 他喜欢操控别人去做他们不想做的事， 那也就是为什么他没有什么朋友的 原因。 |
| 14 | obstacle | /ˈɔbstəkl/ | n. | anything that stands in the way； obstruction | 障碍 物； 阻碍 | Amber felt that her three children are an obstacle to her career. | 安珀觉得她的3 个小孩对她的职业生涯是一个障碍。 |
| 15 | priority | /praiˈɔriti/ | n. | the state or right of coming before others in position or time； something that needs attention， consideration， service， etc. before others | 优先 （权）； 优先考虑的事 | Even though he is an entrepreneur， everyone knows that he places his top priority on his family， not on his business. | 虽然他是个大企业家， 但是每个人都知道， 他把家庭放在第一位， 而不是 事业。 |
| 16 | prohibit | /prəˈhibit/ | vt. | to forbid by law or rule； to prevent or debar | 禁止； 防止； 阻止 | I’m sorry， but we prohibit eating and drinking on the train. | 很抱歉， 火车上禁止饮食。 |
| 17 | reassure | /ˌriːəˈʃuə/ | vt. | to comfort and make free from fear or worry； to bring back confidence to； to assure again | 使安心； 使放心； 使恢复信心； 再 保证 | I would like to reassure you that the animals in the cages are harmless and there is no chance that they will ever escape. | 我愿意再一次保证关在笼子里的动物是不会伤人的， 而且他们不可能逃出笼子。 |
| 18 | remind | /riˈmaind/ | vt. | to cause （someone） to remember （a fact， or to do something） | 提醒； 使想起 | Don’t forget to remind me to stop by the bank and deposit this check after we go to the supermarket. | 不要忘了提醒我， 我们去超市后要去银行将这张支票存进去。 |
| 19 | sustain | /səsˈtein/ | vt. | to keep up the strength， spirits， or determination of； to keep in existence over a long period； to hold up； to suffer | 维持； 持续； 支撑； 蒙受 | It was a good thing that he was wearing his helmet and didn’t sustain any severe head injuries. | 还好他戴了安全帽， 头部没有遭受严重的伤害。 |
| 20 | undertake | /ˌʌndəˈteik/ | vt. | to take upon oneself （a task， etc.）； to promise or agree； to guarantee | 承担； 许诺； 保证 | He has decided to undertake a new project of planning and building a hospital in a developing country in South America. | 他已经决定接下一个新的项目， 计划在南美洲的一个发展中国家兴建一座医院。 |

### Unit 29 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | alter | /ˈɔːltə/ | vt./vi. | to make or become different， but without changing into something else | 改变； 修改 | Would you please proofread my report and alter any mistakes you may come across？ | 可以请你校对我的报告并修正你看到的任何错误吗？ |
| 2 | assurance | /əˈʃuərəns/ | n. | a firm statement that something is certainly true or will certainly happen； a promise or guarantee， etc.； confident belief in one’s own ability and power | 保证； 担保； 自信； 把握 | He has given us the assurance that once we arrive in Italy， they will take care of all the sleeping arrangements and transportation. | 他已经跟我们保证， 我们一到意大利， 他们就会负责安排好我们所有的住宿 和交通问题。 |
| 3 | clarify | /ˈklærifai/ | vt./vi. | to make clearer and easier to understand； to make clear and pure | 澄清； 使纯净 | The teacher used more details to clarify the testing procedure to her students. | 老师更详细地对她的学生们说明了考试的程序。 |
| 4 | discard | /disˈkaːd/ | vt. | to get rid of as useless； to throw away | 抛弃； 丢弃 | It is blatant disregard for our environment to casually discard trash on to the sides of the roads. | 在路边随意丢弃垃圾是非常不重视我们环境的行为。 |
| 5 | entitle | /inˈtaitl/ | vt. | to give a name to a book， play， etc.； to give （someone） the right to do or have something | 定名； 使有资格 | The fact that you are my son doesn’t automatically entitle you to all the money I have. | 虽然你是我的儿子， 可是这并不表示我的钱都是你的钱。 |
| 6 | exhibition | /ˌeksiˈbiʃən/ | n. | a public show or display of objects； a showing of feeling， quality， etc. | 展览会； 表现 | The exhibition of vintage cars drew automobile enthusiasts from around the world. | 古董车展吸引了来自世界各地的车迷。 |
| 7 | impact | /ˈimpækt/ | n. | the force of one object hitting another； a strong or powerful influence or effect | 碰撞力； 影响； 行动 | The tree died because of the impact of the car that ran into it. | 这棵树因为那辆车的撞击力量而死去。 |
| 8 | incentive | /inˈsentiv/ | n. | something that encourages one to greater activity； an inducement to do something | 鼓励； 诱因 | The children in my class hardly speak， so I have started bringing candy to class as an incentive for them to respond. | 我班上的孩子很少发言， 所以我就开始带糖果到学校来鼓励他们应答。 |
| 9 | instruct | /inˈstrʌkt/ | vt. | to teach or educate； to give orders or directions to | 教授； 指导； 下命令； 指示 | My father instructed me on how to put the car in gear and slowly proceed into traffic. | 我的父亲指导我如何挂挡并将车子慢慢开上路。 |
| 10 | interfere | /ˌintəˈfiə/ | vi. | to get in the way； to prevent from working or happening； to come between； to meddle | 妨害； 扰乱； 介入； 干涉 | Every time your cellular phone receives an incoming call， it interferes with my computer screen and I have to wait until you’re finished before I can resume. | 每次你的手机收到电话就会干扰到我的电脑屏幕， 我都得等你通话完毕才能 继续使用电脑。 |
| 11 | involve | /inˈvɔlv/ | vt. | to cause （ someone or oneself） to become connected or concerned； （of a situation or action） to have as the people or things taking part； to have as a necessary part or result | 使卷 入； 牵涉； 伴随 （必然的结果） | I don’t like it when people stick their noses into my personal affairs that do not involve them in any way. | 我不喜欢有人干涉我私人的事情， 这些事明明跟他们不相干。 |
| 12 | merit | /ˈmerit/ | n. | the quality of deserving praise， reward， etc.； personal worth； a good quality | 功劳； 价值； 优点 | Do you feel that your school grades have shown enough merit for you to receive the presidential scholarship？ | 你认为你在校的成绩好到有资格来接受总统奖学金吗？ |
| 13 | permit （1） | /ˈpəːmit/ | n. | an official written statement giving one the right to do something； a license pəˈmit/ vt. /vi. to allow （to be or to come）； to make it possible （for a stated thing to happen） | 许可证； 执照 （2） / 准许； 许可 | Since his hunting permit was lost， he ran into some trouble when he was approached by the forest rangers. | 因为他的狩猎证遗失了， 所以当森林管理员来查询时， 他遇到了一些问题。 |
| 14 | proceed | /prəˈsiːd/ | vi. | to begin or continue， esp. after an interruption | 着 手； 继续进行 | The park ranger said that this trail is very steep and dangerous and we would have to proceed at our own risk. | 森林管理员说， 这条小径非常陡峭也很危险， 如果我们继续前进， 就必须自 己承担风险。 |
| 15 | protest （ 1 ） | /ˈprəutest/ | n. | a complaint or strong expression of disapproval， opposition， etc. prəˈtest/ vt. /vi. to speak strongly against； to express strong objection， esp. formally or publicly | 抗议； 反对 （2） / 抗议； 反对 | They have begun to gather outside the courthouse to protest the judge’s decision to let the convicted killer go free. | 他们已经开始聚集在法院外面， 来抗议法官要释放被判有罪的凶手的决定。 |
| 16 | register | /ˈredʒistə/ | n. | （1） n. （a book containing） an official record or list vt. /vi. to put into an official record or list； to enter one’s name on a list | 登记 （簿）； 注册 （簿） （2） 记录； 注册； 登记姓名 | I still haven’t had a chance to register for classes， and I’m afraid the ones I want to sign up for will be full by now. | 我还没机会去注册课程， 恐怕我想要上的课程现在都已经满额了。 |
| 17 | risk | /risk/ | n. | （1） n. the possibility of harm or loss； danger vt. to put in danger； to take the chance of | 风险； 危险 （2） 使遭受危险； 冒……的危 险 | I took a very big risk when I invested my savings in the computer company， but now it seems that my gamble has paid off. | 我冒了很大的风险用我的积蓄投资那家电脑公司， 但现在看来， 我的赌注似 乎是下对了。 |
| 18 | submit | /səbˈmit/ | vt./vi. | to admit defeat； to yield to the control or power of another； to offer for consideration | 认输； 使服从； 提出 | Do not forget that next Tuesday you have to submit your poems to be entered into the literary contest. | 不要忘了， 下个星期二你必须提交你的诗以便参加文学比赛。 |
| 19 | speculate | /ˈspekjuˌleit/ | vt./vi. | to think about or talk about a matter without having the necessary facts； to ponder； to make guesses； to conjecture； to take part in any risky venture （as buying or selling certain stocks， etc.） on the chance of making huge profits | 沉思； 思索； 猜测； 推测； 投机 | It is useless to speculate about his different motives when none of us will ever have any idea as to what he was thinking. | 去揣测他的种种动机是没有用的， 我们之中永远没有人能真的知道他在想 什么。 |
| 20 | verify | /ˈverifai/ | vt. | to prove the truth of； to confirm or substantiate； to test the accuracy of | 证明； 证实； 确认 | Would you please review the application and verify that all the information listed is accurate and then sign at the bottom？ | 请你检查一下申请书， 并在确认所有填写的资料都正确无误后在下方签名 好吗？ |

## Unit 30　Other Important Adjectives & Adverbs 其他重要形容词及副词

### Unit 30 · Part A（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | absurd | /əbˈsəːd/ | adj. | against reason or common sense | 荒谬的； 不合理 的； 违反常理的 | Many Americans find it absurd that President Clinton considers himself an honest and moral president. | 很多美国人觉得， 克林顿总统认为自己是一个诚实有道德感的总统是件很离 谱的事。 |
| 2 | adequate | /ˈædikwit/ | adj. | enough for what is required； sufficient； suitable | 足够的； 适当的 | I don’t know if we have an adequate amount of water for such a lengthy hike. | 我不知道这么长途跋涉我们的水够不够。 |
| 3 | ample | /ˈæmpl/ | adj. | large in size， scope， etc.； more than enough | 广大 的； 宽敞的； 充足的 | I would have ample time to get there if you could extend the deadline for three more hours. | 如果你们能将截止时间延后3 个小时， 我就有足够的时间到达那里。 |
| 4 | decent | /ˈdiːsnt/ | adj. | socially acceptable； conforming to the standards of propriety； proper | 合宜的； 得体的； 适当的 | Miniskirts are not very decent for an occasion like this. | 像这样的场合穿迷你裙不太得体。 |
| 5 | dreadful | /ˈdredful/ | adj. | causing great fear； terrible | 令人恐怖的； 令人害 怕的 | The old man likes to sing the most dreadful songs after he has been at the bar for a while. | 那老人在酒吧喝一阵子酒之后很喜欢唱一些令人觉得恐怖的歌曲。 |
| 6 | exceptional | /ikˈsepʃənl/ | adj. | forming a rare instance； unusual； unusually good | 例外的； 异常的； 特别优秀的 | He has an exceptional talent for drawing； I believe if he works harder， he will become one of the best artists in the world. | 他有极高的绘画天分， 我相信， 如果他更努力的话， 就可以成为世界上最好 的艺术家之一。 |
| 7 | exquisite | /ˈekskwizit/ | adj. | carefully or elaborately done； beautifully made or designed； （of power to feel） sensitive and delicate | 精细的； 精美的； （感觉） 敏锐的； 细致的 | The paper-thin porcelain bowl from the Ming Dynasty is an exquisite piece of art. | 这个薄如纸的明朝瓷碗是一件非常精美的艺术品。 |
| 8 | farfetched | /ˈfaːˈfetʃt/ | adj. | too improbable to be believed or accepted； forced or strained | 牵强的； 不大可信的； 不自然的 | I have listened to a lot of stories in my life， but that has to be the most farfetched tale I have ever heard！ | 我这辈子听过很多故事， 但那个故事可还真是我所听过最离谱的一个！ |
| 9 | in light of | /inˈlait əv/ | adv. | considering； taking into account | 按照； 根据 | In light of the fact that it is pouring outside， I think it would be best to cancel today’s picnic. | 根据外面下着滂沱大雨的情况来看， 我想最好还是取消今天的野餐。 |
| 10 | ingenious | /inˈdʒiːnjəs/ | adj. | showing cleverness at making or inventing things； clever， resourceful， etc. | 有巧思的； 灵敏的； 有创意的 | His plan is so simple and ingenious that it is bound to work. | 他的计划如此简单， 如此有创意， 一定可行。 |
| 11 | instant | /ˈinstənt/ | adj. | happening or produced at once； （of food） that can be very quickly prepared for use | 立即的； （食品） 快速方便的 | Joe took an instant dislike to the girl. | 乔一见到那女孩就不喜欢她。 |
| 12 | legitimate | /liˈdʒitimit/ | adj. | lawful； conforming to accepted rules， standards， etc.； born of parents legally married to each other | 合法的； 正当的； 婚生的 | Before committing yourself to doing this job， you should investigate it further and make sure it is legitimate. | 在你答应做这件工作以前， 你应该进一步调查， 确定它是合法的。 |
| 13 | moderate | /ˈmɔdərit/ | adj. | within reasonable limits； not excessive or extreme； temperate； mild | 中等的； 适度的； 有节制的； 温和的 | If you restrict your fat intake to a moderate amount， you will greatly reduce the risk of a heart attack or other cardiovascular diseases. | 如果脂肪摄取量控制得宜， 你可以大大地降低得心脏病或其他心脏血管疾病 的概率。 |
| 14 | oblivious | /əˈbliviəs/ | adj. | without remembrance or memory； forgetful； unaware or unmindful | 遗忘的； 易忘的； 不注意的； 不在意的 | Completely oblivious to his surroundings， John would do little in class besides daydreaming. | 约翰完全不在意四周的事物， 在课堂上只会做白日梦。 |
| 15 | random | /ˈrændəm/ | adj. | without any particular pattern or purpose； haphazard | 随便的； 无目的的 | I don’t know what is going on， but I’ve been getting random calls all day. | 不知道怎么回事， 我一整天不断接到莫名其妙的电话。 |
| 16 | relevant | /ˈrelivənt/ | adj. | directly connected with the subject； having practical value or importance | 有关的； 切题的； 有实际价值或重要性的 | If you are going to continue to interrupt our conversation， the least you could do is say things that are relevant to our topic. | 如果你打算继续打断我们的谈话， 至少你也该说一些跟我们的主题相关的事。 |
| 17 | substantially | /səbˈstænʃəli/ | adv. | considerably； significantly | 相当大地； 基本上； 本质地 | Some scientists argue that homosexuals have a substantially different DNA makeup from that of heterosexuals. | 有些科学家声称， 同性恋者 DNA 的构造和异性恋者的有很大的不同。 |
| 18 | temporary | /ˈtempərəri/ | adj. | lasting only for a limited time； not permanent | 暂时的； 临时的 | As she stood up too fast， Jasmine experienced temporary dizziness. | 由于起身太快， 所以贾思敏感觉到短暂的晕眩。 |
| 19 | unprecedented | /ˌʌnˈpresidəntid/ | adj. | never having happened before； unheard of | 史无前例的； 空前的 | He won an unprecedented fifth gold medal in the tournament. | 他在锦标赛中史无前例地赢得了第5 块金牌。 |
| 20 | versatile | /ˈvəːsətail/ | adj. | having many different kinds of skill or ability； having many different uses | 多才多艺的； 有多种用途的 | Tom is so versatile that he can do almost anything well. | 汤姆非常多才多艺， 几乎任何事他都能做得很好。 |

### Unit 30 · Part B（20 词）

| # | 词条 | 音标 | 词性 | 英文注释 | 中文注释 | 例句 | 例句译文 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | accurate | /ˈækjurit/ | adj. | exactly correct； free from errors； precise | 准确的； 正确无误的； 精确的 | We need to have everyone write down their addresses and names clearly so we can be accurate when sending out the invitations. | 我们需要每个人清楚地写下自己的地址和名字， 这样我们才可以准确无误地 寄出邀请卡。 |
| 2 | alert | /əˈləːrt/ | adj. | fully aware and attentive ； watchful； vigilant； nimble | 警 觉的； 留心的； 机警的； 敏捷的 | The red light on the dashboard is to alert the driver of a low tank. | 汽车仪表板上的红灯用来提醒驾驶者油箱快没油了。 |
| 3 | approximately | /əˈprɔksimitli/ | adv. | roughly； just about | 大约； 大概 | The caloric content of a healthy diet should consist of approximately 60% carbohydrates， 30% fat and 10% protein. | 健康的饮食中的热量应该来自大约60% 的碳水化合物、 30% 的脂肪和10% 的蛋白质。 |
| 4 | distinctive | /disˈtiŋktiv/ | adj. | clearly marking a person or thing as different from others； characteristic； notable | 与众不同的； 有特色的； 显著的 | The thief has a distinctive scar above his left eyebrow. | 那个窃贼的左眉毛上方有一个很显眼的疤痕。 |
| 5 | dynamic | /daiˈnæmik/ | adj. | marked by energy and vigor； of force or power that causes movement | 充满活力的； 动力的 | He is a very captivating speaker with a deep， dynamic voice that carries throughout the entire auditorium. | 他是个非常有吸引力的演说家， 低沉而充满活力的声音回荡在整个礼堂 里面。 |
| 6 | excessive | /ikˈsesiv/ | adj. | too much； too great； going beyond what is reasonable or right | 过多的； 过度的； 过分的 | There is always an excessive amount of salt in all her dishes. | 她的每道菜放的盐都太多了。 |
| 7 | extraordinary | /ikˈstrɔːdinəri/ | adj. | beyond what is usual； exceptional； remarkable | 特别的； 格外的； 值得注意的 | Her voice was extraordinary and her acting was superb， making the entire production a great success. | 她的声音非常特别， 演技也非常精彩， 使得整个演出非常成功。 |
| 8 | fragile | /ˈfrædʒail/ | adj. | easily broken or damaged； delicate | 易碎的； 易损 坏的； 脆弱的 | In order to preserve the fragile vases， Linda wrapped each one carefully in a protective cushion. | 为保存这些易碎的花瓶， 琳达将每一个都小心地用保护垫包裹起来。 |
| 9 | inferior | /inˈfiəriə/ | adj. | not good or less good in quality or value； lower in order， status， etc. | 品质差的； 下等的； 下级的 | Jeff constantly tries to make people feel inferior to him. | 杰夫不断想让别人觉得自己不如他。 |
| 10 | initial | /iˈniʃəl/ | adj. | of or at the beginning； first | 开始的； 最初的 | What he is saying now contradicts his initial testimony； apparently he is trying to hide something. | 他现在说的和他最初的证词大相径庭， 很明显地他在试图隐瞒什么。 |
| 11 | irresistible | /ˌiriˈzistəbl/ | adj. | too nice， charming， fascinating， etc. to refuse | 极为诱人的； 无法抗拒的 | Many men in America find Jennifer Lopez totally irresistible. | 很多美国男人觉得珍妮弗·洛佩兹完全令人无法抗拒。 |
| 12 | miscellaneous | /ˌmisiˈleiniːəs/ | adj. | of several kinds or different kinds； too various to be called by a single name | 不同种类的； 各式各样的； 混 杂的 | That little store is stuffed with miscellaneous goods. | 那家小店塞满了杂货。 |
| 13 | noteworthy | /ˈnəutwəːði/ | adj. | deserving attention； remarkable | 值得注意 的； 显著的 | There is nothing particularly noteworthy in today’s newspaper. | 今天的报纸没有什么特别值得注意的消息。 |
| 14 | on and off | /ˈɔn ənd ˈɔːf/ | adv. | not regularly； now and then； sometimes； occasionally | 断断续续的； 偶尔； 有时 | I don’t know what the weather will be like tomorrow， but it has been raining on and off lately. | 我不知道明天天气会如何， 不过最近雨倒是一直断断续续地下着。 |
| 15 | rapid | /ˈræpid/ | adj. | happening， moving， or doing something at great speed； fast； swift | 迅速的； 快速的； 快的 | Thanks to Hong Kong’s rapid transit system， a normally 45-minute ride to the airport now takes only 20 minutes. | 由于有了香港的捷运系统， 平常要花45 分钟坐车到机场的路程现在只要20 分钟。 |
| 16 | self-explanatory | /ˌselfikˈsplænətəri/ | adj. | easily understood and needing no explanation； explaining itself | 不需要加以说明的； 不言自明的 | These instructions are self-explanatory， but please call me should you need any help. | 这些指示很清楚不需说明， 但是万一你需要协助的话， 就叫我一声。 |
| 17 | sufficient | /səˈfiʃənt/ | adj. | as much as is needed； enough | 足够的 | I believe that the water we brought is sufficient to last the entire trip. | 我想我们带来的水足够我们用到整个旅程结束。 |
| 18 | ultimately | /ˈʌltəmitli/ | adv. | at last； in the end | 最后； 最终 | He had tried several different treatments to relieve the pain， but ultimately ， he just had to endure the suffering. | 他试了几种不同的疗法来减轻疼痛， 但是最终还得忍受痛苦。 |
| 19 | vague | /veig/ | adj. | not clearly expressed； indefinite in shape， form， or character； indistinct | 含糊不清的； 模糊不清的 | His directions were too vague； no wonder you got lost. | 他给的指示太含糊了， 难怪你会迷路。 |
| 20 | vivid | /ˈvivid/ | adj. | full of life； （of light or color） bright and intense； lifelike | 生龙活虎的； 鲜艳的； 鲜明的； 生动逼真的 | I have the most vivid memories of the things that happened in my childhood. | 我对童年时所发生的事情有极鲜明的记忆。 |
